# M5 — Settings, i18n, Profiles Editor, Search by Title and gamelist.xml Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Every spec §9 setting is typed, validated, persisted in one versioned `settings.json` (corrupt, old or future files never crash: bad parts fall back to defaults and a backup is kept), applied by the core (scan, identify, match, art output, network, cache, safety), read by both the app and the CLI (CLI flags override), and edited from a Settings screen (all groups, per-field errors, reset with confirmation, theme and language applied live, About). Custom layout profiles can be created, edited and deleted next to the read-only built-ins with a live path preview. Source credentials live in the OS keychain and never reach the webview. The drawer gains "Search by title" (M4-05) backed by a per-ROM manual identity that never renames the target file. Filling a Batocera/Knulli library merges the image paths into `gamelist.xml` (M2-10), safely, journaled and undoable byte for byte.

**Architecture:** `coverly-core::settings` becomes the single settings model (`Settings` with one struct per §9 group, `load_settings`/`save_settings`/`read_settings`, `FieldError` validation, JSON merge patches) plus adapters that turn it into the existing option structs (`ScanOptions`, `FillSettings`, `ApplyOptions`, `NetConfig`, an art-adjusted `Profile`). New core modules: `profiles::custom` (TOML profile files, validation, store, preview), `gamelist` (offset-splicing XML merge over `quick-xml` events, DOCTYPE refused, journaled through `Writer`), `identify::overrides` (manual identities in the data dir), `credentials` (a `CredentialStore` trait + in-memory store). `src-tauri` replaces `library.json` by the settings state (snapshot per job), adds a file logger with a live level, a keyring-backed store, and commands for settings, folders/links, cache, profiles, credentials and drawer search. The React app adds a settings store (theme + language wired at the root), a Settings screen with sections and an About section, a profiles editor and the drawer search; the mock backend mirrors every new command and persists its settings, profiles and credential presence in `localStorage` so the E2E can reload.

**Tech Stack:** Rust 1.90 (edition 2021), Tauri 2.12, `quick-xml` 0.38 (core, new), `keyring` 4 (src-tauri only, new; same line as Versorium), `log` 0.4 (src-tauri, new), `serde_json` (core already), `toml` (core already). React 19 + TypeScript 6 (strict, `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`) + Tailwind 3 (`darkMode: 'class'`) + zustand 5. Tests: `cargo test`, Vitest 5 + Testing Library + jsdom. E2E: Chrome DevTools MCP against `pnpm dev`, plus ignored Rust tests run by hand on copies.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-coverly-roms-design.md` — §5 (profiles: built-in + user-defined TOML, single-slot "which media goes in the slot"), **§8** (drawer actions incl. "search", Settings, About/Updates), **§9 every group**, §10 (errors; `._*` cleanup), **§11** (credentials in the OS keychain, no telemetry), §12, §15 (M5 exit: "All §9 settings persisted and applied"), §16 (refinements win: §16.3 matching method is a setting, §16.5 index TTL, §16.8 Batocera/Knulli create/update `gamelist.xml` with backup, §16.10 safety). Tracker: `docs/TRACKER.md` (decisions D1–D23, rows M5-01..M5-04, M4-05, M2-10, open M2-12). Architecture: `docs/04-architecture/{desktop-app,fill-pipeline,system-architecture,art-sources,identification}.md`. Read-only pattern references: Pluryprint `/Users/miguelangelesparzacalero/Documents/GitHub/pluryprint` (`src/screens/AboutScreen.tsx`, `SettingsScreen.tsx`), Versorium `/Users/miguelangelesparzacalero/Documents/GitHub/versorium-app` (`src-tauri/src/secrets/mod.rs`: keyring 4, closed set of slots, values never sent to the webview).

## Global Constraints

- Branch `feat/m5-settings`; never switch branches, never push, never touch `main`. Every shell command starts with `cd /Users/miguelangelesparzacalero/Documents/GitHub/coverly-roms &&`.
- TDD: write the failing test, run it and see it fail, implement, see it pass.
- After **every** task run all gates and fix anything red before committing: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm format:check`, `pnpm test`, `pnpm build`.
- Commits: conventional, granular, English. **Never** add `Co-Authored-By` or any Claude attribution. Do not push.
- `rust-version = "1.90"`, `edition = "2021"` stay. New crates only: `quick-xml = "0.38"` (core), `keyring = "4"` and `log = "0.4"` (src-tauri). Pin them in the workspace `[workspace.dependencies]`.
- The core never depends on Tauri, holds no UI strings and never touches the OS keychain (the keyring store lives in `src-tauri`). `src-tauri` commands stay one-liners over `state`/`jobs`/`library` functions testable without a Tauri runtime.
- Tests never hit the real network (Rust: `coverly_core::net::testing::MockServer` on 127.0.0.1; UI: the mock backend) and **never touch the real OS keychain**: every test uses `MemoryStore`; the only real-keychain test is `#[ignore]`.
- Tests never read the developer's real config: `AppState::new` takes the config dir; CLI tests set `COVERLY_SETTINGS` to a temp file or to the empty string.
- Real ROM data `/Users/miguelangelesparzacalero/Documents/ROMS` is **strictly read-only**. Never create, modify or delete anything under `/Volumes`. Any real write uses a COPY under `$SC` = `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-miguelangelesparzacalero-Documents-GitHub/35b7c020-f2ae-412f-b757-99c24a9a498b/scratchpad`.
- Every UI string goes through i18n in `locales/en/ui.json` **and** `locales/es/ui.json` (same keys; `src/i18n/parity.test.ts` enforces it).
- Hostile inputs: settings files, profile TOML files, `gamelist.xml`, file names, typed search queries and downloaded data are untrusted. React text only (never `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`); XML values escaped; no entity expansion (DOCTYPE refused); size caps on every file read.
- Secrets: credential values go webview → Rust once (`set_credential`) and never come back (only presence booleans); never written to `settings.json`, logs, journals, reports or the mock's storage.
- The webview never sends a library path, a URL to open or a folder to open: folders and links are chosen by fixed ids in Rust (`open_config_folder`, `open_logs_folder`, `open_link(LinkId)`); `capabilities/default.json` stays `core:default` + `dialog:allow-open`.
- IPC: JS argument names camelCase, payload fields snake_case, errors `{ code, detail? }` shown as `errors.<code>`; codes are stable (only additions). Validation problems are data (`FieldError[]`), not errors.

## Review Focus

1. **A broken, old or future `settings.json`** (garbage bytes, a string where a number goes, `concurrency: 99`, `version: 9` with unknown groups, a 5 MB file, a `default_profile` that was deleted) must start the app with defaults for the bad parts only, a visible notice and a backup copy — never a crash or a silently lost file. Tests: Task 1 (`garbage_is_set_aside_and_defaults_are_used`, `a_wrong_type_resets_only_that_field_and_keeps_a_backup`, `a_newer_file_keeps_what_it_understands`, `huge_files_are_treated_as_corrupt`, `a_deleted_default_profile_falls_back_to_auto`), Task 9 (`a_corrupt_file_at_startup_reports_the_reset`).
2. **A hostile or unusual `gamelist.xml`** (DOCTYPE with entities, malformed tags, non-UTF-8, 40 MB, unknown elements and attributes, comments, duplicate `<game>` for one ROM, `&amp;` in paths, `<gameList/>`, a symlink or folder named `gamelist.xml`) is either merged preserving every byte it does not change or left untouched with a warning — and Undo restores it byte for byte, even after a paused-and-resumed job wrote it twice. Tests: Task 5 (`tests/gamelist.rs`, `tests/gamelist_apply.rs`, `undo_restores_a_gamelist_written_twice_in_one_job`), Task 16 (real Batocera-style copy, `cmp` after undo).
3. **Custom profile templates that escape the library** (`{root}/../x`, `/abs/{stem}.png`, `..` segments, unknown tokens, a slot without `{stem}`, a `{rel_dir}` in one slot only) and hostile files in the profiles folder (bad TOML, wrong id vs file name, 1 MB file, a built-in id) are refused with per-field errors or skipped with a warning. Tests: Task 4.
4. **Credentials never leave Rust** — not in `get_settings`, `credential_status`, events, logs, the settings file, or the mock's `localStorage`; an unavailable or locked keychain is a clear error, not a crash. Tests: Task 8 (`memory_store_sets_clears_and_reports_presence_only`), Task 10 (`credential_values_never_come_back`), Task 11 (`never stores secret values`), Task 13 (`sources: … credentials are stored without coming back`).
5. **Settings changed at the wrong moment** — while a job runs (the job keeps its snapshot; Clear cache is refused), with a library open (scan-affecting changes show "Reopen to apply"), invalid input typed in the form (per-field error, nothing saved, the other fields still save). Tests: Task 9 (`the_fill_config_is_a_snapshot_of_the_settings`, `clear_cache_reports_freed_bytes_and_is_refused_while_a_job_runs`, `scan_changes_ask_for_a_reopen`), Task 12 (`offers to reopen after a scan setting changes`), Task 13 (`network: invalid timeout shows an error, keeps the typed value and other fields still save`).
6. **Search by title picks a differently named image** — the art file keeps the ROM's own name (`<stem>.png`), the manual identity is remembered for later fills and identification, and a hostile query (control characters, 10 kB, `<img onerror>`) is refused or shown as text. Tests: Task 6 (`a_manual_pick_writes_to_the_rom_name_and_undo_removes_it`), Task 10 (`search_pick_is_remembered_and_keeps_the_file_name`, `hostile_queries_and_unlisted_picks_are_refused`), Task 15 (`search renders hostile names as text and refuses an empty title`).

## Deviations from the spec / task brief (decided in this plan)

- **One settings file** `settings.json` (format version 2) replaces `library.json` (version 1). On first start `library.json` is migrated (ignored folders + recents) and left in place as the backup. New decision **D24**.
- **Loading is field-tolerant**: unknown groups/fields are ignored, a field of the wrong type or out of range falls back to its default (both of a misordered pair of thresholds), a file that is not JSON (or > 1 MiB) is renamed `settings.json.bad-<unix>`; whenever loading changed something, a copy `settings.json.bak-<unix>` is kept and the UI shows a notice. A file from a newer version is read the same way (and backed up) instead of being refused. Part of D24.
- **Overwrite policy "ask"** (spec §9) = the fill wizard always asks (the overwrite step has no preselected answer) and the drawer confirms before replacing an existing image; per-file prompts inside a job are not built. The CLI treats `ask` as `never` unless `--overwrite backup` is given. New decision **D25**.
- **Proxy "system"** behaves like "env" (the standard `HTTPS_PROXY`/`HTTP_PROXY` variables): ureq cannot read the macOS/Windows proxy configuration and adding a platform proxy reader is out of scope. "manual" takes an `http(s)://host:port` URL. New decision **D26**.
- **"Hashing on/off" is the matching method** (spec §16.3 wins: `hash-then-name` · `hash-only` · `name-only`; `name-only` = hashing off) plus "max file size to hash". Part of D26.
- **"Backup before overwrite" is always on** and shown locked: Undo relies on backups (ADR-0010). **"Backup location"** moves the whole job data folder (journals + backups); jobs in the old folder are not listed or undone by the app after a change (the UI says so). The folder may not be inside the open library. New decision **D27**.
- **"Dry run by default"**: CLI — `coverly fill` without `--apply`/`--dry-run` follows the setting (default dry run). App — the wizard always plans first; with the setting off it starts the run by itself when the plan has nothing to review. Part of D27.
- **`._*` cleanup** (spec §9 Safety / §10): when on (default) and the library is on a removable volume (`/Volumes/…` on macOS, `/media`, `/run/media`, `/mnt` on Linux), after a job the AppleDouble files (`._*` whose first bytes are the AppleDouble magic `00 05 16 07`) in the folders the job wrote to are deleted. Not journaled (they are metadata junk). Part of D27.
- **Language preference** = a tie-break among candidates the strategy ranks equal (same match kind, score and regions): a candidate whose No-Intro language group (`(En,Fr,De)`) contains the first preferred language wins. Default `["en"]`. New decision **D28**.
- **Single-slot media** ("which media goes in the slot", spec §5): the one slot of Anbernic/Garlic/Onion/MinUI profiles is used for the chosen media (default cover). Output format override (`png`/`jpg`) changes the file extension of the template; RetroArch stays PNG (`Profile.fixed_format`). Changing it needs a library reopen (the UI says "Reopen to apply").
- **Custom profiles** are TOML files `<id>.toml` in `<config dir>/profiles` (or the "custom profiles folder" setting). Template tokens are the built-in set `{root} {system_dir} {rel_dir} {stem} {system_id} {libretro}`; spec §5's `{name}`/`{media}` are not added (every slot is already per media and `{stem}` is the ROM name). Templates must start with `{root}` or `{system_dir}`. Custom systems files (§9 Advanced "custom profiles/systems folder") keep using M1's `systems.toml` override; only the folder setting is new. New decision **D29**.
- **Credentials**: the `CredentialStore` trait and the in-memory store are in the core; the keyring store is in `src-tauri` (the CLI gets one in M6 when a source needs it). Keys are a closed set (`steamgriddb-api-key`, `screenscraper-user`, `screenscraper-password`). SteamGridDB and ScreenScraper show as "coming in a later version" (toggles disabled, kept off by normalisation) but their credentials can already be stored. New decision **D30**.
- **gamelist.xml mapping** (Batocera/Knulli `MetaData`): cover → `<thumbnail>`, and also `<image>` when the game has no usable image; screenshot → `<image>`; title screen → `<titleshot>`. `<marquee>` (and every other element/attribute/comment) is preserved and never written (no logo media in v1). A tag is set when missing, empty or pointing to a file that does not exist, and replaced only in a "replace existing art" job. New games are appended with `<path>`, `<name>` and the art tags. The merge is skipped (warning in the report) for a file with a DOCTYPE, that is not UTF-8, larger than 32 MiB, malformed, or not a regular file. New decision **D31**.
- **Manual identity** (M4-05): a pick from "Search by title" writes the art to the ROM's own target (never renamed) and stores `{ROM absolute path → candidate name, source}` in `<data dir>/identity-overrides.json`; later fills and the drawer's identification use it (`IdMethod::Manual`, confidence 1.0). "Forget manual match" removes it. Overrides are data, not cache: Clear cache keeps them. New decision **D32**.
- **Reset settings** keeps the recent libraries list (history, not a preference) and saves a backup copy first.
- **Updater** (M7): only "check updates on launch" and the channel are stored and shown; About shows "Updates arrive in a later version".
- **Log level**: the app gets a small file logger (`<data dir>/logs/coverly.log`, 1 MiB, one rotation) whose level follows the setting live. The CLI keeps writing to stderr.
- **16 tasks** instead of the 12–15 asked: the Settings screen is split in two tasks (shell + General/Library/About, then the other six groups) so each stays reviewable on its own.
- **Still open after M5**: M2-12 (Anbernic max size, needs the console; the per-profile max size setting is the user's workaround), M2-13, M3-06, M3-07, M3-08 (choose/confirm the profile at open — partly covered by "default profile") → tracker notes in Task 15.

### Task 1: Core — typed, versioned settings: model, defaults, tolerant load, migration, atomic save, validation, patches (M5-01)

**Files:**
- Delete: `crates/coverly-core/src/settings/mod.rs` content (the M3 `LibrarySettings`), replaced by the files below
- Create: `crates/coverly-core/src/settings/{mod.rs, model.rs, validate.rs, patch.rs, load.rs}`
- Delete: `crates/coverly-core/tests/library_settings.rs` (its recents tests are ported)
- Test: `crates/coverly-core/tests/settings_file.rs`
- Modify (compile only, behaviour kept): `src-tauri/src/state.rs`, `src-tauri/tests/{app_state,fill_state,refresh,drawer_actions,real_fill}.rs` — see Step 7

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `crate::scan::ScanOptions::default()` (ignored dirs/exts), `crate::matching::{StrategyKind, parse_region_order}`, `crate::identify::MatchMethod`, `crate::profiles::Media`.
- Produces (all re-exported from `coverly_core::settings`):
  - `pub const SETTINGS_VERSION: u32 = 2; pub const MAX_RECENT: usize = 10; pub const SETTINGS_FILE: &str = "settings.json"; pub const LEGACY_LIBRARY_FILE: &str = "library.json"; pub const MAX_SETTINGS_BYTES: u64 = 1024 * 1024;`
  - enums (all `Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize`, `#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]`): `LanguageChoice { Auto, En, Es }`, `ThemeChoice { System, Light, Dark }`, `UpdateChannel { Stable, Beta }`, `OverwriteSetting { Never, Ask, Backup }`, `OutputFormat { Profile, Png, Jpg }`, `ProxyChoice { System, Env, Manual }`, `LogLevel { Error, Warn, Info, Debug }` (defaults: first listed, except `LogLevel::Info`).
  - `pub struct RecentLibrary { path: PathBuf, name: String, profile_id: String, roms: u32, opened_at: i64 }` (unchanged)
  - `pub struct GeneralSettings { language, theme, check_updates_on_launch: bool, update_channel }`
  - `pub struct LibraryGroup { default_profile: Option<String>, ignored_dirs: Vec<String>, ignored_exts: Vec<String>, include_hidden: bool, follow_symlinks: bool, recent: Vec<RecentLibrary> }` + `set_ignored_dirs(Vec<String>)`, `set_ignored_exts(Vec<String>)`
  - `pub struct MaxSize { width: u32, height: u32 }`
  - `pub struct ArtSettings { media: Vec<Media>, single_slot_media: Media, output_format: OutputFormat, max_size: BTreeMap<String, MaxSize>, keep_aspect: bool, jpeg_quality: u8, overwrite: OverwriteSetting }`
  - `pub struct MatchingSettings { strategy: StrategyKind, regions: Vec<String>, languages: Vec<String>, auto_threshold: f32, review_threshold: f32, fuzzy: bool, arcade_translation: bool, match_method: MatchMethod, max_hash_mb: u32 }`
  - `pub struct SourceEntry { id: String, enabled: bool }`, `pub struct SourcesSettings { order: Vec<SourceEntry> }`, `pub const KNOWN_SOURCES: [&str; 3] = ["libretro", "steamgriddb", "screenscraper"]; pub const AVAILABLE_SOURCES: [&str; 1] = ["libretro"];`
  - `pub struct NetworkSettings { concurrency: u32, timeout_secs: u32, retries: u32, proxy: ProxyChoice, proxy_url: String, cache_max_mb: u32, index_refresh_hours: u32 }`
  - `pub struct SafetySettings { backup_before_overwrite: bool, backup_dir: Option<PathBuf>, dry_run_default: bool, clean_apple_double: bool, undo_depth: u32 }`
  - `pub struct AdvancedSettings { log_level: LogLevel, custom_dir: Option<PathBuf> }`
  - `pub struct Settings { version: u32, general, library, art, matching, sources, network, safety, advanced }` (`Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize`, `#[serde(default)]` on it and every group) with `normalise(&mut self)`, `validate(&self, known_profiles: &[String]) -> Vec<FieldError>`, `with_patch(&self, patch: &serde_json::Value, known_profiles: &[String]) -> Result<Settings, Vec<FieldError>>`, `remember(&mut self, RecentLibrary)`, `forget(&mut self, &Path)`
  - `#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")] pub enum FieldErrorCode { Range, Required, Invalid, Order, Unknown, Duplicate, NotAbsolute }`; `pub struct FieldError { field: String, code: FieldErrorCode, min: Option<f64>, max: Option<f64> }` (`Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize`)
  - `pub struct Range { field: &'static str, min: f64, max: f64 }` (`Serialize`); `pub const RANGES: [Range; 12]`
  - `#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")] pub enum LoadNotice { Migrated, ResetCorrupt, Fixed, FutureVersion }`; `pub struct LoadReport { notice: Option<LoadNotice>, backup: Option<PathBuf>, fixed: Vec<String> }` (`Default`)
  - `pub fn load_settings(dir: &Path, known_profiles: &[String]) -> (Settings, LoadReport)` (may rename/copy files in `dir`, may write `settings.json` when migrating)
  - `pub fn read_settings(path: &Path, known_profiles: &[String]) -> Settings` (no side effects; for the CLI)
  - `pub fn save_settings(dir: &Path, s: &Settings) -> io::Result<()>` (temp file + fsync + rename)
  - `pub fn backup_settings(dir: &Path, tag: &str) -> io::Result<Option<PathBuf>>` (copies `settings.json` to `settings.json.<tag>-<unix>`)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`crates/coverly-core/tests/settings_file.rs`:

```rust
//! Spec §9 settings: defaults, persistence, hostile files, validation, patches.

use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use coverly_core::identify::MatchMethod;
use coverly_core::matching::StrategyKind;
use coverly_core::profiles::{builtin_profiles, Media};
use coverly_core::settings::*;
use serde_json::json;

fn known() -> Vec<String> {
    builtin_profiles().into_iter().map(|p| p.id).collect()
}

fn files(dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut names: Vec<String> = fs::read_dir(dir)
        .unwrap()
        .map(|e| e.unwrap().file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned())
        .collect();
    names.sort();
    names
}

fn write(dir: &Path, text: &str) -> PathBuf {
    let path = dir.join(SETTINGS_FILE);
    fs::write(&path, text).unwrap();
    path
}

#[test]
fn defaults_follow_the_spec() {
    let s = Settings::default();
    assert_eq!(s.version, SETTINGS_VERSION);
    assert_eq!(s.general.language, LanguageChoice::Auto);
    assert_eq!(s.general.theme, ThemeChoice::System);
    assert!(s.general.check_updates_on_launch);
    assert_eq!(s.general.update_channel, UpdateChannel::Stable);
    assert_eq!(s.library.default_profile, None);
    assert_eq!(s.library.ignored_dirs, ["bios", "saves", "states", "Shortcuts"]);
    assert!(s.library.ignored_exts.contains(&"txt".to_string()));
    assert!(!s.library.include_hidden && !s.library.follow_symlinks);
    assert_eq!(s.art.media, Media::ALL);
    assert_eq!(s.art.single_slot_media, Media::Cover);
    assert_eq!(s.art.output_format, OutputFormat::Profile);
    assert!(s.art.keep_aspect);
    assert_eq!(s.art.jpeg_quality, 90);
    assert_eq!(s.art.overwrite, OverwriteSetting::Never);
    assert_eq!(s.matching.strategy, StrategyKind::RomRegion);
    assert_eq!(s.matching.regions, ["rom", "world", "usa", "europe", "japan"]);
    assert_eq!(s.matching.languages, ["en"]);
    assert_eq!(s.matching.auto_threshold, 0.90);
    assert_eq!(s.matching.review_threshold, 0.65);
    assert!(s.matching.fuzzy && s.matching.arcade_translation);
    assert_eq!(s.matching.match_method, MatchMethod::HashThenName);
    assert_eq!(s.matching.max_hash_mb, 64);
    let sources: Vec<(&str, bool)> = s
        .sources
        .order
        .iter()
        .map(|e| (e.id.as_str(), e.enabled))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(
        sources,
        [("libretro", true), ("steamgriddb", false), ("screenscraper", false)]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        (s.network.concurrency, s.network.timeout_secs, s.network.retries),
        (4, 30, 3)
    );
    assert_eq!(s.network.proxy, ProxyChoice::System);
    assert_eq!(s.network.cache_max_mb, 2048);
    assert_eq!(s.network.index_refresh_hours, 48);
    assert!(s.safety.backup_before_overwrite && s.safety.dry_run_default);
    assert!(s.safety.clean_apple_double);
    assert_eq!(s.safety.backup_dir, None);
    assert_eq!(s.safety.undo_depth, 10);
    assert_eq!(s.advanced.log_level, LogLevel::Info);
    assert!(s.validate(&known()).is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn missing_file_gives_defaults_and_writes_nothing() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let (s, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(s, Settings::default());
    assert_eq!(report, LoadReport::default());
    assert!(files(dir.path()).is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn save_then_load_round_trips_without_temp_files() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let nested = dir.path().join("a/b");
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.general.theme = ThemeChoice::Dark;
    s.network.timeout_secs = 90;
    s.art.max_size.insert("onion".into(), MaxSize { width: 300, height: 400 });
    save_settings(&nested, &s).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(files(&nested), [SETTINGS_FILE]);
    let (back, report) = load_settings(&nested, &known());
    assert_eq!(back, s);
    assert_eq!(report.notice, None);
}

#[test]
fn garbage_is_set_aside_and_defaults_are_used() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    write(dir.path(), "{ not json");
    let (s, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(s, Settings::default());
    assert_eq!(report.notice, Some(LoadNotice::ResetCorrupt));
    let backup = report.backup.expect("the bad file is kept");
    assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&backup).unwrap(), "{ not json");
    assert!(backup.file_name().unwrap().to_string_lossy().starts_with("settings.json.bad-"));
    assert!(!dir.path().join(SETTINGS_FILE).exists());
}

#[test]
fn a_json_array_is_corrupt_too() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    write(dir.path(), "[1,2,3]");
    let (s, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(s, Settings::default());
    assert_eq!(report.notice, Some(LoadNotice::ResetCorrupt));
}

#[test]
fn a_wrong_type_resets_only_that_field_and_keeps_a_backup() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let text = r#"{"version":2,"network":{"timeout_secs":"soon","retries":5},"general":{"theme":"dark"}}"#;
    write(dir.path(), text);
    let (s, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(s.network.timeout_secs, 30);
    assert_eq!(s.network.retries, 5);
    assert_eq!(s.general.theme, ThemeChoice::Dark);
    assert_eq!(report.notice, Some(LoadNotice::Fixed));
    assert_eq!(report.fixed, ["network.timeout_secs"]);
    let backup = report.backup.expect("a copy of what was read");
    assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(backup).unwrap(), text);
    // The file itself is untouched until the user saves.
    assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join(SETTINGS_FILE)).unwrap(), text);
}

#[test]
fn out_of_range_values_fall_back_to_defaults() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    write(
        dir.path(),
        r#"{"network":{"concurrency":99},"art":{"jpeg_quality":0},"safety":{"undo_depth":3}}"#,
    );
    let (s, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(s.network.concurrency, 4);
    assert_eq!(s.art.jpeg_quality, 90);
    assert_eq!(s.safety.undo_depth, 3);
    assert_eq!(report.notice, Some(LoadNotice::Fixed));
    assert!(report.fixed.contains(&"network.concurrency".to_string()));
    assert!(report.fixed.contains(&"art.jpeg_quality".to_string()));
}

#[test]
fn thresholds_out_of_order_are_both_reset() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    write(
        dir.path(),
        r#"{"matching":{"auto_threshold":0.5,"review_threshold":0.9}}"#,
    );
    let (s, _) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(s.matching.auto_threshold, 0.90);
    assert_eq!(s.matching.review_threshold, 0.65);
}

#[test]
fn a_newer_file_keeps_what_it_understands() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    write(
        dir.path(),
        r#"{"version":9,"general":{"theme":"light","hologram":true},"telemetry":{"on":true}}"#,
    );
    let (s, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(s.general.theme, ThemeChoice::Light);
    assert_eq!(s.version, SETTINGS_VERSION);
    assert_eq!(report.notice, Some(LoadNotice::FutureVersion));
    assert!(report.backup.is_some());
}

#[test]
fn huge_files_are_treated_as_corrupt() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let big = " ".repeat(MAX_SETTINGS_BYTES as usize + 1);
    write(dir.path(), &big);
    let (s, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(s, Settings::default());
    assert_eq!(report.notice, Some(LoadNotice::ResetCorrupt));
}

#[test]
fn a_deleted_default_profile_falls_back_to_auto() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    write(dir.path(), r#"{"library":{"default_profile":"gone"}}"#);
    let (s, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(s.library.default_profile, None);
    assert_eq!(report.fixed, ["library.default_profile"]);
}

#[test]
fn legacy_library_json_is_migrated_and_kept() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let legacy = r#"{"version":1,"ignored_dirs":["bios","Junk"],"recent":[
        {"path":"/sd","name":"sd","profile_id":"onion","roms":12,"opened_at":5},
        {"path":7}
    ]}"#;
    fs::write(dir.path().join(LEGACY_LIBRARY_FILE), legacy).unwrap();
    let (s, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(report.notice, Some(LoadNotice::Migrated));
    assert_eq!(s.library.ignored_dirs, ["bios", "Junk"]);
    assert_eq!(s.library.recent.len(), 1, "the broken recent is dropped");
    assert_eq!(s.library.recent[0].profile_id, "onion");
    assert!(dir.path().join(SETTINGS_FILE).exists(), "migrated once");
    assert_eq!(
        fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join(LEGACY_LIBRARY_FILE)).unwrap(),
        legacy,
        "the old file stays as the backup"
    );
    let (again, report) = load_settings(dir.path(), &known());
    assert_eq!(again, s);
    assert_eq!(report.notice, None);
}

#[test]
fn read_settings_has_no_side_effects() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = write(dir.path(), "garbage");
    assert_eq!(read_settings(&path, &known()), Settings::default());
    assert_eq!(files(dir.path()), [SETTINGS_FILE]);
    assert_eq!(
        read_settings(&dir.path().join("none.json"), &known()),
        Settings::default()
    );
}

fn codes(errors: &[FieldError]) -> Vec<(String, FieldErrorCode)> {
    errors.iter().map(|e| (e.field.clone(), e.code)).collect()
}

#[test]
fn validation_reports_each_field_with_its_range() {
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.network.timeout_secs = 1;
    s.network.retries = 11;
    s.art.jpeg_quality = 101;
    s.matching.max_hash_mb = 0;
    s.network.index_refresh_hours = 0;
    s.safety.undo_depth = 0;
    let errors = s.validate(&known());
    let timeout = errors.iter().find(|e| e.field == "network.timeout_secs").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(timeout.code, FieldErrorCode::Range);
    assert_eq!((timeout.min, timeout.max), (Some(5.0), Some(300.0)));
    for field in [
        "network.retries",
        "art.jpeg_quality",
        "matching.max_hash_mb",
        "network.index_refresh_hours",
        "safety.undo_depth",
    ] {
        assert!(errors.iter().any(|e| e.field == field), "{field}: {errors:?}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn cross_field_and_list_rules() {
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.matching.review_threshold = 0.95;
    s.matching.regions = vec!["rom".into(), "mars".into()];
    s.matching.languages = vec!["english".into()];
    s.art.media.clear();
    s.art.max_size.insert("nope".into(), MaxSize { width: 300, height: 400 });
    s.art.max_size.insert("onion".into(), MaxSize { width: 8, height: 400 });
    s.network.proxy = ProxyChoice::Manual;
    s.safety.backup_before_overwrite = false;
    s.safety.backup_dir = Some("relative/dir".into());
    s.advanced.custom_dir = Some("also/relative".into());
    s.library.default_profile = Some("nope".into());
    let got = codes(&s.validate(&known()));
    for want in [
        ("matching.review_threshold", FieldErrorCode::Order),
        ("matching.regions", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("matching.languages", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("art.media", FieldErrorCode::Required),
        ("art.max_size.nope", FieldErrorCode::Unknown),
        ("art.max_size.onion.width", FieldErrorCode::Range),
        ("network.proxy_url", FieldErrorCode::Required),
        ("safety.backup_before_overwrite", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("safety.backup_dir", FieldErrorCode::NotAbsolute),
        ("advanced.custom_dir", FieldErrorCode::NotAbsolute),
        ("library.default_profile", FieldErrorCode::Unknown),
    ] {
        assert!(
            got.contains(&(want.0.to_string(), want.1)),
            "missing {want:?} in {got:?}"
        );
    }
    let mut dup = Settings::default();
    dup.matching.regions = vec!["usa".into(), "USA".into()];
    assert_eq!(
        codes(&dup.validate(&known())),
        [("matching.regions".to_string(), FieldErrorCode::Duplicate)]
    );
    let mut bad_url = Settings::default();
    bad_url.network.proxy = ProxyChoice::Manual;
    bad_url.network.proxy_url = "socks5://x:1".into();
    assert_eq!(
        codes(&bad_url.validate(&known())),
        [("network.proxy_url".to_string(), FieldErrorCode::Invalid)]
    );
}

#[test]
fn normalise_cleans_lists_and_keeps_unavailable_sources_off() {
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.library.set_ignored_dirs(vec![" bios ".into(), "BIOS".into(), "a/b".into(), "..".into(), "".into()]);
    s.library.set_ignored_exts(vec![".PNG".into(), "png".into(), " txt ".into(), "a b".into(), "".into()]);
    s.sources.order = vec![
        SourceEntry { id: "steamgriddb".into(), enabled: true },
        SourceEntry { id: "evil".into(), enabled: true },
        SourceEntry { id: "libretro".into(), enabled: true },
        SourceEntry { id: "libretro".into(), enabled: false },
    ];
    s.art.media = vec![Media::Title, Media::Cover, Media::Title];
    s.matching.regions = vec![" USA ".into(), "rom".into()];
    s.normalise();
    assert_eq!(s.library.ignored_dirs, ["bios"]);
    assert_eq!(s.library.ignored_exts, ["png", "txt"]);
    let order: Vec<(&str, bool)> = s.sources.order.iter().map(|e| (e.id.as_str(), e.enabled)).collect();
    assert_eq!(
        order,
        [("steamgriddb", false), ("libretro", true), ("screenscraper", false)]
    );
    assert_eq!(s.art.media, [Media::Cover, Media::Title]);
    assert_eq!(s.matching.regions, ["usa", "rom"]);
}

#[test]
fn with_patch_applies_a_valid_change() {
    let s = Settings::default();
    let next = s
        .with_patch(
            &json!({"network": {"timeout_secs": 60}, "general": {"language": "es"}}),
            &known(),
        )
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(next.network.timeout_secs, 60);
    assert_eq!(next.general.language, LanguageChoice::Es);
    assert_eq!(next.network.retries, s.network.retries);
    let cleared = next
        .with_patch(&json!({"safety": {"backup_dir": null}}), &known())
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(cleared.safety.backup_dir, None);
}

#[test]
fn with_patch_names_the_field_of_a_type_error_and_of_a_range_error() {
    let s = Settings::default();
    let errors = s
        .with_patch(
            &json!({"network": {"timeout_secs": "x", "retries": 2}}),
            &known(),
        )
        .unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(
        codes(&errors),
        [("network.timeout_secs".to_string(), FieldErrorCode::Invalid)]
    );
    let errors = s
        .with_patch(&json!({"network": {"concurrency": 7}}), &known())
        .unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(
        codes(&errors),
        [("network.concurrency".to_string(), FieldErrorCode::Range)]
    );
}

#[test]
fn remembers_recent_libraries_most_recent_first() {
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    for i in 0..12 {
        s.remember(RecentLibrary {
            path: format!("/lib{i}").into(),
            name: format!("lib{i}"),
            profile_id: "onion".into(),
            roms: i,
            opened_at: i64::from(i),
        });
    }
    assert_eq!(s.library.recent.len(), MAX_RECENT);
    assert_eq!(s.library.recent[0].path, PathBuf::from("/lib11"));
    s.remember(RecentLibrary {
        path: "/lib5".into(),
        name: "lib5".into(),
        profile_id: "onion".into(),
        roms: 99,
        opened_at: 99,
    });
    assert_eq!(s.library.recent[0].roms, 99);
    assert_eq!(s.library.recent.iter().filter(|r| r.path == Path::new("/lib5")).count(), 1);
    s.forget(Path::new("/lib5"));
    assert!(s.library.recent.iter().all(|r| r.path != Path::new("/lib5")));
}

#[test]
fn ranges_table_is_the_validation_source() {
    let fields: Vec<&str> = RANGES.iter().map(|r| r.field).collect();
    assert!(fields.contains(&"network.timeout_secs"));
    let json = serde_json::to_value(&RANGES[0]).unwrap();
    assert!(json.get("field").is_some() && json.get("min").is_some());
}
```

`crates/coverly-core/src/settings/mod.rs`:

```rust
//! Persisted settings (spec §9): one struct per group with the spec
//! defaults, stored as a versioned JSON file (`settings.json`) in the app
//! config dir (see [`crate::paths::config_dir`]). Loading never fails and
//! never loses a file: unknown fields are ignored, a bad field falls back
//! to its default, a file that cannot be read as settings is set aside,
//! and a copy is kept whenever loading changed something (D24).

mod load;
mod model;
mod patch;
mod validate;

pub use load::{
    backup_settings, load_settings, read_settings, save_settings, LoadNotice, LoadReport,
    LEGACY_LIBRARY_FILE, MAX_SETTINGS_BYTES, SETTINGS_FILE,
};
pub use model::*;
pub use validate::{FieldError, FieldErrorCode, Range, RANGES};
```

`crates/coverly-core/src/settings/model.rs`:

```rust
//! The settings types and their defaults (spec §9).

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::identify::MatchMethod;
use crate::matching::StrategyKind;
use crate::profiles::Media;
use crate::scan::ScanOptions;

/// Format version written by this build (`library.json` was 1).
pub const SETTINGS_VERSION: u32 = 2;
/// Recent libraries kept.
pub const MAX_RECENT: usize = 10;
/// Every art source the settings know, in default order.
pub const KNOWN_SOURCES: [&str; 3] = ["libretro", "steamgriddb", "screenscraper"];
/// Sources this build can use; the others stay off until they ship (M6).
pub const AVAILABLE_SOURCES: [&str; 1] = ["libretro"];

macro_rules! choice {
    ($(#[$doc:meta])* $name:ident { $(#[$d:meta])* $default:ident $(, $other:ident)* }) => {
        $(#[$doc])*
        #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
        #[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
        pub enum $name {
            $(#[$d])*
            #[default]
            $default,
            $($other),*
        }
    };
}

choice!(/// UI language (`auto` follows the system).
    LanguageChoice { Auto, En, Es });
choice!(/// UI theme (`system` follows the OS).
    ThemeChoice { System, Light, Dark });
choice!(/// Update channel (the updater arrives in M7).
    UpdateChannel { Stable, Beta });
choice!(/// What a fill does with existing art (D25: `ask` = the wizard asks).
    OverwriteSetting { Never, Ask, Backup });
choice!(/// Output image format (`profile` = what the layout says).
    OutputFormat { Profile, Png, Jpg });
choice!(/// Proxy source (D26: `system` reads the standard environment variables).
    ProxyChoice { System, Env, Manual });

/// App log level.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum LogLevel {
    Error,
    Warn,
    #[default]
    Info,
    Debug,
}

/// One library the user opened.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RecentLibrary {
    /// Folder as the user picked it.
    pub path: PathBuf,
    /// Display name (the folder name).
    pub name: String,
    /// Profile it was opened with.
    pub profile_id: String,
    /// ROMs found the last time.
    pub roms: u32,
    /// When it was last opened, in Unix seconds.
    pub opened_at: i64,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct GeneralSettings {
    pub language: LanguageChoice,
    pub theme: ThemeChoice,
    pub check_updates_on_launch: bool,
    pub update_channel: UpdateChannel,
}

impl Default for GeneralSettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        GeneralSettings {
            language: LanguageChoice::Auto,
            theme: ThemeChoice::System,
            check_updates_on_launch: true,
            update_channel: UpdateChannel::Stable,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct LibraryGroup {
    /// Profile used when opening a library; `None` = detect.
    pub default_profile: Option<String>,
    /// Folder names never scanned (case-insensitive, at any depth).
    pub ignored_dirs: Vec<String>,
    /// Extensions never taken as ROMs (lowercase, no dot).
    pub ignored_exts: Vec<String>,
    pub include_hidden: bool,
    pub follow_symlinks: bool,
    /// Most recent first, at most [`MAX_RECENT`].
    pub recent: Vec<RecentLibrary>,
}

impl Default for LibraryGroup {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let scan = ScanOptions::default();
        LibraryGroup {
            default_profile: None,
            ignored_dirs: scan.ignored_dirs,
            ignored_exts: scan.ignored_exts,
            include_hidden: scan.include_hidden,
            follow_symlinks: scan.follow_symlinks,
            recent: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

impl LibraryGroup {
    /// Trimmed, without empty names, `.`/`..` or names holding a path
    /// separator, without case-insensitive duplicates (first spelling wins).
    pub fn set_ignored_dirs(&mut self, dirs: Vec<String>) {
        let mut out: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        for dir in dirs {
            let dir = dir.trim();
            if dir.is_empty() || dir == "." || dir == ".." || dir.contains(['/', '\\']) {
                continue;
            }
            if out.iter().any(|o| o.to_lowercase() == dir.to_lowercase()) {
                continue;
            }
            out.push(dir.to_string());
        }
        self.ignored_dirs = out;
    }

    /// Trimmed, lowercase, without a leading dot; only ASCII letters,
    /// digits, `-` and `_`; no duplicates.
    pub fn set_ignored_exts(&mut self, exts: Vec<String>) {
        let mut out: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        for ext in exts {
            let ext = ext.trim().trim_start_matches('.').to_ascii_lowercase();
            let ok = !ext.is_empty()
                && ext.len() <= 16
                && ext.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-' || b == b'_');
            if ok && !out.contains(&ext) {
                out.push(ext);
            }
        }
        self.ignored_exts = out;
    }
}

/// A box an image must fit in.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MaxSize {
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct ArtSettings {
    /// Media a fill handles by default (filtered by what the profile stores).
    pub media: Vec<Media>,
    /// Media written to the one slot of single-slot layouts (spec §5).
    pub single_slot_media: Media,
    pub output_format: OutputFormat,
    /// Per profile id: the largest image written (overrides the profile).
    pub max_size: BTreeMap<String, MaxSize>,
    /// Off: images are stretched to exactly the box.
    pub keep_aspect: bool,
    /// 1–100.
    pub jpeg_quality: u8,
    pub overwrite: OverwriteSetting,
}

impl Default for ArtSettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        ArtSettings {
            media: Media::ALL.to_vec(),
            single_slot_media: Media::Cover,
            output_format: OutputFormat::Profile,
            max_size: BTreeMap::new(),
            keep_aspect: true,
            jpeg_quality: 90,
            overwrite: OverwriteSetting::Never,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]

impl Default for MatchingSettings {

            strategy: StrategyKind::RomRegion,
            regions: ["rom", "world", "usa", "europe", "japan"].map(String::from).to_vec(),
            languages: vec!["en".to_string()],
            auto_threshold: 0.90,
            review_threshold: 0.65,
            fuzzy: true,
            arcade_translation: true,

            max_hash_mb: 64,

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]

    pub custom_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
}

/// Every setting of spec §9.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct Settings {
    pub version: u32,
    pub general: GeneralSettings,
    pub library: LibraryGroup,
    pub art: ArtSettings,
    pub matching: MatchingSettings,
    pub sources: SourcesSettings,
    pub network: NetworkSettings,
    pub safety: SafetySettings,
    pub advanced: AdvancedSettings,
}

impl Default for Settings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Settings {
            version: SETTINGS_VERSION,
            general: GeneralSettings::default(),
            library: LibraryGroup::default(),
            art: ArtSettings::default(),
            matching: MatchingSettings::default(),
            sources: SourcesSettings::default(),
            network: NetworkSettings::default(),
            safety: SafetySettings::default(),
            advanced: AdvancedSettings::default(),
        }
    }
}

impl Settings {
    /// Puts `entry` first, dropping an older entry with the same path and
    /// anything beyond [`MAX_RECENT`].
    pub fn remember(&mut self, entry: RecentLibrary) {
        self.library.recent.retain(|r| r.path != entry.path);
        self.library.recent.insert(0, entry);
        self.library.recent.truncate(MAX_RECENT);
    }

    /// Drops the library at `path` from the recent list.
    pub fn forget(&mut self, path: &Path) {
        self.library.recent.retain(|r| r.path != path);
    }

    /// Cleans what can be cleaned without asking: list spelling and
    /// duplicates, unknown sources dropped, missing ones appended (off),
    /// sources this build cannot use kept off, the version stamped.
    pub fn normalise(&mut self) {
        self.version = SETTINGS_VERSION;
        let dirs = std::mem::take(&mut self.library.ignored_dirs);
        self.library.set_ignored_dirs(dirs);
        let exts = std::mem::take(&mut self.library.ignored_exts);
        self.library.set_ignored_exts(exts);
        self.library.recent.truncate(MAX_RECENT);
        self.art.media = Media::ALL
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|m| self.art.media.contains(m))
            .collect();
        let mut regions: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        for r in &self.matching.regions {
            let r = r.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
            if !r.is_empty() && !regions.contains(&r) {
                regions.push(r);
            }
        }
        // Duplicates in another spelling (`usa`/`USA`) collapse here; a
        // duplicate the user typed is reported by `validate` before this.
        self.matching.regions = regions;
        let mut langs: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        for l in &self.matching.languages {
            let l = l.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
            if !l.is_empty() && !langs.contains(&l) {
                langs.push(l);
            }
        }
        self.matching.languages = langs;
        let mut order: Vec<SourceEntry> = Vec::new();
        for e in &self.sources.order {
            if KNOWN_SOURCES.contains(&e.id.as_str()) && !order.iter().any(|o| o.id == e.id) {
                order.push(SourceEntry {
                    id: e.id.clone(),
                    enabled: e.enabled && AVAILABLE_SOURCES.contains(&e.id.as_str()),
                });
            }
        }
        for id in KNOWN_SOURCES {
            if !order.iter().any(|o| o.id == id) {
                order.push(SourceEntry {
                    id: id.to_string(),
                    enabled: false,
                });
            }
        }
        self.sources.order = order;
        self.network.proxy_url = self.network.proxy_url.trim().to_string();
    }
}
```

> Note for the implementer: `validate` must see the regions **before** `normalise` collapses `usa`/`USA` (the `cross_field_and_list_rules` test validates without normalising, so this works as written: `validate` compares case-insensitively and reports `Duplicate`).

```rust
//! Range and consistency rules; problems are data (`FieldError`), shown
//! next to the field in the UI.

use serde::Serialize;

use super::model::{ProxyChoice, Settings, KNOWN_SOURCES};
use crate::matching::parse_region_order;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum FieldErrorCode {
    /// Outside `min..=max`.
    Range,
    /// Must not be empty.
    Required,
    /// Not a valid value (wrong type, bad syntax, locked setting).
    Invalid,
    /// Inconsistent with another field (review above auto threshold).
    Order,
    /// Names something that does not exist (profile, source).
    Unknown,
    /// The same value twice.
    Duplicate,
    /// A folder that must be an absolute path.
    NotAbsolute,
}

/// One problem with one field (`field` is a dotted path).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct FieldError {
    pub field: String,
    pub code: FieldErrorCode,
    pub min: Option<f64>,
    pub max: Option<f64>,
}

impl FieldError {
    pub fn new(field: impl Into<String>, code: FieldErrorCode) -> FieldError {
        FieldError {
            field: field.into(),
            code,
            min: None,
            max: None,
        }
    }
}

/// An inclusive numeric range of one setting (shared with the UI through
/// the IPC contract sample).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct Range {
    pub field: &'static str,
    pub min: f64,
    pub max: f64,
}

const fn range(field: &'static str, min: f64, max: f64) -> Range {
    Range { field, min, max }
}

pub const RANGES: [Range; 12] = [
    range("art.jpeg_quality", 1.0, 100.0),
    range("art.max_size.width", 16.0, 4096.0),
    range("art.max_size.height", 16.0, 4096.0),
    range("matching.auto_threshold", 0.3, 1.0),
    range("matching.review_threshold", 0.3, 1.0),
    range("matching.max_hash_mb", 1.0, 4096.0),
    range("network.concurrency", 1.0, 6.0),
    range("network.timeout_secs", 5.0, 300.0),
    range("network.retries", 0.0, 10.0),
    range("network.cache_max_mb", 64.0, 102_400.0),
    range("network.index_refresh_hours", 1.0, 720.0),
    range("safety.undo_depth", 1.0, 100.0),
];

fn check(out: &mut Vec<FieldError>, rule: &str, field: String, value: f64) {
    let r = RANGES
        .iter()
        .find(|r| r.field == rule)
        .expect("every checked field has a range");
    if !(r.min..=r.max).contains(&value) || value.is_nan() {
        out.push(FieldError {
            field,
            code: FieldErrorCode::Range,
            min: Some(r.min),
            max: Some(r.max),
        });
    }
}

fn simple(out: &mut Vec<FieldError>, field: &str, value: f64) {
    check(out, field, field.to_string(), value);
}

impl Settings {
    /// Every problem, in a stable order. `known_profiles`: built-in and
    /// custom profile ids.
    pub fn validate(&self, known_profiles: &[String]) -> Vec<FieldError> {
        use FieldErrorCode::*;
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(id) = &self.library.default_profile {
            if !known_profiles.iter().any(|k| k == id) {
                out.push(FieldError::new("library.default_profile", Unknown));
            }
        }
        if self.art.media.is_empty() {
            out.push(FieldError::new("art.media", Required));
        }
        for (id, size) in &self.art.max_size {
            if !known_profiles.iter().any(|k| k == id) {
                out.push(FieldError::new(format!("art.max_size.{id}"), Unknown));
                continue;
            }
            check(&mut out, "art.max_size.width", format!("art.max_size.{id}.width"), f64::from(size.width));
            check(&mut out, "art.max_size.height", format!("art.max_size.{id}.height"), f64::from(size.height));
        }
        simple(&mut out, "art.jpeg_quality", f64::from(self.art.jpeg_quality));
        let m = &self.matching;
        if m.regions.is_empty() {
            out.push(FieldError::new("matching.regions", Required));
        } else if parse_region_order(&m.regions.join(",")).is_err() {
            out.push(FieldError::new("matching.regions", Invalid));
        } else {
            let lower: Vec<String> = m.regions.iter().map(|r| r.trim().to_ascii_lowercase()).collect();
            if (1..lower.len()).any(|i| lower[..i].contains(&lower[i])) {
                out.push(FieldError::new("matching.regions", Duplicate));
            }
        }
        let lang_ok = |l: &String| l.len() == 2 && l.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase());
        if m.languages.len() > 10 || !m.languages.iter().all(lang_ok) {
            out.push(FieldError::new("matching.languages", Invalid));
        }
        simple(&mut out, "matching.auto_threshold", f64::from(m.auto_threshold));
        simple(&mut out, "matching.review_threshold", f64::from(m.review_threshold));
        if m.review_threshold > m.auto_threshold {
            out.push(FieldError::new("matching.review_threshold", Order));
        }
        simple(&mut out, "matching.max_hash_mb", f64::from(m.max_hash_mb));
        let order = &self.sources.order;
        if order.iter().any(|e| !KNOWN_SOURCES.contains(&e.id.as_str())) {
            out.push(FieldError::new("sources.order", Unknown));
        } else if (1..order.len()).any(|i| order[..i].iter().any(|o| o.id == order[i].id)) {
            out.push(FieldError::new("sources.order", Duplicate));
        } else if !order.iter().any(|e| e.enabled) {
            out.push(FieldError::new("sources.order", Required));
        }
        let n = &self.network;
        simple(&mut out, "network.concurrency", f64::from(n.concurrency));
        simple(&mut out, "network.timeout_secs", f64::from(n.timeout_secs));
        simple(&mut out, "network.retries", f64::from(n.retries));
        simple(&mut out, "network.cache_max_mb", f64::from(n.cache_max_mb));
        simple(&mut out, "network.index_refresh_hours", f64::from(n.index_refresh_hours));
        if n.proxy == ProxyChoice::Manual {
            let url = n.proxy_url.trim();
            if url.is_empty() {
                out.push(FieldError::new("network.proxy_url", Required));
            } else if !(url.starts_with("http://") || url.starts_with("https://"))
                || url.chars().any(char::is_whitespace)
            {
                out.push(FieldError::new("network.proxy_url", Invalid));
            }
        }
        let s = &self.safety;
        if !s.backup_before_overwrite {
            out.push(FieldError::new("safety.backup_before_overwrite", Invalid));
        }
        if s.backup_dir.as_ref().is_some_and(|d| !d.is_absolute()) {
            out.push(FieldError::new("safety.backup_dir", NotAbsolute));
        }
        simple(&mut out, "safety.undo_depth", f64::from(s.undo_depth));
        if self.advanced.custom_dir.as_ref().is_some_and(|d| !d.is_absolute()) {
            out.push(FieldError::new("advanced.custom_dir", NotAbsolute));
        }
        out
    }
}
```

`crates/coverly-core/src/settings/patch.rs`:

```rust
//! Changes from the UI arrive as JSON merge patches (RFC 7386): objects
//! merge, everything else (arrays included) replaces, `null` removes (an
//! optional setting becomes `None`, any other its default).

use serde_json::Value;

use super::model::Settings;
use super::validate::{FieldError, FieldErrorCode};

/// Applies `patch` onto `target` in place.
pub fn merge_patch(target: &mut Value, patch: &Value) {
    let Value::Object(patch) = patch else {
        *target = patch.clone();
        return;
    };
    if !target.is_object() {
        *target = Value::Object(serde_json::Map::new());
    }
    let map = target.as_object_mut().expect("just made an object");
    for (key, value) in patch {
        if value.is_null() {
            map.remove(key);
        } else {
            merge_patch(map.entry(key.clone()).or_insert(Value::Null), value);
        }
    }
}

/// Dotted paths of the leaves of `patch` (objects are walked, anything
/// else is a leaf) with the one-leaf patch that sets each.
fn leaves(patch: &Value, prefix: &str, out: &mut Vec<(String, Value)>) {
    match patch {
        Value::Object(map) if !map.is_empty() => {
            for (k, v) in map {
                let path = if prefix.is_empty() { k.clone() } else { format!("{prefix}.{k}") };
                leaves(v, &path, out);
            }
        }
        leaf => {
            let mut single = leaf.clone();
            for part in prefix.rsplit('.') {
                let mut m = serde_json::Map::new();
                m.insert(part.to_string(), single);
                single = Value::Object(m);
            }
            out.push((prefix.to_string(), single));
        }
    }
}

impl Settings {
    /// `self` with `patch` applied, normalised and validated; nothing is
    /// saved. A value of the wrong type is `Invalid` on its own field.
    pub fn with_patch(
        &self,
        patch: &Value,
        known_profiles: &[String],
    ) -> Result<Settings, Vec<FieldError>> {
        let current = serde_json::to_value(self).expect("settings serialise");
        let mut merged = current.clone();
        merge_patch(&mut merged, patch);
        match serde_json::from_value::<Settings>(merged) {
            Ok(mut next) => {
                let errors = next.validate(known_profiles);
                if !errors.is_empty() {
                    return Err(errors);
                }
                next.normalise();
                Ok(next)
            }
            Err(_) => {
                let mut all = Vec::new();
                leaves(patch, "", &mut all);
                let mut bad: Vec<FieldError> = all
                    .iter()
                    .filter(|(_, single)| {
                        let mut v = current.clone();
                        merge_patch(&mut v, single);
                        serde_json::from_value::<Settings>(v).is_err()
                    })
                    .map(|(path, _)| FieldError::new(path.clone(), FieldErrorCode::Invalid))
                    .collect();
                if bad.is_empty() {
                    bad = all
                        .into_iter()
                        .take(1)
                        .map(|(path, _)| FieldError::new(path, FieldErrorCode::Invalid))
                        .collect();
                }
                Err(bad)
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Reading and writing `settings.json` (D24).

use std::fs::{self, File};
use std::io::{self, Read, Write};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use serde::Serialize;
use serde_json::Value;

use super::model::{RecentLibrary, Settings, SETTINGS_VERSION};
use super::validate::FieldErrorCode;

pub const SETTINGS_FILE: &str = "settings.json";
/// The M3 file holding ignored folders and recents (migrated once).
pub const LEGACY_LIBRARY_FILE: &str = "library.json";
/// Larger files are not settings.
pub const MAX_SETTINGS_BYTES: u64 = 1024 * 1024;

/// What loading had to do, for a notice in the UI.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum LoadNotice {
    /// `library.json` was turned into `settings.json`.
    Migrated,
    /// The file was not settings: defaults are used, the file set aside.
    ResetCorrupt,
    /// Some fields were wrong and fell back to their defaults.
    Fixed,
    /// Written by a newer version: what this version understands is used.
    FutureVersion,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct LoadReport {
    pub notice: Option<LoadNotice>,
    /// The copy (or the set-aside file) holding what was on disk.
    pub backup: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// Dotted fields that fell back to their defaults.
    pub fixed: Vec<String>,
}

enum Read_ {
    Missing,
    TooLarge,
    Unreadable,
    Text(String),
}

fn read_capped(path: &Path) -> Read_ {
    let file = match File::open(path) {
        Ok(f) => f,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Read_::Missing,
        Err(_) => return Read_::Unreadable,
    };
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    if file.take(MAX_SETTINGS_BYTES + 1).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() {
        return Read_::Unreadable;
    }
    if bytes.len() as u64 > MAX_SETTINGS_BYTES {
        return Read_::TooLarge;
    }
    match String::from_utf8(bytes) {
        Ok(text) => Read_::Text(text),
        Err(_) => Read_::TooLarge,
    }
}

fn now() -> i64 {
    crate::time::now_unix()
}

/// `settings.json.<tag>-<unix>` (with `-<n>` if taken).
fn aside_path(path: &Path, tag: &str) -> PathBuf {
    let name = path.file_name().map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
    let base = format!("{name}.{tag}-{}", now());
    let mut candidate = path.with_file_name(&base);
    let mut n = 1;
    while candidate.exists() {
        candidate = path.with_file_name(format!("{base}-{n}"));
        n += 1;
    }
    candidate
}

/// Copies `settings.json` aside; `None` when there is no file.
pub fn backup_settings(dir: &Path, tag: &str) -> io::Result<Option<PathBuf>> {
    let path = dir.join(SETTINGS_FILE);
    let bytes = match fs::read(&path) {
        Ok(b) => b,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
        Err(e) => return Err(e),
    };
    let dest = aside_path(&path, tag);
    write_synced(&dest, &bytes)?;
    Ok(Some(dest))
}

fn write_synced(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) -> io::Result<()> {
    let mut file = File::create(path)?;
    file.write_all(bytes)?;
    file.sync_all()
}

/// Writes `settings.json` in `dir` atomically (temp file, fsync, rename).
pub fn save_settings(dir: &Path, s: &Settings) -> io::Result<()> {
    fs::create_dir_all(dir)?;
    let mut text = serde_json::to_string_pretty(s).map_err(io::Error::other)?;
    text.push('\n');
    let tmp = dir.join(format!(".{SETTINGS_FILE}.tmp"));
    if let Err(e) = write_synced(&tmp, text.as_bytes()).and_then(|()| fs::rename(&tmp, dir.join(SETTINGS_FILE))) {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp);
        return Err(e);
    }
    Ok(())
}

struct Parsed {
    settings: Settings,
    fixed: Vec<String>,
    future: bool,
}

fn get_path<'a>(v: &'a Value, dotted: &str) -> Option<&'a Value> {
    dotted.split('.').try_fold(v, |v, k| v.get(k))
}

/// Resets the group field `group.field` of `s` (only the first two parts
/// of `dotted` count) to its default.
fn reset_field(s: &mut Settings, dotted: &str) {
    let mut parts = dotted.splitn(3, '.');
    let (Some(group), Some(field)) = (parts.next(), parts.next()) else { return };
    let defaults = serde_json::to_value(Settings::default()).expect("defaults serialise");
    let mut v = serde_json::to_value(&*s).expect("settings serialise");
    if let (Some(slot), Some(def)) = (
        v.get_mut(group).and_then(|g| g.get_mut(field)),
        get_path(&defaults, &format!("{group}.{field}")),
    ) {
        *slot = def.clone();
        if let Ok(next) = serde_json::from_value(v) {
            *s = next;
        }
    }
}

/// Field-level tolerant parse (see the module docs); `None` when `text` is
/// not a JSON object.
fn parse_tolerant(text: &str, known: &[String]) -> Option<Parsed> {
    let Ok(Value::Object(user)) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(text) else {
        return None;
    };
    let future = user
        .get("version")
        .and_then(Value::as_u64)
        .is_some_and(|v| v > u64::from(SETTINGS_VERSION));
    let mut merged = serde_json::to_value(Settings::default()).expect("defaults serialise");
    let mut fixed = Vec::new();
    for (group, fields) in &user {
        if group == "version" {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(defaults) = merged.get(group).and_then(Value::as_object).cloned() else {
            continue; // an unknown group (newer version): ignored
        };
        let Value::Object(fields) = fields else {
            fixed.push(group.clone());
            continue;
        };
        for (field, value) in fields {
            if !defaults.contains_key(field) {
                continue; // an unknown field: ignored
            }
            let before = merged[group.as_str()][field.as_str()].clone();
            merged[group.as_str()][field.as_str()] = value.clone();
            if serde_json::from_value::<Settings>(merged.clone()).is_err() {
                merged[group.as_str()][field.as_str()] = before;
                fixed.push(format!("{group}.{field}"));
            }
        }
    }
    let mut settings: Settings = serde_json::from_value(merged).ok()?;
    for _ in 0..3 {
        let errors = settings.validate(known);
        if errors.is_empty() {
            break;
        }
        for e in errors {
            if e.code == FieldErrorCode::Order {
                reset_field(&mut settings, "matching.auto_threshold");
                reset_field(&mut settings, "matching.review_threshold");
            } else {
                reset_field(&mut settings, &e.field);
            }
            let key: String = e.field.splitn(3, '.').take(2).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(".");
            if !fixed.contains(&key) {
                fixed.push(key);
            }
        }
    }
    if !settings.validate(known).is_empty() {
        return Some(Parsed { settings: Settings::default(), fixed, future });
    }
    settings.normalise();
    Some(Parsed { settings, fixed, future })
}

/// Loads `dir/settings.json` (see the module docs). Side effects: a file
/// that is not settings is renamed aside; a file loading changed is
/// copied aside; a missing file is migrated from `library.json`.
pub fn load_settings(dir: &Path, known: &[String]) -> (Settings, LoadReport) {
    let path = dir.join(SETTINGS_FILE);
    let corrupt = |path: &Path| {
        let dest = aside_path(path, "bad");
        let backup = fs::rename(path, &dest).ok().map(|()| dest);
        (
            Settings::default(),
            LoadReport { notice: Some(LoadNotice::ResetCorrupt), backup, fixed: Vec::new() },
        )
    };
    match read_capped(&path) {
        Read_::Missing => migrate_legacy(dir),
        Read_::Unreadable => (
            Settings::default(),
            LoadReport { notice: Some(LoadNotice::ResetCorrupt), backup: None, fixed: Vec::new() },
        ),
        Read_::TooLarge => corrupt(&path),
        Read_::Text(text) => match parse_tolerant(&text, known) {
            None => corrupt(&path),
            Some(p) => {
                let notice = if p.future {
                    Some(LoadNotice::FutureVersion)
                } else if !p.fixed.is_empty() {
                    Some(LoadNotice::Fixed)
                } else {
                    None
                };
                let backup = notice.and_then(|_| backup_settings(dir, "bak").ok().flatten());
                (p.settings, LoadReport { notice, backup, fixed: p.fixed })
            }
        },
    }
}

/// Like [`load_settings`] without touching the disk (the CLI).
pub fn read_settings(path: &Path, known: &[String]) -> Settings {
    match read_capped(path) {
        Read_::Text(text) => parse_tolerant(&text, known).map(|p| p.settings).unwrap_or_default(),
        _ => Settings::default(),
    }
}

/// `library.json` → `settings.json` (ignored folders + recents), once.
fn migrate_legacy(dir: &Path) -> (Settings, LoadReport) {
    let legacy = dir.join(LEGACY_LIBRARY_FILE);
    let Read_::Text(text) = read_capped(&legacy) else {
        return (Settings::default(), LoadReport::default());
    };
    let Ok(Value::Object(old)) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&text) else {
        return (
            Settings::default(),
            LoadReport { notice: Some(LoadNotice::ResetCorrupt), backup: Some(legacy), fixed: Vec::new() },
        );
    };
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    if let Some(Value::Array(dirs)) = old.get("ignored_dirs") {
        s.library.set_ignored_dirs(dirs.iter().filter_map(|d| d.as_str().map(String::from)).collect());
    }
    if let Some(Value::Array(recent)) = old.get("recent") {
        s.library.recent = recent
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|r| serde_json::from_value::<RecentLibrary>(r.clone()).ok())
            .collect();
    }
    s.normalise();
    let _ = save_settings(dir, &s);
    (s, LoadReport { notice: Some(LoadNotice::Migrated), backup: Some(legacy), fixed: Vec::new() })
}
```

```rust
//! Settings reach the scan, the plan and the art output.

mod common;

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::Path;

use coverly_core::gather::{GatherItem, GatherTargetSummary, Gathered};
use coverly_core::identify::names::{languages, parse_name, Region};
use coverly_core::identify::{IdMethod, Identity, MatchMethod};
use coverly_core::image::{process_image, Fit, ImageLimits, OutputSpec};
use coverly_core::matching::StrategyKind;
use coverly_core::net::testing::png_bytes;
use coverly_core::plan::{build_plan, PlanAction, PlanOptions};
use coverly_core::profiles::{ImageFormat, Media};
use coverly_core::scan::scan_library;
use coverly_core::settings::{MaxSize, OutputFormat, OverwriteSetting, Settings};
use coverly_core::sources::{Candidate, MatchKind};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_core::write::OverwritePolicy;

#[test]
fn scan_options_follow_the_library_group() {
    let lib = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let r = lib.path();
    common::mkdir(r, "anbernic");
    common::write(r, "ROMS/GBA/Keep.gba", "a");
    common::write(r, "ROMS/GBA/Junk/Skip.gba", "b");
    common::write(r, "ROMS/GBA/.hidden.gba", "c");
    common::write(r, "ROMS/GBA/Odd.zzz", "d");
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.library.set_ignored_dirs(vec!["junk".into()]);
    s.library.set_ignored_exts(vec!["gba".into()]);
    s.library.include_hidden = true;
    s.library.follow_symlinks = true;
    let opts = s.scan_options();
    assert!(opts.include_hidden && opts.follow_symlinks);
    assert_eq!(opts.ignored_dirs, ["junk"]);
    assert_eq!(opts.ignored_exts, ["gba"]);

    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.library.set_ignored_dirs(vec!["junk".into()]);
    let scan = scan_library(r, &common::profile("anbernic-stock"), &SystemDb::builtin(), &s.scan_options());
    let stems: Vec<String> = scan
        .systems
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|sys| sys.roms.iter().map(|rom| rom.stem.clone()))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(stems, ["Keep"], "Junk/ is ignored, hidden files are not scanned");
}

#[test]
fn fill_settings_map_the_matching_group() {
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.matching.strategy = StrategyKind::Priority;
    s.matching.regions = vec!["japan".into(), "rom".into()];
    s.matching.languages = vec!["es".into()];
    s.matching.auto_threshold = 0.95;
    s.matching.review_threshold = 0.5;
    s.matching.fuzzy = false;
    s.matching.arcade_translation = false;
    s.matching.match_method = MatchMethod::NameOnly;
    s.matching.max_hash_mb = 8;
    s.art.media = vec![Media::Cover, Media::Title];
    s.network.index_refresh_hours = 6;
    let f = s.fill_settings();
    assert_eq!(f.strategy, StrategyKind::Priority);
    assert_eq!(f.regions.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(f.languages, ["es"]);
    assert_eq!((f.auto_threshold, f.review_threshold), (0.95, 0.5));
    assert!(!f.fuzzy && !f.arcade_translation);
    assert_eq!(f.match_method, MatchMethod::NameOnly);

    assert_eq!(f.media, [Media::Cover, Media::Title]);

fn cand(name: &str) -> Candidate {
    Candidate {
        source: "libretro".into(),
        media: Media::Cover,
        name: name.into(),
        url: format!("https://x/{name}.png"),
        fallback_url: None,
        regions: vec![Region::Europe],
        kind: MatchKind::TitleExact,
        score: 100,
        votes: None,
        consensus: 0,
    }
}

fn one_rom_plan(root: &Path, cands: Vec<Candidate>, opts: &PlanOptions) -> Option<String> {
    let profile = common::profile("anbernic-stock");
    let system_dir = root.join("ROMS/GBA");
    let gathered = Gathered {
        items: vec![GatherItem {
            target: GatherTargetSummary {
                system_id: "gba".into(),
                folder: "GBA".into(),
                system_dir: system_dir.clone(),
                rel_dir: "".into(),
                path: system_dir.join("Final Fantasy.gba"),
                stem: "Final Fantasy".into(),
                media: vec![Media::Cover],
            },
            identity: Identity {
                canonical: None,
                method: IdMethod::Name,
                confidence: 0.0,
                parsed: parse_name("Final Fantasy", false),
                hash: None,
            },
            candidates: BTreeMap::from([(Media::Cover, cands)]),
            errors: BTreeMap::new(),
        }],
        ..Gathered::default()
    };
    let plan = build_plan(root, &profile, &SystemDb::builtin(), &gathered, opts);
    match &plan.items[0].action {
        PlanAction::Auto { chosen, .. } | PlanAction::Review { chosen, .. } => Some(chosen.name.clone()),
        PlanAction::None { .. } => None,
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_preferred_language_breaks_a_strategy_tie() {
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cands = || vec![cand("Final Fantasy (Europe) (De,Fr)"), cand("Final Fantasy (Europe) (Es,It)")];
    let none = PlanOptions::default();
    assert_eq!(one_rom_plan(root.path(), cands(), &none).unwrap(), "Final Fantasy (Europe) (De,Fr)");
    let italian = PlanOptions { languages: vec!["it".into()], ..PlanOptions::default() };
    assert_eq!(one_rom_plan(root.path(), cands(), &italian).unwrap(), "Final Fantasy (Europe) (Es,It)");
    let german_first = PlanOptions { languages: vec!["ja".into(), "de".into()], ..PlanOptions::default() };
    assert_eq!(one_rom_plan(root.path(), cands(), &german_first).unwrap(), "Final Fantasy (Europe) (De,Fr)");
}

#[test]
fn language_never_beats_a_stronger_match() {
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let mut weaker = cand("Final Fantasy (Europe) (Es,It)");
    weaker.kind = MatchKind::Fuzzy;
    weaker.score = 90;
    let cands = vec![cand("Final Fantasy (Europe) (De,Fr)"), weaker];
    let italian = PlanOptions { languages: vec!["it".into()], ..PlanOptions::default() };
    assert_eq!(one_rom_plan(root.path(), cands, &italian).unwrap(), "Final Fantasy (Europe) (De,Fr)");
}

#[test]
fn stretch_fills_the_box_exactly_and_contain_keeps_the_aspect() {
    let src = png_bytes(100, 50);
    let spec = |fit| OutputSpec { format: ImageFormat::Png, max_size: Some((40, 40)), fit, jpeg_quality: 90 };
    let img = process_image(&src, &spec(Fit::Stretch), &ImageLimits::default()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((img.width, img.height), (40, 40));
    let img = process_image(&src, &spec(Fit::Contain), &ImageLimits::default()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((img.width, img.height), (40, 20));
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    assert_eq!(s.fit(), Fit::Contain);
    s.art.keep_aspect = false;
    assert_eq!(s.fit(), Fit::Stretch);
}

#[test]
fn art_profile_moves_the_single_slot_and_overrides_format_and_size() {
    let base = common::profile("anbernic-stock");
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.art.single_slot_media = Media::Screenshot;
    s.art.output_format = OutputFormat::Jpg;
    s.art.max_size.insert("anbernic-stock".into(), MaxSize { width: 320, height: 240 });
    let p = s.art_profile(&base);
    assert!(p.slot(Media::Cover).is_none());
    let slot = p.slot(Media::Screenshot).expect("the one slot holds screenshots");
    assert_eq!(slot.format, ImageFormat::Jpg);
    assert!(slot.template.ends_with("{stem}.jpg"), "{}", slot.template);
    assert_eq!(slot.max_size, Some((320, 240)));
    // Idempotent: applying again changes nothing.
    assert_eq!(s.art_profile(&p), p);
    // Defaults leave a profile as it is.
    assert_eq!(Settings::default().art_profile(&base), base);
}

#[test]
fn multi_slot_layouts_keep_their_slots_and_retroarch_stays_png() {
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.art.single_slot_media = Media::Title;
    s.art.output_format = OutputFormat::Jpg;
    let esde = s.art_profile(&common::profile("esde"));
    assert_eq!(esde.slots.len(), 3);
    assert!(esde.slot(Media::Cover).unwrap().template.ends_with(".jpg"));
    let ra = s.art_profile(&common::profile("retroarch"));
    assert!(ra.fixed_format);
    assert_eq!(ra.slot(Media::Cover).unwrap().format, ImageFormat::Png);
    assert!(ra.slot(Media::Cover).unwrap().template.ends_with(".png"));
}
```

In `crates/coverly-core/src/identify/names.rs` (next to the region parsing, reusing the private `split_groups`):

```rust
/// No-Intro language codes (as they appear: `En`, `Fr`…).
const LANGUAGE_CODES: [&str; 26] = [
    "En", "Fr", "De", "Es", "It", "Nl", "Pt", "Sv", "No", "Da", "Fi", "Ja", "Zh", "Ko", "Pl",
    "Ru", "Ca", "Eu", "Gd", "Cs", "Hu", "El", "Tr", "Ar", "He", "Hr",
];

/// The languages of a No-Intro name's `(En,Fr,De)` group(s), lowercase, in
/// order. A group counts only when every comma-separated token is a code.
pub fn languages(name: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let (_, groups) = split_groups(name);
    let mut out: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    for group in &groups {
        let tokens: Vec<&str> = group.split(',').map(str::trim).collect();
        if !tokens.is_empty() && tokens.iter().all(|t| LANGUAGE_CODES.contains(t)) {
            for t in tokens {
                let code = t.to_ascii_lowercase();
                if !out.contains(&code) {
                    out.push(code);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    out
}
```

> `split_groups` returns `(outside, groups)` where each group is the text inside one `(...)`/`[...]` (check its exact signature in `names.rs`; adapt the destructuring if the groups carry brackets).

In `crates/coverly-core/src/plan/mod.rs`: add to `PlanOptions` `pub languages: Vec<String>,` (doc: "Preferred languages (D28); empty = no preference") and `languages: Vec::new(),` to its `Default`. Add:

```rust
/// D28: among the leading candidates the strategy ranks equal (same kind,
/// score and regions as the first), the one carrying the earliest
/// preferred language comes first. Stable, so ties keep the strategy order.
fn prefer_languages(order: &mut [usize], cands: &[Candidate], langs: &[String]) {
    if langs.is_empty() || order.len() < 2 {
        return;
    }
    let Some(top) = order.first().and_then(|&i| cands.get(i)) else {
        return;
    };
    let tied = order
        .iter()
        .take_while(|&&i| {
            cands.get(i).is_some_and(|c| {
                c.kind == top.kind && c.score == top.score && c.regions == top.regions
            })
        })
        .count();
    let rank = |i: usize| {
        let have = crate::identify::names::languages(&cands[i].name);
        langs
            .iter()
            .position(|l| have.contains(l))
            .unwrap_or(langs.len())
    };
    order[..tied].sort_by_key(|&i| rank(i));
}
```

and in `build_plan`, replace `let order = strategy.rank(&ctx, cands);` with:
