```rust
    if let Some((mw, mh)) = spec.max_size {
        if mw == 0 || mh == 0 {
            return Err(ImageError::Encode(format!("empty target box {mw}x{mh}")));
        }
        if spec.fit == Fit::Stretch {
            if (img.width(), img.height()) != (mw, mh) {
                img = img.resize_exact(mw, mh, FilterType::Lanczos3);
            }
        } else {
            if img.width() > mw || img.height() > mh {
                img = img.resize(mw, mh, FilterType::Lanczos3);
            }
            if let Fit::Pad { rgba } = spec.fit {
                let mut canvas = RgbaImage::from_pixel(mw, mh, Rgba(rgba));
                let (x, y) = ((mw - img.width()) / 2, (mh - img.height()) / 2);
                ::image::imageops::overlay(&mut canvas, &img.to_rgba8(), i64::from(x), i64::from(y));
                img = DynamicImage::ImageRgba8(canvas);
            }
        }
    }
```

```rust
/// What one fill does (spec §9 defaults; `Settings::fill_settings` builds
/// it from the user's settings).
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct FillSettings {
    /// System folders or ids (empty = all).
    pub systems: Vec<String>,
    pub media: Vec<Media>,
    pub scope: Scope,
    pub strategy: StrategyKind,
    pub regions: Vec<RegionPref>,
    pub match_method: MatchMethod,
    /// Ignore cached candidate lists and indexes.
    pub refresh: bool,
    /// Larger ROMs are identified by name.
    pub hash_max_bytes: u64,
    pub arcade_translation: bool,
    pub fuzzy: bool,
    pub auto_threshold: f32,
    pub review_threshold: f32,
    /// Preferred languages (D28).
    pub languages: Vec<String>,
    /// Enabled source ids, preferred first.
    pub sources: Vec<String>,
    /// How long a listing stays fresh.
    pub index_ttl_secs: i64,
}

impl Default for FillSettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let plan = PlanOptions::default();
        FillSettings {
            systems: Vec::new(),
            media: vec![Media::Cover],
            scope: Scope::Missing,
            strategy: StrategyKind::RomRegion,
            regions: default_region_order(),
            match_method: MatchMethod::HashThenName,
            refresh: false,
            hash_max_bytes: crate::identify::hash::DEFAULT_HASH_MAX_BYTES,
            arcade_translation: true,
            fuzzy: true,
            auto_threshold: plan.auto_threshold,
            review_threshold: plan.review_threshold,
            languages: Vec::new(),
            sources: plan.sources,
            index_ttl_secs: crate::sources::libretro::INDEX_TTL_SECS,
        }
    }
}
```

`crates/coverly-core/src/settings/apply.rs` (and `mod apply;` in `settings/mod.rs`):

```rust
//! Settings → the option structs the scan, fill and writes take.

use crate::fill::FillSettings;
use crate::image::Fit;
use crate::matching::{default_region_order, parse_region_order};
use crate::profiles::{ImageFormat, Profile};
use crate::scan::ScanOptions;
use crate::write::OverwritePolicy;

use super::model::{OutputFormat, OverwriteSetting, Settings};

/// `template` with its image extension set to `format`'s.
fn with_extension(template: &str, format: ImageFormat) -> String {
    let ext = match format {
        ImageFormat::Png => "png",
        ImageFormat::Jpg => "jpg",
    };
    for old in [".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg"] {
        if let Some(stem) = template.strip_suffix(old) {
            return format!("{stem}.{ext}");
        }
    }
    template.to_string()
}

impl Settings {
    /// The Library group as scan options.
    pub fn scan_options(&self) -> ScanOptions {
        ScanOptions {
            include_hidden: self.library.include_hidden,
            follow_symlinks: self.library.follow_symlinks,
            ignored_dirs: self.library.ignored_dirs.clone(),
            ignored_exts: self.library.ignored_exts.clone(),
        }
    }

    /// The Art media + Matching + Sources groups as fill settings (systems,
    /// scope and refresh keep their defaults; callers set them).
    pub fn fill_settings(&self) -> FillSettings {
        let m = &self.matching;
        FillSettings {
            media: self.art.media.clone(),
            strategy: m.strategy,
            regions: parse_region_order(&m.regions.join(",")).unwrap_or_else(|_| default_region_order()),
            match_method: m.match_method,
            hash_max_bytes: u64::from(m.max_hash_mb) * 1024 * 1024,
            arcade_translation: m.arcade_translation,
            fuzzy: m.fuzzy,
            auto_threshold: m.auto_threshold,
            review_threshold: m.review_threshold,
            languages: m.languages.clone(),
            sources: self
                .sources
                .order
                .iter()
                .filter(|e| e.enabled)
                .map(|e| e.id.clone())
                .collect(),
            index_ttl_secs: i64::from(self.network.index_refresh_hours) * 3600,
            ..FillSettings::default()
        }
    }

    /// `base` with the Art group applied: the one slot of a single-slot
    /// layout holds `single_slot_media`; the output format (unless the
    /// layout fixes it) and the per-profile max size override the slots.
    /// Idempotent.
    pub fn art_profile(&self, base: &Profile) -> Profile {
        let mut p = base.clone();
        if p.slots.len() == 1 {
            if let Some((_, slot)) = p.slots.drain().next() {
                p.slots.insert(self.art.single_slot_media, slot);
            }
        }
        let format = match (p.fixed_format, self.art.output_format) {
            (true, _) | (_, OutputFormat::Profile) => None,
            (false, OutputFormat::Png) => Some(ImageFormat::Png),
            (false, OutputFormat::Jpg) => Some(ImageFormat::Jpg),
        };
        let size = self.art.max_size.get(&p.id).map(|m| (m.width, m.height));
        for slot in p.slots.values_mut() {
            if let Some(f) = format {
                slot.format = f;
                slot.template = with_extension(&slot.template, f);
            }
            if size.is_some() {
                slot.max_size = size;
            }
        }
        p
    }

    /// How images fit the slot box ("keep aspect").
    pub fn fit(&self) -> Fit {
        if self.art.keep_aspect {
            Fit::Contain
        } else {
            Fit::Stretch
        }
    }

    /// The overwrite policy inside a job (D25: `ask` is asked before the
    /// job starts, so inside it, it is `never` unless the user chose to
    /// replace).
    pub fn overwrite_policy(&self) -> OverwritePolicy {
        match self.art.overwrite {
            OverwriteSetting::Backup => OverwritePolicy::Backup,
            OverwriteSetting::Never | OverwriteSetting::Ask => OverwritePolicy::Never,
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Network, cache and safety settings.

mod common;

use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime};

use coverly_core::cache::{cache_usage, clear_cache, Cache, CACHE_FILE};
use coverly_core::jobs::{trim_art_store, ArtStore};
use coverly_core::net::ProxyMode;
use coverly_core::settings::{ProxyChoice, Settings};
use coverly_core::write::{clean_apple_doubles, on_removable_volume};

const APPLE_DOUBLE: [u8; 8] = [0x00, 0x05, 0x16, 0x07, 0x00, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00];

#[test]
fn net_config_follows_the_network_group() {
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.network.concurrency = 2;
    s.network.timeout_secs = 12;
    s.network.retries = 0;
    let n = s.net_config();
    assert_eq!(n.concurrency, 2);
    assert_eq!(n.timeout, Duration::from_secs(12));
    assert_eq!(n.retries, 0);
    assert_eq!(n.proxy, ProxyMode::Env, "system reads the environment (D26)");
    s.network.proxy = ProxyChoice::Env;
    assert_eq!(s.net_config().proxy, ProxyMode::Env);
    s.network.proxy = ProxyChoice::Manual;
    s.network.proxy_url = "http://proxy.local:3128".into();
    assert_eq!(s.net_config().proxy, ProxyMode::Manual("http://proxy.local:3128".into()));
}

#[test]
fn apply_options_follow_art_network_and_safety() {
    let mut s = Settings::default();
    s.art.keep_aspect = false;
    s.art.jpeg_quality = 70;
    s.network.concurrency = 3;
    s.network.cache_max_mb = 100;
    s.safety.undo_depth = 4;
    let o = s.apply_options(Path::new("/Users/me/sd-copy"));
    assert_eq!(o.fit, coverly_core::image::Fit::Stretch);
    assert_eq!(o.jpeg_quality, 70);
    assert_eq!(o.concurrency, 3);
    assert_eq!(o.keep_jobs, 4);
    assert_eq!(o.cache_max_bytes, Some(100 * 1024 * 1024));
    assert!(!o.clean_apple_double, "not a removable volume");
    let m = s.manual_options();
    assert_eq!((m.jpeg_quality, m.keep_jobs), (70, 4));
}

#[test]
fn removable_volumes_are_recognised_by_their_mount_point() {
    if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
        assert!(on_removable_volume(Path::new("/Volumes/SD/ROMS")));
        assert!(!on_removable_volume(Path::new("/Users/me/ROMS")));
    }
    if cfg!(target_os = "linux") {
        assert!(on_removable_volume(Path::new("/media/me/SD")));
        assert!(on_removable_volume(Path::new("/run/media/me/SD")));
        assert!(!on_removable_volume(Path::new("/home/me/ROMS")));
    }
}

#[test]
fn clean_apple_doubles_removes_only_real_appledouble_files() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let d = dir.path();
    fs::write(d.join("._a.png"), APPLE_DOUBLE).unwrap();
    fs::write(d.join("._notes.txt"), b"plain text that starts with ._").unwrap();
    fs::write(d.join("a.png"), b"png").unwrap();
    fs::create_dir(d.join("._folder")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(clean_apple_doubles(d), 1);
    assert!(!d.join("._a.png").exists());
    assert!(d.join("._notes.txt").exists() && d.join("a.png").exists() && d.join("._folder").is_dir());
    assert_eq!(clean_apple_doubles(&d.join("missing")), 0);

#[test]
fn trim_art_store_drops_the_oldest_files_first() {
    let cache_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let art = cache_dir.path().join("art");
    let mut paths = Vec::new();
    for (i, size) in [300usize, 300, 300].into_iter().enumerate() {
        let sha = format!("{:040x}", i + 1);
        let path = art.join(&sha[..2]).join(&sha);
        fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        fs::write(&path, vec![0u8; size]).unwrap();
        let t = SystemTime::now() - Duration::from_secs(1000 - i as u64 * 100);
        filetime::set_file_mtime(&path, filetime::FileTime::from_system_time(t)).unwrap();
        paths.push(path);
    }
    let removed = trim_art_store(cache_dir.path(), 650).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(removed, 300);
    assert!(!paths[0].exists(), "the oldest goes first");
    assert!(paths[1].exists() && paths[2].exists());
    assert_eq!(trim_art_store(cache_dir.path(), 10_000).unwrap(), 0);
    assert_eq!(trim_art_store(&cache_dir.path().join("none"), 1).unwrap(), 0);
}

#[test]
fn a_trimmed_url_is_a_cache_miss() {
    let cache_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cache = Cache::open(&cache_dir.path().join(CACHE_FILE)).unwrap();
    let store = ArtStore::new(cache_dir.path(), &cache);
    store.put("https://x/a.png", b"image-a").unwrap();
    assert!(store.get("https://x/a.png").is_some());
    trim_art_store(cache_dir.path(), 0).unwrap();
    assert!(store.get("https://x/a.png").is_none());
}

#[test]
fn usage_and_clear_cover_the_database_and_the_art_store() {
    let cache_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    {
        let cache = Cache::open(&cache_dir.path().join(CACHE_FILE)).unwrap();
        ArtStore::new(cache_dir.path(), &cache).put("https://x/a.png", &[7u8; 4096]).unwrap();
    }
    let used = cache_usage(cache_dir.path());
    assert!(used >= 4096, "{used}");
    let freed = clear_cache(cache_dir.path()).unwrap();
    assert!(freed >= 4096);
    assert_eq!(cache_usage(cache_dir.path()), 0);
    // A fresh cache opens again after a clear.
    Cache::open(&cache_dir.path().join(CACHE_FILE)).unwrap();
    // Nothing to clear is fine.
    clear_cache(&cache_dir.path().join("none")).unwrap();
}
```

```rust
/// The first bytes of an AppleDouble file.
const APPLE_DOUBLE_MAGIC: [u8; 4] = [0x00, 0x05, 0x16, 0x07];

/// Deletes the AppleDouble files (`._*` regular files starting with the
/// AppleDouble magic) directly in `dir`; returns how many. Never recurses,
/// never touches anything else; errors only skip files.
pub fn clean_apple_doubles(dir: &Path) -> usize {
    let Ok(read) = fs::read_dir(dir) else { return 0 };
    let mut removed = 0;
    for entry in read.flatten() {
        let name = entry.file_name();
        if !name.to_string_lossy().starts_with("._") {
            continue;
        }
        let path = entry.path();
        let is_file = fs::symlink_metadata(&path).is_ok_and(|m| m.file_type().is_file());
        let mut head = [0u8; 4];
        let magic = is_file
            && File::open(&path)
                .and_then(|mut f| std::io::Read::read_exact(&mut f, &mut head))
                .is_ok()
            && head == APPLE_DOUBLE_MAGIC;
        if magic && fs::remove_file(&path).is_ok() {
            removed += 1;
        }
    }
    removed
}

/// Whether `root` is on a removable volume by its mount point (macOS
/// `/Volumes/…`; Linux `/media`, `/run/media`, `/mnt`). Other systems: no.
pub fn on_removable_volume(root: &Path) -> bool {
    let prefixes: &[&str] = if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
        &["/Volumes/"]
    } else if cfg!(target_os = "linux") {
        &["/media/", "/run/media/", "/mnt/"]
    } else {
        &[]
    };
    let text = root.to_string_lossy();
    prefixes.iter().any(|p| text.starts_with(p))
}
```

`crates/coverly-core/src/cache/mod.rs`:

```rust
fn walk_size(path: &Path) -> u64 {
    let Ok(meta) = std::fs::symlink_metadata(path) else { return 0 };
    if meta.is_file() {
        return meta.len();
    }
    if !meta.is_dir() {
        return 0;
    }
    std::fs::read_dir(path)
        .map(|r| r.flatten().map(|e| walk_size(&e.path())).sum())
        .unwrap_or(0)
}

/// Bytes used by the cache database (with its journal files) and the
/// downloaded-art store.
pub fn cache_usage(cache_dir: &Path) -> u64 {
    let db: u64 = ["", "-journal", "-wal", "-shm"]
        .iter()
        .map(|s| walk_size(&cache_dir.join(format!("{CACHE_FILE}{s}"))))
        .sum();
    db + walk_size(&cache_dir.join("art"))
}

/// Deletes the cache database and the art store; returns the bytes freed.
/// The next `Cache::open` creates a fresh database. Identity overrides and
/// job data live in the data dir and are kept.
pub fn clear_cache(cache_dir: &Path) -> std::io::Result<u64> {
    let before = cache_usage(cache_dir);
    for s in ["", "-journal", "-wal", "-shm"] {
        match std::fs::remove_file(cache_dir.join(format!("{CACHE_FILE}{s}"))) {
            Err(e) if e.kind() != std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Err(e),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    match std::fs::remove_dir_all(cache_dir.join("art")) {
        Err(e) if e.kind() != std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Err(e),
        _ => {}
    }
    Ok(before.saturating_sub(cache_usage(cache_dir)))
}
```

```rust
/// Deletes the oldest files (by modification time) of the art store until
/// it holds at most `max_bytes`; returns the bytes removed. A removed
/// file is a cache miss next time (`ArtStore::get` checks the bytes).
pub fn trim_art_store(cache_dir: &Path, max_bytes: u64) -> io::Result<u64> {
    let mut files = Vec::new();
    let art = cache_dir.join("art");
    let shards = match fs::read_dir(&art) {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(0),
        Err(e) => return Err(e),
    };
    for shard in shards.flatten() {
        let Ok(read) = fs::read_dir(shard.path()) else { continue };
        for f in read.flatten() {
            if let Ok(meta) = f.metadata() {
                if meta.is_file() {
                    let mtime = meta.modified().unwrap_or(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH);
                    files.push((mtime, meta.len(), f.path()));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let mut total: u64 = files.iter().map(|(_, len, _)| len).sum();
    files.sort();
    let mut removed = 0;
    for (_, len, path) in files {
        if total <= max_bytes {
            break;
        }
        if fs::remove_file(&path).is_ok() {
            total -= len;
            removed += len;
        }
    }
    Ok(removed)
}
```

```rust
/// Targets of the items written (filled or replaced) so far.
fn done_targets<'a>(
    items: &'a [PlanItem],
    results: &'a [Option<ItemStatus>],
) -> impl Iterator<Item = &'a Path> + 'a {
    items.iter().zip(results).filter_map(|(item, st)| {
        matches!(st, Some(ItemStatus::Filled { .. } | ItemStatus::Replaced { .. }))
            .then_some(item.target.as_path())
    })
}
```

(`results` must already hold the workers' outcomes there: move the `for (n, st) in done.into_inner()…` loop above the cleanup block if it is not.)

```rust
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::Duration;

use crate::fill::ManualOptions;
use crate::jobs::ApplyOptions;
use crate::net::{NetConfig, ProxyMode};

use super::model::ProxyChoice;

impl Settings {
    /// The Network group as HTTP settings (D26: `system` = environment).
    pub fn net_config(&self) -> NetConfig {
        let n = &self.network;
        NetConfig {
            concurrency: n.concurrency.clamp(1, 6) as usize,
            timeout: Duration::from_secs(u64::from(n.timeout_secs)),
            retries: n.retries,
            proxy: match n.proxy {
                ProxyChoice::System | ProxyChoice::Env => ProxyMode::Env,
                ProxyChoice::Manual => ProxyMode::Manual(n.proxy_url.trim().to_string()),
            },
            ..NetConfig::default()
        }
    }

    /// Apply options for a job on the library at `root` (scope, review and
    /// resume are set by the caller).
    pub fn apply_options(&self, root: &Path) -> ApplyOptions {
        ApplyOptions {
            overwrite: self.overwrite_policy(),
            concurrency: self.network.concurrency.clamp(1, 6) as usize,
            fit: self.fit(),
            jpeg_quality: self.art.jpeg_quality.clamp(1, 100),
            keep_jobs: self.safety.undo_depth.max(1) as usize,
            cache_max_bytes: Some(u64::from(self.network.cache_max_mb) * 1024 * 1024),
            clean_apple_double: self.safety.clean_apple_double
                && crate::write::on_removable_volume(root),
            ..ApplyOptions::default()
        }
    }

    pub fn manual_options(&self) -> ManualOptions {
        ManualOptions {
            fit: self.fit(),
            jpeg_quality: self.art.jpeg_quality.clamp(1, 100),
            keep_jobs: self.safety.undo_depth.max(1) as usize,
        }
    }

    /// Where jobs keep journals and backups (D27).
    pub fn data_dir(&self, default: &Path) -> PathBuf {
        self.safety
            .backup_dir
            .clone()
            .unwrap_or_else(|| default.to_path_buf())
    }

    /// Whether going from `self` to `next` changes what an open library
    /// shows (scan options, the single-slot media, the output format or
    /// the custom profiles folder): the library must be reopened.
    pub fn needs_reopen(&self, next: &Settings) -> bool {
        let lib = |s: &Settings| {
            (
                s.library.ignored_dirs.clone(),
                s.library.ignored_exts.clone(),
                s.library.include_hidden,
                s.library.follow_symlinks,
            )
        };
        lib(self) != lib(next)
            || self.art.single_slot_media != next.art.single_slot_media
            || self.art.output_format != next.art.output_format
            || self.art.max_size != next.art.max_size
            || self.advanced.custom_dir != next.advanced.custom_dir
    }
}
```

```rust
//! User-defined layout profiles (spec §5, D29).

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fs;

use coverly_core::profiles::custom::*;
use coverly_core::profiles::{builtin_profiles, ImageFormat, Media};
use coverly_core::settings::FieldErrorCode;
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;

fn builtin_ids() -> Vec<String> {
    builtin_profiles().into_iter().map(|p| p.id).collect()
}

fn sample() -> ProfileFile {
    ProfileFile {
        id: "my-sd".into(),
        name: "My SD".into(),
        roms_root: "Roms".into(),
        art_dirs: vec!["Imgs".into()],
        gamelist: false,
        slots: BTreeMap::from([(
            "cover".to_string(),
            SlotFile {
                template: "{system_dir}/{rel_dir}/Imgs/{stem}.png".into(),
                format: ImageFormat::Png,
                max_width: Some(250),
                max_height: Some(360),
            },
        )]),
    }
}

fn errors(p: &ProfileFile) -> Vec<(String, FieldErrorCode)> {
    validate_profile(p, &builtin_ids())
        .into_iter()
        .map(|e| (e.field, e.code))
        .collect()
}

fn has(p: &ProfileFile, field: &str, code: FieldErrorCode) -> bool {
    errors(p).contains(&(field.to_string(), code))
}

#[test]
fn a_valid_profile_round_trips_through_toml_and_into_a_profile() {
    let p = sample();
    assert!(errors(&p).is_empty(), "{:?}", errors(&p));
    let text = p.to_toml();
    assert!(text.contains("id = \"my-sd\""), "{text}");
    assert_eq!(ProfileFile::parse_toml(&text).unwrap(), p);
    let profile = p.to_profile();
    assert_eq!(profile.id, "my-sd");
    assert!(profile.mirror_subdirs);
    assert!(!profile.fixed_format && !profile.gamelist);
    let slot = profile.slot(Media::Cover).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(slot.max_size, Some((250, 360)));
    assert_eq!(ProfileFile::from_profile(&profile), p);
}

#[test]
fn every_builtin_profile_converts_and_validates_except_its_reserved_id() {
    for b in builtin_profiles() {
        let mut f = ProfileFile::from_profile(&b);
        assert!(has(&f, "id", FieldErrorCode::Duplicate), "{}", b.id);
        f.id = format!("copy-of-{}", b.id);
        assert!(errors(&f).is_empty(), "{}: {:?}", b.id, errors(&f));
        assert_eq!(f.to_profile().slots, b.slots, "{}", b.id);
    }
}

#[test]
fn ids_names_and_folders_are_checked() {
    let mut p = sample();
    p.id = "My SD!".into();
    assert!(has(&p, "id", FieldErrorCode::Invalid));
    p.id = "../evil".into();
    assert!(has(&p, "id", FieldErrorCode::Invalid));
    let mut p = sample();
    p.name = "  ".into();
    assert!(has(&p, "name", FieldErrorCode::Required));
    p.name = "bad\u{7}".into();
    assert!(has(&p, "name", FieldErrorCode::Invalid));
    for root in ["../up", "/abs", "a/../b", "C:\\x", "a\\b"] {
        let mut p = sample();
        p.roms_root = root.into();
        assert!(has(&p, "roms_root", FieldErrorCode::Invalid), "{root}");
    }
    let mut p = sample();
    p.art_dirs = vec!["a/b".into()];
    assert!(has(&p, "art_dirs", FieldErrorCode::Invalid));
}

#[test]
fn templates_must_stay_inside_the_library() {
    let cases = [
        ("{root}/../outside/{stem}.png", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("/abs/{stem}.png", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("Imgs/{stem}.png", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("{system_dir}/./Imgs/{stem}.png", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("{system_dir}//{stem}.png", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("{system_dir}\\{stem}.png", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("{system_dir}/{nope}/{stem}.png", FieldErrorCode::Unknown),
        ("{system_dir}/Imgs/cover.png", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("{system_dir}/Imgs/{stem}.jpg", FieldErrorCode::Invalid),
        ("", FieldErrorCode::Required),
    ];
    for (template, code) in cases {
        let mut p = sample();
        p.slots.get_mut("cover").unwrap().template = template.into();
        assert!(has(&p, "slots.cover.template", code), "{template}: {:?}", errors(&p));
    }
}

#[test]
fn slots_are_checked_as_a_set() {
    let mut p = sample();
    p.slots.clear();
    assert!(has(&p, "slots", FieldErrorCode::Required));
    let mut p = sample();
    p.slots.insert(
        "marquee".into(),
        SlotFile { template: "{system_dir}/{stem}.png".into(), format: ImageFormat::Png, max_width: None, max_height: None },
    );
    assert!(has(&p, "slots.marquee", FieldErrorCode::Unknown));
    let mut p = sample();
    p.slots.insert(
        "title".into(),
        SlotFile { template: "{system_dir}/titles/{stem}.png".into(), format: ImageFormat::Png, max_width: None, max_height: None },
    );
    assert!(has(&p, "slots", FieldErrorCode::Order), "{{rel_dir}} in one slot only");
    let mut p = sample();
    p.slots.get_mut("cover").unwrap().max_width = Some(8);
    assert!(has(&p, "slots.cover.max_width", FieldErrorCode::Range));
}

#[test]
fn preview_renders_library_relative_paths() {
    let mut p = sample();
    p.slots.insert(
        "title".into(),
        SlotFile { template: "{root}/thumbs/{libretro}/{rel_dir}/{stem}.png".into(), format: ImageFormat::Png, max_width: None, max_height: None },
    );
    let ex = PreviewExample {
        system_folder: "SFC".into(),
        rel_dir: "Hacks".into(),
        file_name: "Zelda (USA).sfc".into(),
    };
    let paths = preview_paths(&p, &ex, &SystemDb::builtin());
    assert_eq!(paths["cover"], "Roms/SFC/Hacks/Imgs/Zelda (USA).png");
    assert_eq!(
        paths["title"],
        "thumbs/Nintendo - Super Nintendo Entertainment System/Hacks/Zelda (USA).png"
    );
    let mut broken = p.clone();
    broken.slots.get_mut("cover").unwrap().template = "{root}/../{stem}.png".into();
    let paths = preview_paths(&broken, &ex, &SystemDb::builtin());
    assert!(!paths.contains_key("cover"), "a bad template has no preview");
}

#[test]
fn the_store_saves_renames_deletes_and_skips_hostile_files() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let store = ProfileStore::new(dir.path().join(PROFILES_DIR));
    let (none, warnings) = store.load();
    assert!(none.is_empty() && warnings.is_empty());
    let p = sample();
    store.save(&p, None).unwrap();
    let mut renamed = p.clone();
    renamed.id = "my-sd-2".into();
    store.save(&renamed, Some("my-sd")).unwrap();
    let pdir = dir.path().join(PROFILES_DIR);
    assert!(!pdir.join("my-sd.toml").exists() && pdir.join("my-sd-2.toml").exists());
    fs::write(pdir.join("broken.toml"), "id = [").unwrap();
    fs::write(pdir.join("liar.toml"), sample().to_toml()).unwrap(); // id my-sd ≠ liar
    fs::write(pdir.join("big.toml"), "#".repeat(MAX_PROFILE_BYTES as usize + 1)).unwrap();
    let mut builtin = sample();
    builtin.id = "onion".into();
    fs::write(pdir.join("onion.toml"), builtin.to_toml()).unwrap();
    fs::write(pdir.join("notes.txt"), "ignored").unwrap();
    let (loaded, warnings) = store.load();
    assert_eq!(loaded.iter().map(|p| p.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["my-sd-2"]);
    assert_eq!(warnings.len(), 4, "{warnings:?}");
    assert!(store.delete("my-sd-2").unwrap());
    assert!(!store.delete("my-sd-2").unwrap());
    assert!(store.delete("../../etc/passwd").is_err());
}

#[test]
fn custom_profiles_are_listed_after_the_builtins_and_found_by_id() {
    let customs = vec![sample()];
    let all = all_profiles(&customs);
    assert_eq!(all.len(), builtin_profiles().len() + 1);
    assert!(all[0].1, "built-ins first");
    assert_eq!(all.last().unwrap().0.id, "my-sd");
    assert!(!all.last().unwrap().1);
    assert_eq!(find_profile(&customs, "MY-SD").unwrap().id, "my-sd");
    assert_eq!(find_profile(&customs, "onion").unwrap().id, "onion");
    assert!(find_profile(&customs, "nope").is_none());
}

fn only_batocera_writes_a_gamelist() {
    for p in builtin_profiles() {
        assert_eq!(p.gamelist, p.id == "batocera", "{}", p.id);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `profiles/custom.rs`**

```rust
//! User-defined layout profiles (spec §5, D29): one TOML file per profile
//! in the custom profiles folder. Files are untrusted: size-capped,
//! validated, skipped with a warning when bad. Built-ins stay read-only.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fs;
use std::io;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use super::{builtin_profiles, render, ImageFormat, Media, MediaSlot, Profile, TemplateVars};
use crate::settings::{FieldError, FieldErrorCode};
use crate::systems::SystemDb;

/// Folder (inside the config dir) holding the profiles by default.
pub const PROFILES_DIR: &str = "profiles";
/// Larger files are not profiles.
pub const MAX_PROFILE_BYTES: u64 = 64 * 1024;
/// Template tokens (see [`render`]).
pub const TOKENS: [&str; 6] = ["{root}", "{system_dir}", "{rel_dir}", "{stem}", "{system_id}", "{libretro}"];

fn default_format() -> ImageFormat {
    ImageFormat::Png
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SlotFile {
    pub template: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_format")]
    pub format: ImageFormat,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub max_width: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub max_height: Option<u32>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ProfileFile {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub roms_root: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub art_dirs: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub gamelist: bool,
    /// Keyed by `cover`, `screenshot` or `title`.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub slots: BTreeMap<String, SlotFile>,
}

pub fn media_key(m: Media) -> &'static str {
    match m {
        Media::Cover => "cover",
        Media::Screenshot => "screenshot",
        Media::Title => "title",
    }
}

fn media_of(key: &str) -> Option<Media> {
    Media::ALL.into_iter().find(|m| media_key(*m) == key)
}

impl ProfileFile {
    pub fn from_profile(p: &Profile) -> ProfileFile {
        ProfileFile {
            id: p.id.clone(),
            name: p.name.clone(),
            roms_root: p.roms_root.clone(),
            art_dirs: p.art_dir_names.clone(),
            gamelist: p.gamelist,
            slots: Media::ALL
                .into_iter()
                .filter_map(|m| {
                    p.slot(m).map(|s| {
                        (
                            media_key(m).to_string(),
                            SlotFile {
                                template: s.template.clone(),
                                format: s.format,
                                max_width: s.max_size.map(|(w, _)| w),
                                max_height: s.max_size.map(|(_, h)| h),
                            },
                        )
                    })
                })
                .collect(),
        }
    }

    /// The profile this file describes (call on a validated file).
    pub fn to_profile(&self) -> Profile {
        let slots = self
            .slots
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|(k, s)| {
                let size = match (s.max_width, s.max_height) {
                    (Some(w), Some(h)) => Some((w, h)),
                    _ => None,
                };
                media_of(k).map(|m| {
                    (m, MediaSlot { template: s.template.clone(), format: s.format, max_size: size })
                })
            })
            .collect();
        Profile {
            id: self.id.clone(),
            name: self.name.trim().to_string(),
            roms_root: self.roms_root.clone(),
            art_dir_names: self.art_dirs.clone(),
            mirror_subdirs: self.slots.values().any(|s| s.template.contains("{rel_dir}")),
            fixed_format: false,
            gamelist: self.gamelist,
            slots,
        }
    }

    pub fn parse_toml(text: &str) -> Result<ProfileFile, String> {
        toml::from_str(text).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
    }

    pub fn to_toml(&self) -> String {
        toml::to_string_pretty(self).expect("a profile file serialises")
    }
}

fn plain_name(s: &str) -> bool {
    let s = s.trim();
    !s.is_empty() && s != "." && s != ".." && !s.contains(['/', '\\', ':']) && !s.chars().any(char::is_control)
}

fn valid_id(id: &str) -> bool {
    let b = id.as_bytes();
    !b.is_empty()
        && b.len() <= 40
        && (b[0].is_ascii_lowercase() || b[0].is_ascii_digit())
        && b.iter().all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || *c == b'-')
}

/// Every token of `template` (`{…}`), in order.
fn tokens(template: &str) -> Vec<&str> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let mut rest = template;
    while let Some(open) = rest.find('{') {
        match rest[open..].find('}') {
            Some(close) => {
                out.push(&rest[open..open + close + 1]);
                rest = &rest[open + close + 1..];
            }
            None => {
                out.push(&rest[open..]);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

fn template_error(t: &SlotFile) -> Option<FieldErrorCode> {
    use FieldErrorCode::*;
    let s = t.template.trim();
    if s.is_empty() {
        return Some(Required);
    }
    if tokens(s).iter().any(|tok| !TOKENS.contains(tok)) {
        return Some(Unknown);
    }
    if !(s.starts_with("{root}/") || s.starts_with("{system_dir}/")) || s.contains('\\') || s.contains("//") {
        return Some(Invalid);
    }
    let segments: Vec<&str> = s.split('/').collect();
    if segments.iter().any(|seg| *seg == "." || *seg == ".." || seg.is_empty()) {
        return Some(Invalid);
    }
    let last = segments.last().copied().unwrap_or("");
    if !last.contains("{stem}") {
        return Some(Invalid);
    }
    let ext_ok = match t.format {
        ImageFormat::Png => last.ends_with(".png"),
        ImageFormat::Jpg => last.ends_with(".jpg") || last.ends_with(".jpeg"),
    };
    (!ext_ok).then_some(Invalid)
}

/// Problems of `p` as per-field errors (`slots.<media>.<field>`).
pub fn validate_profile(p: &ProfileFile, builtin_ids: &[String]) -> Vec<FieldError> {
    use FieldErrorCode::*;
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    if !valid_id(&p.id) {
        out.push(FieldError::new("id", Invalid));
    } else if builtin_ids.iter().any(|b| b.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&p.id)) {
        out.push(FieldError::new("id", Duplicate));
    }
    let name = p.name.trim();
    if name.is_empty() {
        out.push(FieldError::new("name", Required));
    } else if name.chars().count() > 60 || name.chars().any(char::is_control) {
        out.push(FieldError::new("name", Invalid));
    }
    let root = &p.roms_root;
    if !root.is_empty() && (root.starts_with('/') || !root.split('/').all(plain_name)) {
        out.push(FieldError::new("roms_root", Invalid));
    }
    if !p.art_dirs.iter().all(|d| plain_name(d)) {
        out.push(FieldError::new("art_dirs", Invalid));
    }
    if p.slots.is_empty() {
        out.push(FieldError::new("slots", Required));
    }
    for (key, slot) in &p.slots {
        if media_of(key).is_none() {
            out.push(FieldError::new(format!("slots.{key}"), Unknown));
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(code) = template_error(slot) {
            out.push(FieldError::new(format!("slots.{key}.template"), code));
        }
        for (field, v) in [("max_width", slot.max_width), ("max_height", slot.max_height)] {
            if let Some(v) = v {
                if !(16..=4096).contains(&v) {
                    out.push(FieldError {
                        field: format!("slots.{key}.{field}"),
                        code: Range,
                        min: Some(16.0),
                        max: Some(4096.0),
                    });
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let mirrors: Vec<bool> = p.slots.values().map(|s| s.template.contains("{rel_dir}")).collect();
    if mirrors.iter().any(|m| *m) && !mirrors.iter().all(|m| *m) {
        out.push(FieldError::new("slots", Order));
    }
    out
}

/// Where each valid slot of `p` puts the art of the example ROM, relative
/// to the library root, `/`-separated.
pub fn preview_paths(p: &ProfileFile, ex: &PreviewExample, systems: &SystemDb) -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
    let root = Path::new("/__library__");
    let system_dir = root.join(&p.roms_root).join(ex.system_folder.trim());
    let system = systems.resolve_folder(ex.system_folder.trim());
    let stem = Path::new(ex.file_name.trim())
        .file_stem()
        .map(|s| s.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
    for (key, slot) in &p.slots {
        if media_of(key).is_none() || template_error(slot).is_some() {
            continue;
        }
        let path = render(
            &slot.template,
            &TemplateVars {
                root,
                system_dir: &system_dir,
                rel_dir: Path::new(ex.rel_dir.trim().trim_matches('/')),
                stem: &stem,
                system_id: system.map_or("unknown", |s| s.id.as_str()),
                libretro: system.and_then(|s| s.libretro.as_deref()),
            },
        );
        if let Ok(rel) = path.strip_prefix(root) {
            let shown: Vec<String> = rel.components().map(|c| c.as_os_str().to_string_lossy().into_owned()).collect();
            out.insert(key.clone(), shown.join("/"));
        }
    }
    out
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PreviewExample {
    pub system_folder: String,
    pub rel_dir: String,
    pub file_name: String,
}

/// The custom profiles folder.
pub struct ProfileStore {
    dir: PathBuf,
}

impl ProfileStore {
    pub fn new(dir: PathBuf) -> ProfileStore {
        ProfileStore { dir }
    }

    pub fn dir(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.dir
    }

    fn path_of(&self, id: &str) -> io::Result<PathBuf> {
        if !valid_id(id) {
            return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, format!("not a profile id: {id}")));
        }
        Ok(self.dir.join(format!("{id}.toml")))
    }

    /// Every valid profile file, sorted by id; problems as warnings.
    pub fn load(&self) -> (Vec<ProfileFile>, Vec<String>) {
        let mut found = Vec::new();
        let mut warnings = Vec::new();
        let Ok(read) = fs::read_dir(&self.dir) else {
            return (found, warnings);
        };
        let builtin_ids: Vec<String> = builtin_profiles().into_iter().map(|p| p.id).collect();
        let mut entries: Vec<PathBuf> = read.flatten().map(|e| e.path()).collect();
        entries.sort();
        for path in entries {
            if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) != Some("toml") {
                continue;
            }
            let shown = path.display().to_string();
            let meta = match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
                Ok(m) if m.is_file() => m,
                _ => continue,
            };
            if meta.len() > MAX_PROFILE_BYTES {
                warnings.push(format!("{shown}: larger than {MAX_PROFILE_BYTES} bytes"));
                continue;
            }
            let parsed = fs::read_to_string(&path)
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())
                .and_then(|t| ProfileFile::parse_toml(&t));
            let file = match parsed {
                Ok(f) => f,
                Err(e) => {
                    warnings.push(format!("{shown}: {e}"));
                    continue;
                }
            };
            let stem = path.file_stem().map(|s| s.to_string_lossy().into_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
            if stem != file.id {
                warnings.push(format!("{shown}: its id `{}` does not match the file name", file.id));
                continue;
            }
            let problems = validate_profile(&file, &builtin_ids);
            if !problems.is_empty() {
                let fields: Vec<String> = problems.into_iter().map(|e| e.field).collect();
                warnings.push(format!("{shown}: invalid {}", fields.join(", ")));
                continue;
            }
            found.push(file);
        }
        (found, warnings)
    }

    /// Writes `p` (validated by the caller) atomically; with `replaces`,
    /// the old file goes once the new one is written.
    pub fn save(&self, p: &ProfileFile, replaces: Option<&str>) -> io::Result<()> {
        let path = self.path_of(&p.id)?;
        let old = replaces.map(|id| self.path_of(id)).transpose()?;
        fs::create_dir_all(&self.dir)?;
        let tmp = self.dir.join(format!(".{}.toml.tmp", p.id));
        fs::write(&tmp, p.to_toml())?;
        fs::rename(&tmp, &path)?;
        if let Some(old) = old.filter(|o| *o != path) {
            match fs::remove_file(old) {
                Err(e) if e.kind() != io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Err(e),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Deletes profile `id`; `false` when it did not exist.
    pub fn delete(&self, id: &str) -> io::Result<bool> {
        match fs::remove_file(self.path_of(id)?) {
            Ok(()) => Ok(true),
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(false),
            Err(e) => Err(e),
        }
    }
}

/// Built-ins first (`true`), then the custom ones.
pub fn all_profiles(custom: &[ProfileFile]) -> Vec<(Profile, bool)> {
    builtin_profiles()
        .into_iter()
        .map(|p| (p, true))
        .chain(custom.iter().map(|f| (f.to_profile(), false)))
        .collect()
}

/// The profile `id` (case-insensitive), built-ins first.
pub fn find_profile(custom: &[ProfileFile], id: &str) -> Option<Profile> {
    all_profiles(custom)
        .into_iter()
        .map(|(p, _)| p)
        .find(|p| p.id.eq_ignore_ascii_case(id))
}
```

```rust
//! The gamelist.xml merge (D31): only the art tags change, nothing else.

use coverly_core::gamelist::{merge, GameArt, MergeError, MAX_GAMELIST_BYTES};
use coverly_core::profiles::Media;

fn cover(rom: &str, art: &str) -> GameArt {
    GameArt {
        rom: rom.into(),
        name: rom.trim_start_matches("./").rsplit_once('.').map_or(rom, |(s, _)| s).into(),
        art: vec![(Media::Cover, art.into())],
    }
}

fn yes(_: &str) -> bool {
    true
}

fn no(_: &str) -> bool {
    false
}

#[test]
fn creates_a_gamelist_when_none_exists() {
    let out = merge(None, &[cover("./Zelda.sfc", "./images/Zelda-image.png")], false, &no)
        .unwrap()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(out.starts_with("<?xml version=\"1.0\"?>\n<gameList>"), "{out}");
    assert!(out.contains("<path>./Zelda.sfc</path>"));
    assert!(out.contains("<name>Zelda</name>"));
    assert!(out.contains("<thumbnail>./images/Zelda-image.png</thumbnail>"));
    assert!(out.contains("<image>./images/Zelda-image.png</image>"));
    // The result parses and merging again changes nothing.
    assert_eq!(merge(Some(&out), &[cover("./Zelda.sfc", "./images/Zelda-image.png")], false, &yes).unwrap(), None);
}

const EXISTING: &str = "<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"UTF-8\"?>\r\n\
<!-- scraped by Skraper -->\r\n\
<gameList>\r\n\
\t<provider><System>snes</System></provider>\r\n\

        Some(EXISTING),

        &no,

    .unwrap()
    .unwrap();
    // Inserted with the file's own line ending and indentation.

    assert!(kept.contains("<image>./images/Other-image.png</image>"));

#[test]
fn hostile_and_broken_files_are_refused() {
    let bomb = "<?xml version=\"1.0\"?><!DOCTYPE gameList [<!ENTITY a \"aaaaaaaa\"><!ENTITY b \"&a;&a;&a;&a;\">]><gameList>&b;</gameList>";
    let g = [cover("./A.sfc", "./images/A.png")];
    assert_eq!(merge(Some(bomb), &g, false, &no), Err(MergeError::Doctype));
    assert!(matches!(merge(Some("<gameList><game></gameList>"), &g, false, &no), Err(MergeError::Malformed(_))));
    assert!(matches!(merge(Some("<gameList>"), &g, false, &no), Err(MergeError::Malformed(_))));
    assert_eq!(merge(Some("<foo/>"), &g, false, &no), Err(MergeError::NoRoot));
    assert_eq!(merge(Some(""), &g, false, &no), Err(MergeError::NoRoot));
    assert_eq!(merge(Some("<gameList/><gameList/>"), &g, false, &no).map(|_| ()).unwrap_err(), MergeError::Malformed("two root elements".into()));
    let deep = format!("<gameList>{}{}</gameList>", "<a>".repeat(100), "</a>".repeat(100));
    assert!(matches!(merge(Some(&deep), &g, false, &no), Err(MergeError::Malformed(_))));
    let big = " ".repeat(MAX_GAMELIST_BYTES + 1);
    assert_eq!(merge(Some(&big), &g, false, &no), Err(MergeError::TooLarge));
}

fn only_the_first_of_duplicate_games_is_updated() {
    let existing = "<gameList><game><path>./A.sfc</path></game><game><path>./A.sfc</path></game></gameList>";

    let g = GameArt {

    assert!(!out.contains("<img"), "{out}");

    assert_eq!(merge(Some(&out), &[g], false, &yes).unwrap(), None, "the escaped path matches again");

    assert!(out.contains("<!-- note -->"));
```

```rust
//! Batocera/Knulli `gamelist.xml` (spec §16.8, M2-10, D31): after a fill,
//! the art paths are merged into `<system dir>/gamelist.xml`. The file is
//! untrusted. It is parsed with `quick-xml` only to find byte offsets; the
//! output is the input with a few spliced edits, so every byte the merge
//! does not change (declaration, BOM, comments, unknown elements and
//! attributes, line endings) is kept. Entities are never expanded and a
//! DOCTYPE is refused. The write goes through the job's `Writer` (backup +
//! journal), so Undo restores the file byte for byte.

use std::fs;
use std::io;
use std::path::{Component, Path};

use quick_xml::events::Event;
use quick_xml::Reader;
use unicode_normalization::UnicodeNormalization;

use crate::profiles::Media;
use crate::write::{OverwritePolicy, Writer};

pub const GAMELIST_FILE: &str = "gamelist.xml";
pub const MAX_GAMELIST_BYTES: usize = 32 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_DEPTH: usize = 64;

/// The art written for one ROM.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct GameArt {
    /// ROM path relative to the system dir, `./`-prefixed, `/`-separated.
    pub rom: String,
    /// Display name for a new `<game>` (the ROM stem).
    pub name: String,
    /// Media and art path (relative like `rom`).
    pub art: Vec<(Media, String)>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum MergeError {
    #[error("gamelist.xml is larger than 32 MiB")]
    TooLarge,
    #[error("gamelist.xml is not UTF-8")]
    NotUtf8,
    #[error("gamelist.xml declares a DOCTYPE; it is left as it is")]
    Doctype,
    #[error("gamelist.xml is not well-formed: {0}")]
    Malformed(String),
    #[error("gamelist.xml has no <gameList> root")]
    NoRoot,
    #[error("gamelist.xml is not a regular file")]
    NotAFile,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Mode {
    /// Set when missing, empty or pointing to a missing file; replaced in
    /// a "replace existing art" job.
    Normal,
    /// Set only when missing, empty or pointing to a missing file.
    OnlyIfUnusable,
}

/// D31: cover → thumbnail (+ image when unusable), screenshot → image,
/// title → titleshot.
fn wanted(art: &[(Media, String)]) -> Vec<(&'static str, String, Mode)> {
    let of = |m: Media| art.iter().find(|(x, _)| *x == m).map(|(_, v)| v.clone());
    let (cover, shot, title) = (of(Media::Cover), of(Media::Screenshot), of(Media::Title));
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    if let Some(c) = &cover {
        out.push(("thumbnail", c.clone(), Mode::Normal));
    }
    match (shot, &cover) {
        (Some(s), _) => out.push(("image", s, Mode::Normal)),
        (None, Some(c)) => out.push(("image", c.clone(), Mode::OnlyIfUnusable)),
        _ => {}
    }
    if let Some(t) = title {
        out.push(("titleshot", t, Mode::Normal));
    }
    out
}

/// Byte offsets of one element: `start..inner_start` is the start tag,
/// `inner_end..end` the end tag (all equal ends for `<x/>`).
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct Elem {
    name: String,
    start: usize,
    inner_start: usize,
    inner_end: usize,
    end: usize,
    empty: bool,
}

struct GameNode {
    elem: Elem,
    children: Vec<Elem>,
}

struct Doc {
    root: Elem,
    games: Vec<GameNode>,
}

fn pos(reader: &Reader<&[u8]>) -> usize {
    usize::try_from(reader.buffer_position()).unwrap_or(usize::MAX)
}

fn scan(xml: &str) -> Result<Doc, MergeError> {
    let mut reader = Reader::from_str(xml);
    reader.config_mut().check_end_names = true;
    let mut stack: Vec<(String, usize, usize)> = Vec::new();
    let mut root: Option<Elem> = None;
    let mut games = Vec::new();
    let mut current: Option<GameNode> = None;
    let malformed = |m: &str| MergeError::Malformed(m.to_string());
    loop {
        let before = pos(&reader);
        let event = reader
            .read_event()
            .map_err(|e| MergeError::Malformed(e.to_string()))?;
        let after = pos(&reader);
        match event {
            Event::DocType(_) => return Err(MergeError::Doctype),
            Event::Start(e) => {
                if stack.len() >= MAX_DEPTH {
                    return Err(malformed("nested too deeply"));
                }
                if stack.is_empty() && root.is_some() {
                    return Err(malformed("two root elements"));
                }
                let name = String::from_utf8_lossy(e.name().as_ref()).into_owned();
                if stack.len() == 1 && name == "game" {
                    current = Some(GameNode {
                        elem: Elem { name: name.clone(), start: before, inner_start: after, inner_end: after, end: after, empty: false },
                        children: Vec::new(),
                    });
                }
                stack.push((name, before, after));
            }
            Event::Empty(e) => {
                let name = String::from_utf8_lossy(e.name().as_ref()).into_owned();
                let elem = Elem { name, start: before, inner_start: after, inner_end: after, end: after, empty: true };
                match stack.len() {
                    0 if root.is_some() => return Err(malformed("two root elements")),
                    0 => root = Some(elem),
                    2 => {
                        if let Some(g) = current.as_mut() {
                            g.children.push(elem);
                        }
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
            Event::End(_) => {
                let (name, start, inner_start) = stack.pop().ok_or_else(|| malformed("unexpected end tag"))?;
                let elem = Elem { name, start, inner_start, inner_end: before, end: after, empty: false };
                match stack.len() {
                    0 => root = Some(elem),
                    1 if elem.name == "game" => {
                        if let Some(mut g) = current.take() {
                            g.elem = elem;
                            games.push(g);
                        }
                    }
                    2 => {
                        if let Some(g) = current.as_mut() {
                            g.children.push(elem);
                        }
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
            Event::Eof => break,
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    if !stack.is_empty() {
        return Err(malformed("unclosed element"));
    }
    let root = root.ok_or(MergeError::NoRoot)?;
    if root.name != "gameList" {
        return Err(MergeError::NoRoot);
    }
    Ok(Doc { root, games })
}

fn escape(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    for c in s.chars() {
        match c {
            '&' => out.push_str("&amp;"),
            '<' => out.push_str("&lt;"),
            '>' => out.push_str("&gt;"),
            '"' => out.push_str("&quot;"),
            '\'' => out.push_str("&apos;"),
            c if c.is_control() && c != '\t' => {}
            c => out.push(c),
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Decodes the five predefined entities and character references; `None`
/// for any other entity (never expanded).
fn unescape(s: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    let mut rest = s;
    while let Some(amp) = rest.find('&') {
        out.push_str(&rest[..amp]);
        let tail = &rest[amp + 1..];
        let semi = tail.find(';')?;
        let name = &tail[..semi];
        let c = match name {
            "amp" => '&',
            "lt" => '<',
            "gt" => '>',
            "quot" => '"',
            "apos" => '\'',
            n if n.starts_with("#x") || n.starts_with("#X") => char::from_u32(u32::from_str_radix(&n[2..], 16).ok()?)?,
            n if n.starts_with('#') => char::from_u32(n[1..].parse().ok()?)?,
            _ => return None,
        };
        out.push(c);
        rest = &tail[semi + 1..];
    }
    out.push_str(rest);
    Some(out)
}

/// The plain text of `e` (`None` when it holds markup or an unknown entity).
fn text_of(xml: &str, e: &Elem) -> Option<String> {
    if e.empty {
        return Some(String::new());
    }
    let raw = xml[e.inner_start..e.inner_end].trim();
    if let Some(inner) = raw.strip_prefix("<![CDATA[").and_then(|r| r.strip_suffix("]]>")) {
        return Some(inner.to_string());
    }
    if raw.contains('<') {
        return None;
    }
    unescape(raw)
}

/// Comparison form of a gamelist path.
fn norm(p: &str) -> String {
    let p = p.trim().replace('\\', "/");
    let p = p.strip_prefix("./").unwrap_or(&p);
    p.nfc().collect()
}

fn find_game(xml: &str, doc: &Doc, key: &str) -> Option<usize> {
    let paths: Vec<Option<String>> = doc
        .games
        .iter()
        .map(|g| g.children.iter().find(|c| c.name == "path").and_then(|c| text_of(xml, c)).map(|p| norm(&p)))
        .collect();
    if let Some(i) = paths.iter().position(|p| p.as_deref() == Some(key)) {
        return Some(i);
    }
    let lower = key.to_lowercase();
    let matches: Vec<usize> = paths
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .filter(|(_, p)| p.as_ref().is_some_and(|p| p.to_lowercase() == lower))
        .map(|(i, _)| i)
        .collect();
    // Two games differing only by case: the first one (as ES does).
    matches.first().copied()
}

/// Whitespace before `at` on its line (the indentation of an element).
fn indent_before(xml: &str, at: usize) -> &str {
    let line_start = xml[..at].rfind('\n').map_or(0, |i| i + 1);
    let ws = &xml[line_start..at];
    if ws.chars().all(|c| c == ' ' || c == '\t') {
        ws
    } else {
        ""
    }
}

fn newline_of(xml: &str) -> &'static str {
    if xml.contains("\r\n") {
        "\r\n"
    } else {
        "\n"
    }
}

/// Merges `games` into `existing` (a new file when `None`). `exists` says
/// whether a gamelist path (as written in the file) points to a file.
pub fn merge(
    existing: Option<&str>,
    games: &[GameArt],
    replace: bool,
    exists: &dyn Fn(&str) -> bool,
) -> Result<Option<String>, MergeError> {
    if games.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let fresh = "<?xml version=\"1.0\"?>\n<gameList>\n</gameList>\n";
    let xml = existing.unwrap_or(fresh);
    if xml.len() > MAX_GAMELIST_BYTES {
        return Err(MergeError::TooLarge);
    }
    let doc = scan(xml)?;
    let nl = newline_of(xml);
    let mut edits: Vec<(usize, usize, String)> = Vec::new();
    let mut appended = String::new();
    for game in games {
        let tags = wanted(&game.art);
        match find_game(xml, &doc, &norm(&game.rom)) {
            Some(i) => {
                let g = &doc.games[i];
                let child_indent = g
                    .children
                    .first()
                    .map(|c| indent_before(xml, c.start).to_string())
                    .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "\t\t".to_string());
                let mut inserted = String::new();
                for (tag, value, mode) in tags {
                    let escaped = escape(&value);
                    match g.children.iter().find(|c| c.name == tag) {
                        Some(child) => {
                            let current = text_of(xml, child).unwrap_or_default();
                            if norm(&current) == norm(&value) {
                                continue;
                            }
                            let usable = !current.trim().is_empty() && exists(&current);
                            let set = match mode {
                                Mode::Normal => !usable || replace,
                                Mode::OnlyIfUnusable => !usable,
                            };
                            if !set {
                                continue;
                            }
                            if child.empty {
                                edits.push((child.start, child.end, format!("<{tag}>{escaped}</{tag}>")));
                            } else {
                                edits.push((child.inner_start, child.inner_end, escaped));
                            }
                        }
                        None => {
                            inserted.push_str(&format!("{nl}{child_indent}<{tag}>{escaped}</{tag}>"));
                        }
                    }
                }
                if !inserted.is_empty() {
                    let inner = &xml[g.elem.inner_start..g.elem.inner_end];
                    let at = g.elem.inner_start + inner.trim_end().len();
                    let compact = !inner.contains('\n');
                    let text = if compact {
                        inserted.replace(&format!("{nl}{child_indent}"), "")
                    } else {
                        inserted
                    };
                    edits.push((at, at, text));
                }
            }
            None => {
                appended.push_str(&format!("{nl}\t<game>{nl}\t\t<path>{}</path>{nl}\t\t<name>{}</name>", escape(&game.rom), escape(&game.name)));
                for (tag, value, _) in tags {
                    appended.push_str(&format!("{nl}\t\t<{tag}>{}</{tag}>", escape(&value)));
                }
                appended.push_str(&format!("{nl}\t</game>"));
            }
        }
    }
    if !appended.is_empty() {
        let r = &doc.root;
        if r.empty {
            edits.push((r.start, r.end, format!("<gameList>{appended}{nl}</gameList>")));
        } else {
            let inner = &xml[r.inner_start..r.inner_end];
            let at = r.inner_start + inner.trim_end().len();
            let tail = if inner[inner.trim_end().len()..].contains('\n') { "" } else { nl };
            edits.push((at, at, format!("{appended}{tail}")));
        }
    }
    if edits.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    edits.sort_by(|a, b| b.0.cmp(&a.0));
    let mut out = xml.to_string();
    for (start, end, text) in edits {
        out.replace_range(start..end, &text);
    }
    Ok(Some(out))
}

/// `./a/b` form of a relative path.
pub fn rel_slash(p: &Path) -> String {
    let parts: Vec<String> = p.components().map(|c| c.as_os_str().to_string_lossy().into_owned()).collect();
    format!("./{}", parts.join("/"))
}

/// Merges `games` into `system_dir/gamelist.xml` through `writer`
/// (backed up and journaled; Undo restores it). `Ok(false)` when nothing
/// changed. Errors are warnings for the job report: the art is written
/// either way.
pub fn merge_into(writer: &mut Writer, system_dir: &Path, games: &[GameArt], replace: bool) -> Result<bool, String> {
    let path = system_dir.join(GAMELIST_FILE);
    let existing = match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
        Ok(m) if m.file_type().is_file() => {
            if m.len() > MAX_GAMELIST_BYTES as u64 {
                return Err(MergeError::TooLarge.to_string());
            }
            let bytes = fs::read(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            Some(String::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|_| MergeError::NotUtf8.to_string())?)
        }
        Ok(_) => return Err(MergeError::NotAFile.to_string()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => None,
        Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
    };
    let exists = |rel: &str| {
        let n = norm(rel);
        let p = Path::new(&n);
        // Absolute or `..` paths are the user's business: treat as usable.
        if p.components().any(|c| !matches!(c, Component::Normal(_))) {
            return true;
        }
        system_dir.join(p).exists()
    };
    match merge(existing.as_deref(), games, replace, &exists).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
        None => Ok(false),
        Some(text) => {
            writer
                .write(&path, text.as_bytes(), OverwritePolicy::Backup)
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            Ok(true)
        }
    }
}
```

> quick-xml 0.38: `Reader::from_str`, `config_mut().check_end_names`, `read_event()`, `buffer_position()` (returns `u64`). A `&amp;` inside text may come as a separate `Event::GeneralRef` in 0.38 — the merge never reads text events (it slices the original bytes), so that does not matter. The `compact` branch keeps one-line games one-line (`replace_mode…` test); insertions use the file's own line ending (`newline_of`) and the indentation of the game's first child. If quick-xml skips a leading BOM without counting it in `buffer_position`, add its 3 bytes back to the offsets (the BOM test pins this).

```rust
//! A Batocera fill merges gamelist.xml; Undo restores it byte for byte.

mod common;

use std::fs;
use std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool;

use coverly_core::art::resolve_art;
use coverly_core::cache::Cache;
use coverly_core::fill::{Endpoints, FillEnv, FillSettings};
use coverly_core::jobs::ApplyOptions;
use coverly_core::net::testing::{png_bytes, MockServer};
use coverly_core::net::{NetConfig, PoliteHttp, UreqHttp};
use coverly_core::profiles::Media;
use coverly_core::progress::NoProgress;
use coverly_core::scan::{scan_library, ScanOptions};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_core::write::{undo_last, OverwritePolicy};

const SNES: &str = "Nintendo - Super Nintendo Entertainment System";
const ORIGINAL: &str = "<?xml version=\"1.0\"?>\n<gameList>\n\t<game id=\"9\">\n\t\t<path>./Other.sfc</path>\n\t\t<desc>keep me</desc>\n\t</game>\n</gameList>\n";

fn run(gamelist: Option<&[u8]>, gamelist_is_dir: bool) -> (tempfile::TempDir, coverly_core::jobs::JobReport, tempfile::TempDir) {
    let lib = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let r = lib.path();
    common::write(r, "roms/snes/Zelda.sfc", "zelda");
    common::write(r, "roms/snes/Other.sfc", "other");
    if gamelist_is_dir {
        common::mkdir(r, "roms/snes/gamelist.xml");
    } else if let Some(bytes) = gamelist {
        fs::write(r.join("roms/snes/gamelist.xml"), bytes).unwrap();
    }
    let server = MockServer::start();
    server.serve_libretro_root(&[SNES]);
    server.serve_libretro_index(SNES, "Named_Boxarts", &["Zelda (USA).png"]);
    server.serve_libretro_image(SNES, "Named_Boxarts", "Zelda (USA).png", png_bytes(400, 560));
    let cfg = NetConfig { retries: 0, ..NetConfig::default() };
    let http = PoliteHttp::new(UreqHttp::new(&cfg), &cfg);
    let cache = Cache::in_memory().unwrap();
    let endpoints = Endpoints { libretro: server.base(), libretro_raw: server.url("/raw"), database: server.base() };
    let cache_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let env = FillEnv { cache: &cache, http: &http, endpoints: &endpoints, cache_dir: cache_dir.path(), data_dir: data.path() };
    let profile = common::profile("batocera");
    let db = SystemDb::builtin();
    let scan = scan_library(r, &profile, &db, &ScanOptions::default());
    let art = resolve_art(&scan, &profile, &db, &[Media::Cover]);
    let settings = FillSettings { media: vec![Media::Cover], ..FillSettings::default() };
    let plan = env.plan(r, &profile, &db, &art, &settings, &NoProgress, &AtomicBool::new(false));
    let report = env
        .apply(&plan, &profile, &ApplyOptions { overwrite: OverwritePolicy::Never, ..ApplyOptions::default() }, &NoProgress, &AtomicBool::new(false))
        .unwrap();
    (lib, report, data)
}

#[test]
fn a_batocera_fill_merges_the_gamelist_and_undo_restores_it() {
    let (lib, report, data) = run(Some(ORIGINAL.as_bytes()), false);
    assert_eq!(report.filled, 1, "{report:?}");
    assert!(report.warnings.is_empty(), "{:?}", report.warnings);
    let path = lib.path().join("roms/snes/gamelist.xml");
    let merged = fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
    assert!(merged.contains("<desc>keep me</desc>") && merged.contains("<game id=\"9\">"));
    assert!(merged.contains("<path>./Zelda.sfc</path>"));
    assert!(merged.contains("<thumbnail>./images/Zelda-image.png</thumbnail>"));
    assert!(lib.path().join("roms/snes/images/Zelda-image.png").exists());
    let undo = undo_last(data.path()).unwrap();
    assert!(undo.skipped_modified.is_empty(), "{undo:?}");
    assert_eq!(fs::read(&path).unwrap(), ORIGINAL.as_bytes(), "byte for byte");
    assert!(!lib.path().join("roms/snes/images/Zelda-image.png").exists());
}

#[test]
fn a_missing_gamelist_is_created_and_undo_removes_it() {
    let (lib, report, data) = run(None, false);
    assert_eq!(report.filled, 1);
    let path = lib.path().join("roms/snes/gamelist.xml");
    assert!(fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap().contains("<path>./Zelda.sfc</path>"));
    undo_last(data.path()).unwrap();
    assert!(!path.exists());
}

#[test]
fn a_broken_or_odd_gamelist_is_left_alone_with_a_warning() {
    for (bytes, is_dir) in [
        (Some(b"<!DOCTYPE x><gameList/>".as_slice()), false),
        (Some(b"\xff\xfe<gameList/>".as_slice()), false),
        (Some(b"<gameList><game>".as_slice()), false),
        (None, true),
    ] {
        let (lib, report, _data) = run(bytes, is_dir);
        assert_eq!(report.filled, 1, "the art is written anyway");
        assert_eq!(report.warnings.len(), 1, "{:?}", report.warnings);
        let path = lib.path().join("roms/snes/gamelist.xml");
        if let Some(b) = bytes {
            assert_eq!(fs::read(&path).unwrap(), b);
        } else {
            assert!(path.is_dir());
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_profile_without_the_gamelist_flag_never_touches_one() {
    let (lib, report, _data) = {
        // Same library, laid out as Batocera, but with the flag off.
        let lib = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let r = lib.path();
        common::write(r, "roms/snes/Zelda.sfc", "zelda");
        fs::write(r.join("roms/snes/gamelist.xml"), ORIGINAL).unwrap();
        let server = MockServer::start();
        server.serve_libretro_root(&[SNES]);
        server.serve_libretro_index(SNES, "Named_Boxarts", &["Zelda (USA).png"]);
        server.serve_libretro_image(SNES, "Named_Boxarts", "Zelda (USA).png", png_bytes(400, 560));
        let cfg = NetConfig { retries: 0, ..NetConfig::default() };
        let http = PoliteHttp::new(UreqHttp::new(&cfg), &cfg);
        let cache = Cache::in_memory().unwrap();
        let endpoints = Endpoints { libretro: server.base(), libretro_raw: server.url("/raw"), database: server.base() };
        let cache_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let data = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let env = FillEnv { cache: &cache, http: &http, endpoints: &endpoints, cache_dir: cache_dir.path(), data_dir: data.path() };
        let mut profile = common::profile("batocera");
        profile.gamelist = false;
        let db = SystemDb::builtin();
        let scan = scan_library(r, &profile, &db, &ScanOptions::default());
        let art = resolve_art(&scan, &profile, &db, &[Media::Cover]);
        let settings = FillSettings { media: vec![Media::Cover], ..FillSettings::default() };
        let plan = env.plan(r, &profile, &db, &art, &settings, &NoProgress, &AtomicBool::new(false));
        let report = env.apply(&plan, &profile, &ApplyOptions::default(), &NoProgress, &AtomicBool::new(false)).unwrap();
        (lib, report, data)
    };
    assert_eq!(report.filled, 1);
    assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(lib.path().join("roms/snes/gamelist.xml")).unwrap(), ORIGINAL);
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn undo_restores_a_gamelist_written_twice_in_one_job() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let root = tmp.path().join("lib");
    let data = tmp.path().join("data");
    fs::create_dir_all(root.join("roms/snes")).unwrap();
    let gl = root.join("roms/snes/gamelist.xml");
    fs::write(&gl, b"ORIGINAL").unwrap();
    let mut w = Writer::begin(&root, &data).unwrap();
    w.write(&gl, b"FIRST", OverwritePolicy::Backup).unwrap();
    let job = w.job_id().to_string();
    w.finish().unwrap();
    let mut w = Writer::resume(&root, &data, &job).unwrap();
    w.write(&gl, b"SECOND", OverwritePolicy::Backup).unwrap();
    w.finish().unwrap();
    let report = undo_job(&data, &job).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(fs::read(&gl).unwrap(), b"ORIGINAL");
    assert!(report.skipped_modified.is_empty(), "{report:?}");
}

#[test]
fn undo_removes_a_file_created_then_replaced_in_one_job() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let root = tmp.path().join("lib");
    let data = tmp.path().join("data");
    fs::create_dir_all(root.join("roms/snes")).unwrap();
    let gl = root.join("roms/snes/gamelist.xml");
    let mut w = Writer::begin(&root, &data).unwrap();
    w.write(&gl, b"FIRST", OverwritePolicy::Backup).unwrap();
    let job = w.job_id().to_string();
    w.finish().unwrap();
    let mut w = Writer::resume(&root, &data, &job).unwrap();
    w.write(&gl, b"SECOND", OverwritePolicy::Backup).unwrap();
    w.finish().unwrap();
    let report = undo_job(&data, &job).unwrap();
    assert!(!gl.exists());
    assert!(report.skipped_modified.is_empty(), "{report:?}");
}
```

(Add any missing `use` lines — `std::fs`, `coverly_core::write::{undo_job, OverwritePolicy, Writer}` — at the top of `journal_resume.rs`.)

```rust
//! Manual identities (M4-05, D32): "this ROM is <name>", chosen by the
//! user in "Search by title". Kept in the data dir (not the cache: Clear
//! cache keeps them), keyed by the ROM's absolute path.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fs;
use std::io;
use std::path::Path;

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use unicode_normalization::UnicodeNormalization;

pub const OVERRIDES_FILE: &str = "identity-overrides.json";
const MAX_OVERRIDES_BYTES: u64 = 8 * 1024 * 1024;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ManualIdentity {
    /// The name the sources know the game by (e.g. a libretro file name).
    pub name: String,
    /// The source it came from.
    pub source: String,
    /// Unix seconds.
    pub set_at: i64,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Overrides {
    #[serde(default)]
    entries: BTreeMap<String, ManualIdentity>,
}

fn key(rom: &Path) -> String {
    let abs = std::path::absolute(rom).unwrap_or_else(|_| rom.to_path_buf());
    abs.to_string_lossy().nfc().collect()
}

fn valid_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let n = name.trim();
    !n.is_empty() && n.chars().count() <= 255 && !n.chars().any(char::is_control)
}

impl Overrides {
    fn parse(path: &Path) -> Result<Overrides, ()> {
        let meta = match fs::metadata(path) {
            Ok(m) => m,
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(Overrides::default()),
            Err(_) => return Err(()),
        };
        if !meta.is_file() || meta.len() > MAX_OVERRIDES_BYTES {
            return Err(());
        }
        let text = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|_| ())?;
        let mut o: Overrides = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|_| ())?;
        o.entries.retain(|_, v| valid_name(&v.name));
        Ok(o)
    }

    /// The overrides at `path`; empty when missing or unreadable.
    pub fn read(path: &Path) -> Overrides {
        Overrides::parse(path).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    /// Like [`Overrides::read`], but a file that cannot be read as
    /// overrides is renamed aside first, so saving never loses it.
    pub fn load_for_update(path: &Path) -> Overrides {
        match Overrides::parse(path) {
            Ok(o) => o,
            Err(()) => {
                let aside = path.with_file_name(format!("{OVERRIDES_FILE}.bad-{}", crate::time::now_unix()));
                let _ = fs::rename(path, aside);
                Overrides::default()
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
        if let Some(dir) = path.parent() {
            fs::create_dir_all(dir)?;
        }
        let tmp = path.with_extension("json.tmp");
        fs::write(&tmp, serde_json::to_string_pretty(self).map_err(io::Error::other)?)?;
        fs::rename(&tmp, path)
    }

    pub fn get(&self, rom: &Path) -> Option<&ManualIdentity> {
        self.entries.get(&key(rom))
    }

    pub fn set(&mut self, rom: &Path, mut id: ManualIdentity) -> bool {
        if !valid_name(&id.name) {
            return false;
        }
        id.name = id.name.trim().to_string();
        self.entries.insert(key(rom), id);
        true
    }

    pub fn remove(&mut self, rom: &Path) -> bool {
        self.entries.remove(&key(rom)).is_some()
    }

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.entries.len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.entries.is_empty()
    }
}

/// Records "`rom` is `name`" in `data_dir`.
pub fn remember_manual(data_dir: &Path, rom: &Path, name: &str, source: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
    let path = data_dir.join(OVERRIDES_FILE);
    let mut o = Overrides::load_for_update(&path);
    let ok = o.set(rom, ManualIdentity { name: name.into(), source: source.into(), set_at: crate::time::now_unix() });
    if !ok {
        return Err(io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput, "not a usable name"));
    }
    o.save(&path)
}

/// Forgets the manual identity of `rom`; `false` when it had none.
pub fn forget_manual(data_dir: &Path, rom: &Path) -> io::Result<bool> {
    let path = data_dir.join(OVERRIDES_FILE);
    let mut o = Overrides::load_for_update(&path);
    let removed = o.remove(rom);
    if removed {
        o.save(&path)?;
    }
    Ok(removed)
}
```

```rust
impl Plan {
    /// A plan of one item (the drawer's manual pick).
    pub fn single(root: &Path, profile: &Profile, item: PlanItem) -> Plan {
        let mut plan = Plan {
            root: root.to_path_buf(),
            profile_id: profile.id.clone(),
            created_at: crate::time::rfc3339_utc(std::time::SystemTime::now()),
            strategy: StrategyKind::Priority,
            items: vec![item],
            totals: Counts::default(),
            per_system: BTreeMap::new(),
            warnings: Vec::new(),
            paused: None,
            pause_kind: None,
        };
        plan.recount();
        plan
    }
}
```

```rust
/// Most candidates a title search returns.
pub const SEARCH_LIMIT: usize = 12;
/// Lowest fuzzy score a title search keeps (lower than a fill's: the user
/// reads the list).
const SEARCH_MIN_SCORE: u8 = 50;

/// One ROM, for writes outside a plan.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct RomRef<'a> {
    pub system_id: &'a str,
    pub folder: &'a str,
    pub system_dir: &'a Path,
    /// Relative to `system_dir`.
    pub rel_path: &'a Path,
    pub stem: &'a str,
}

/// A typed title, trimmed; refused when empty, over 120 characters or
/// holding control characters.
pub fn clean_query(q: &str) -> Result<String, ArtError> {
    let t = q.trim();
    if t.is_empty() || t.chars().count() > 120 || t.chars().any(char::is_control) {
        return Err(ArtError::BadQuery);
    }
    Ok(t.to_string())
}
```

```rust
        let identifier = Identifier::new(self.cache, &dats, IdentifyOptions { … }).with_overrides(&overrides);
```

New methods:

```rust
    /// "Search by title" (M4-05): candidates of every enabled source for a
    /// typed title, best first, at most [`SEARCH_LIMIT`].
    pub fn search_title(
        &self,
        system: &System,
        media: Media,
        query: &str,
        settings: &FillSettings,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Candidate>, ArtError> {
        let q = clean_query(query)?;
        let libretro = self.libretro(false, settings.index_ttl_secs);
        if !settings.sources.iter().any(|s| s == libretro.id()) {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }
        let parsed = crate::identify::names::parse_name(&q, false);
        let query = ArtQuery {
            system,
            media,
            canonical: None,
            rom: &parsed,
            stem: &q,
            by_name: true,
            fuzzy: true,
            min_score: SEARCH_MIN_SCORE,
            max_candidates: SEARCH_LIMIT,
        };
        let mut found = libretro.candidates(&query)?;
        crate::sources::sort_candidates(&mut found);
        let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        found.retain(|c| seen.insert((c.source.clone(), c.name.clone())));
        found.truncate(SEARCH_LIMIT);
        Ok(found)
    }

    /// Writes candidate `c` as the `media` art of `rom` at `target` (the
    /// ROM's own target: never renamed), replacing existing art with a
    /// backup, in a journaled job of its own (none when nothing changed).
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn apply_candidate(
        &self,
        root: &Path,
        profile: &Profile,
        rom: &RomRef,
        media: Media,
        target: &Path,
        c: &Candidate,
        keep_jobs: usize,
    ) -> Result<JobReport, WriteError> {
        let item = PlanItem {
            system_id: rom.system_id.to_string(),
            folder: rom.folder.to_string(),
            rel_path: rom.rel_path.to_path_buf(),
            stem: rom.stem.to_string(),
            media,
            target: target.to_path_buf(),
            identity_method: IdMethod::Manual,
            canonical: Some(c.name.clone()),
            replaces_existing: target.exists(),
            note: None,
            system_dir: rom.system_dir.to_path_buf(),
            action: PlanAction::Auto { chosen: c.clone(), confidence: 1.0, alternatives: Vec::new() },
        };
        let plan = Plan::single(root, profile, item);
        let opts = ApplyOptions {
            overwrite: OverwritePolicy::Backup,
            include_review: false,
            concurrency: 1,
            discard_empty: true,
            keep_jobs,
            ..ApplyOptions::default()
        };
        self.apply(&plan, profile, &opts, &NoProgress, &AtomicBool::new(false))
    }
```

`write_local_image` takes `rom: &RomRef` after `profile`; after a successful `writer.write(…)`:

```rust
    if profile.gamelist && outcome.as_ref().is_ok_and(|o| !matches!(o, WriteOutcome::SkippedExists { .. })) {
        if let Ok(art_rel) = target.strip_prefix(rom.system_dir) {
            let game = GameArt {

                name: rom.stem.to_string(),
                art: vec![(media, rel_slash(art_rel))],
```
