```rust
//! The CLI reads the app's settings file; flags override it.

use std::fs;

use assert_cmd::Command;
use coverly_core::net::testing::{png_bytes, MockServer};
use predicates::prelude::*;
use predicates::str::contains;
use tempfile::TempDir;

const GBA: &str = "Nintendo - Game Boy Advance";

struct Fx {
    lib: TempDir,
    cfg: TempDir,
    cache: TempDir,
    data: TempDir,
    server: MockServer,
}

fn fx() -> Fx {
    let lib = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    fs::create_dir_all(lib.path().join("anbernic")).unwrap();
    fs::create_dir_all(lib.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Junk")).unwrap();
    fs::write(lib.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Golden Sun.gba"), b"gs").unwrap();
    fs::write(lib.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Junk/Other.gba"), b"o").unwrap();
    let server = MockServer::start();
    server.serve_libretro_root(&[GBA]);
    server.serve_libretro_index(GBA, "Named_Boxarts", &["Golden Sun (USA, Europe).png"]);
    server.serve_libretro_image(GBA, "Named_Boxarts", "Golden Sun (USA, Europe).png", png_bytes(1000, 1400));
    Fx { lib, cfg: TempDir::new().unwrap(), cache: TempDir::new().unwrap(), data: TempDir::new().unwrap(), server }
}

fn settings(f: &Fx, json: &str) {
    fs::write(f.cfg.path().join("settings.json"), json).unwrap();
}

fn coverly(f: &Fx) -> Command {
    let mut c = Command::cargo_bin("coverly").unwrap();
    c.env("COVERLY_SETTINGS", f.cfg.path().join("settings.json"));
    c
}

fn fill(f: &Fx) -> Command {
    let mut c = coverly(f);
    c.arg("fill").arg(f.lib.path());
    c.arg("--cache-dir").arg(f.cache.path()).arg("--data-dir").arg(f.data.path());
    c.arg("--libretro-url").arg(f.server.base());
    c.arg("--libretro-raw-url").arg(f.server.url("/raw"));
    c.arg("--database-url").arg(f.server.base());
    c
}

fn cover(f: &Fx) -> std::path::PathBuf {
    f.lib.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Imgs/Golden Sun.png")
}

#[test]
fn dry_run_default_off_applies_and_the_flag_still_wins() {
    let f = fx();
    settings(&f, r#"{"safety":{"dry_run_default":false}}"#);
    fill(&f).arg("--dry-run").assert().success().stdout(contains("Dry run"));
    assert!(!cover(&f).exists());
    fill(&f).assert().success().stdout(contains("filled 1"));
    assert!(cover(&f).exists());
}

    settings(&f, r#"{"matching":{"strategy":"priority"}}"#);

    fill(&f)
        .args(["--report", "json", "--strategy", "rom-region"])
        .assert()
        .success()
        .stdout(contains("\"strategy\": \"rom-region\""));
}

    let f = fx();
    coverly(&f).arg("audit").arg(f.lib.path()).args(["--format", "json"]).assert().success().stdout(contains("Other"));
    settings(&f, r#"{"library":{"ignored_dirs":["Junk"]}}"#);

    fs::create_dir_all(plain.path().join("Games/GBA")).unwrap();
    fs::write(plain.path().join("Games/GBA/Golden Sun.gba"), b"gs").unwrap();

    coverly(&f).arg("profiles").assert().success().stdout(contains("my-sd")).stdout(contains("custom"));
}

    c.env("COVERLY_SETTINGS", "").arg("audit").arg(f.lib.path()).args(["--format", "json"]);
```

```rust
//! The app's settings, read-only (spec §9: the CLI uses the same file;
//! flags override it).

use std::path::PathBuf;

use coverly_core::paths::config_dir;
use coverly_core::profiles::builtin_profiles;
use coverly_core::profiles::custom::{ProfileFile, ProfileStore, PROFILES_DIR};
use coverly_core::settings::{read_settings, Settings, SETTINGS_FILE};

/// Settings file override: unset = the app's, empty = defaults, else a path.
pub const SETTINGS_ENV: &str = "COVERLY_SETTINGS";

pub struct CliSettings {
    pub settings: Settings,
    pub custom: Vec<ProfileFile>,
}

fn ids(custom: &[ProfileFile]) -> Vec<String> {
    builtin_profiles()
        .into_iter()
        .map(|p| p.id)
        .chain(custom.iter().map(|c| c.id.clone()))
        .collect()
}

pub fn load_cli_settings() -> CliSettings {
    let path: Option<PathBuf> = match std::env::var_os(SETTINGS_ENV) {
        Some(v) if v.is_empty() => None,
        Some(v) => Some(PathBuf::from(v)),
        None => config_dir().map(|d| d.join(SETTINGS_FILE)),
    };
    let Some(path) = path else {
        return CliSettings { settings: Settings::default(), custom: Vec::new() };
    };
    let base = path.parent().map(|p| p.join(PROFILES_DIR)).unwrap_or_default();
    let mut custom = ProfileStore::new(base).load().0;
    let mut settings = read_settings(&path, &ids(&custom));
    if let Some(dir) = settings.advanced.custom_dir.clone() {
        custom = ProfileStore::new(dir).load().0;
        settings = read_settings(&path, &ids(&custom));
    }
    CliSettings { settings, custom }
}
```

- `id` = the flag, else `cli.settings.library.default_profile`, `"auto"`/`None` = detect;
- a named id is looked up with `find_profile(&cli.custom, id)`; unknown → usage error listing built-in and custom ids;
- detection unchanged (built-ins only).
`profile_ids()` lists custom ids too.

```rust
//! Credentials (spec §11, D30): a closed set of keys, values never shown.

use coverly_core::credentials::*;

#[test]
fn keys_are_a_closed_set() {
    assert_eq!(CredentialKey::parse("steamgriddb-api-key"), Some(CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey));
    assert_eq!(CredentialKey::parse("screenscraper-user"), Some(CredentialKey::ScreenscraperUser));
    assert_eq!(CredentialKey::parse("screenscraper-password"), Some(CredentialKey::ScreenscraperPassword));
    for bad in ["", "github", "../x", "STEAMGRIDDB-API-KEY"] {
        assert_eq!(CredentialKey::parse(bad), None, "{bad}");
    }
    let accounts: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = CredentialKey::ALL.iter().map(|k| k.account()).collect();
    assert_eq!(accounts.len(), 3);
}

#[test]
fn memory_store_sets_clears_and_reports_presence_only() {
    let store = MemoryStore::default();
    store_secret(&store, CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey, "  sk-123  ").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(store.get(CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey).unwrap().as_deref(), Some("sk-123"));
    let st = status(&store);
    assert!(st.available);
    assert!(st.set[&CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey]);
    assert!(!st.set[&CredentialKey::ScreenscraperUser]);
    let json = serde_json::to_string(&st).unwrap();
    assert!(!json.contains("sk-123"), "{json}");
    assert!(json.contains("\"steamgriddb-api-key\":true"), "{json}");
    assert!(!format!("{store:?}").contains("sk-123"));
    store_secret(&store, CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey, "   ").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(store.get(CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey).unwrap(), None, "empty clears");
    store.clear(CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey).unwrap();
}

#[test]
fn bad_values_are_refused() {
    let store = MemoryStore::default();
    for bad in ["a\u{0}b".to_string(), "x".repeat(MAX_SECRET_CHARS + 1)] {
        assert_eq!(store_secret(&store, CredentialKey::ScreenscraperPassword, &bad), Err(CredentialError::BadValue));
    }
}

#[test]
fn an_unavailable_store_says_so() {
    let store = MemoryStore::unavailable();
    let st = status(&store);
    assert!(!st.available);
    assert!(st.set.values().all(|v| !v));
    assert_eq!(store_secret(&store, CredentialKey::ScreenscraperUser, "me"), Err(CredentialError::Unavailable));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the store trait, the memory store and the keychain store**

`crates/coverly-core/src/credentials.rs`:

```rust
//! Source credentials (spec §11, D30). Values live in a `CredentialStore`
//! (the OS keychain in the app, memory in tests); the UI only ever learns
//! whether a key is set.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::sync::Mutex;

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum CredentialKey {
    SteamgriddbApiKey,
    ScreenscraperUser,
    ScreenscraperPassword,
}

impl CredentialKey {
    pub const ALL: [CredentialKey; 3] = [
        CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey,
        CredentialKey::ScreenscraperUser,
        CredentialKey::ScreenscraperPassword,
    ];

    /// Account name in the store (also the wire name).
    pub fn account(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey => "steamgriddb-api-key",
            CredentialKey::ScreenscraperUser => "screenscraper-user",
            CredentialKey::ScreenscraperPassword => "screenscraper-password",
        }
    }

    /// Only the exact wire names; anything else is refused.
    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<CredentialKey> {
        CredentialKey::ALL.into_iter().find(|k| k.account() == name)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum CredentialError {
    #[error("there is no credential store on this system")]
    Unavailable,
    #[error("the credential store is locked")]
    Locked,
    #[error("credential store error: {0}")]
    Failed(String),
    #[error("the value is too long or holds control characters")]
    BadValue,
}

pub trait CredentialStore: Send + Sync {
    fn available(&self) -> bool;
    fn get(&self, key: CredentialKey) -> Result<Option<String>, CredentialError>;
    fn set(&self, key: CredentialKey, value: &str) -> Result<(), CredentialError>;
    /// Already absent is success.
    fn clear(&self, key: CredentialKey) -> Result<(), CredentialError>;
}

pub const MAX_SECRET_CHARS: usize = 512;

/// Stores `value` trimmed; an empty value clears the key.
pub fn store_secret(store: &dyn CredentialStore, key: CredentialKey, value: &str) -> Result<(), CredentialError> {
    if !store.available() {
        return Err(CredentialError::Unavailable);
    }
    let v = value.trim();
    if v.is_empty() {
        return store.clear(key);
    }
    if v.chars().count() > MAX_SECRET_CHARS || v.chars().any(char::is_control) {
        return Err(CredentialError::BadValue);
    }
    store.set(key, v)
}

/// Whether the store works and which keys hold a value (never the values).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct CredentialStatus {
    pub available: bool,
    pub set: BTreeMap<CredentialKey, bool>,
}

pub fn status(store: &dyn CredentialStore) -> CredentialStatus {
    let available = store.available();
    CredentialStatus {
        available,
        set: CredentialKey::ALL
            .into_iter()
            .map(|k| (k, available && matches!(store.get(k), Ok(Some(_)))))
            .collect(),
    }
}

/// Tests and the browser-less fallback: values in memory.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct MemoryStore {
    values: Mutex<BTreeMap<CredentialKey, String>>,
    unavailable: bool,
}

impl MemoryStore {
    pub fn unavailable() -> MemoryStore {
        MemoryStore { unavailable: true, ..MemoryStore::default() }
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for MemoryStore {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        let keys: Vec<CredentialKey> = self.values.lock().map(|v| v.keys().copied().collect()).unwrap_or_default();
        f.debug_struct("MemoryStore").field("keys", &keys).finish()
    }
}

impl CredentialStore for MemoryStore {
    fn available(&self) -> bool {
        !self.unavailable
    }
    fn get(&self, key: CredentialKey) -> Result<Option<String>, CredentialError> {
        if self.unavailable {
            return Err(CredentialError::Unavailable);
        }
        Ok(self.values.lock().map_err(|_| CredentialError::Failed("poisoned".into()))?.get(&key).cloned())
    }
    fn set(&self, key: CredentialKey, value: &str) -> Result<(), CredentialError> {
        if self.unavailable {
            return Err(CredentialError::Unavailable);
        }
        self.values.lock().map_err(|_| CredentialError::Failed("poisoned".into()))?.insert(key, value.to_string());
        Ok(())
    }
    fn clear(&self, key: CredentialKey) -> Result<(), CredentialError> {
        if self.unavailable {
            return Err(CredentialError::Unavailable);
        }
        self.values.lock().map_err(|_| CredentialError::Failed("poisoned".into()))?.remove(&key);
        Ok(())
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/keychain.rs` (pattern from Versorium `src-tauri/src/secrets/mod.rs`; every call blocks, so callers run on the blocking pool):

```rust
//! The OS credential store (macOS Keychain, Windows Credential Manager,
//! Secret Service) behind `CredentialStore`. Values never leave Rust.

use coverly_core::credentials::{CredentialError, CredentialKey, CredentialStore};

/// The bundle identifier: entries are recognisable in Keychain Access.
pub const SERVICE: &str = "com.maecly.coverlyroms";

pub struct KeychainStore {
    service: String,
}

impl KeychainStore {
    pub fn new() -> KeychainStore {
        KeychainStore::with_service(SERVICE)
    }

    pub fn with_service(service: &str) -> KeychainStore {
        KeychainStore { service: service.to_string() }
    }

    fn entry(&self, key: CredentialKey) -> Result<keyring::Entry, CredentialError> {
        keyring::Entry::new(&self.service, key.account()).map_err(|e| classify(&e))
    }
}

impl Default for KeychainStore {
    fn default() -> Self {
        KeychainStore::new()
    }
}

pub(crate) fn classify(e: &keyring::Error) -> CredentialError {
    match e {
        keyring::Error::NoStorageAccess(_) => CredentialError::Locked,
        keyring::Error::NoDefaultStore => CredentialError::Unavailable,
        other => CredentialError::Failed(other.to_string()),
    }
}

impl CredentialStore for KeychainStore {
    fn available(&self) -> bool {
        keyring::Entry::store_status().is_ok()
    }
    fn get(&self, key: CredentialKey) -> Result<Option<String>, CredentialError> {
        match self.entry(key)?.get_password() {
            Ok(v) => Ok(Some(v)),
            Err(keyring::Error::NoEntry) => Ok(None),
            Err(e) => Err(classify(&e)),
        }
    }
    fn set(&self, key: CredentialKey, value: &str) -> Result<(), CredentialError> {
        self.entry(key)?.set_password(value).map_err(|e| classify(&e))
    }
    fn clear(&self, key: CredentialKey) -> Result<(), CredentialError> {
        match self.entry(key)?.delete_credential() {
            Ok(()) | Err(keyring::Error::NoEntry) => Ok(()),
            Err(e) => Err(classify(&e)),
        }
    }
}
```

> Use exactly Versorium's keyring line (`keyring = "4"`, same features if any) and check `cargo tree -p coverly-roms -i keyring` resolves the platform stores. If `Entry::store_status` does not exist in the resolved version, implement `available()` as "`Entry::new` succeeds" (as Versorium's comment explains, `Entry::new` flattens init errors) and note the deviation.

```rust
//! Settings in the app: load once, update with field errors, reset,
//! snapshots per job, applied when opening a library.

mod common;

use std::fs;
use std::time::Duration;

use coverly_core::profiles::Media;
use coverly_core::settings::{FieldErrorCode, LoadNotice, ProxyChoice, ThemeChoice, SETTINGS_FILE};
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_roms_lib::error::IpcError;
use coverly_roms_lib::jobs::FillConfig;
use coverly_roms_lib::state::AppState;
use serde_json::json;
use tempfile::TempDir;

fn state(dir: &TempDir) -> AppState {
    AppState::new(
        dir.path().join("config"),
        FillConfig::new(dir.path().join("cache"), dir.path().join("data")),
    )
}

#[test]
fn starts_with_defaults_and_no_notice() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let view = st.settings_view();
    assert_eq!(view.settings, coverly_core::settings::Settings::default());
    assert_eq!(view.notice, None);
    assert!(!dir.path().join("config").join(SETTINGS_FILE).exists(), "nothing written until a change");
}

#[test]
fn a_corrupt_file_at_startup_reports_the_reset() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    fs::create_dir_all(dir.path().join("config")).unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.path().join("config").join(SETTINGS_FILE), "\u{0}\u{1}garbage").unwrap();
    let view = state(&dir).settings_view();
    assert_eq!(view.notice, Some(LoadNotice::ResetCorrupt));
    assert!(view.backup.unwrap().contains("settings.json.bad-"));
}

#[test]
fn update_saves_valid_changes_and_returns_field_errors_without_saving() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let ok = st.update_settings(&json!({"general": {"theme": "dark"}, "network": {"timeout_secs": 60}})).unwrap();
    assert!(ok.errors.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(ok.settings.general.theme, ThemeChoice::Dark);
    let saved = fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join("config").join(SETTINGS_FILE)).unwrap();
    assert!(saved.contains("\"theme\": \"dark\""));
    let bad = st.update_settings(&json!({"network": {"timeout_secs": 1, "proxy": "manual"}})).unwrap();
    let fields: Vec<(&str, FieldErrorCode)> = bad.errors.iter().map(|e| (e.field.as_str(), e.code)).collect();
    assert!(fields.contains(&("network.timeout_secs", FieldErrorCode::Range)));
    assert!(fields.contains(&("network.proxy_url", FieldErrorCode::Required)));
    assert_eq!(bad.settings.network.timeout_secs, 60, "the current settings come back");
    assert_eq!(st.settings().network.proxy, ProxyChoice::System, "nothing was saved");
}

#[test]
fn scan_changes_ask_for_a_reopen() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    assert!(!st.update_settings(&json!({"general": {"theme": "light"}})).unwrap().reopen);
    assert!(st.update_settings(&json!({"library": {"include_hidden": true}})).unwrap().reopen);
}

#[test]
fn reset_keeps_recents_and_backs_up_the_file() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let lib = common::small_library();
    st.open(lib.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {}).unwrap();
    st.update_settings(&json!({"art": {"jpeg_quality": 70}})).unwrap();
    let view = st.reset_settings().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(view.settings.art.jpeg_quality, 90);
    assert_eq!(view.settings.library.recent.len(), 1, "recents survive a reset");
    let names: Vec<String> = fs::read_dir(dir.path().join("config"))
        .unwrap()
        .map(|e| e.unwrap().file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned())
        .collect();
    assert!(names.iter().any(|n| n.starts_with("settings.json.reset-")), "{names:?}");
}

#[test]
fn the_fill_config_is_a_snapshot_of_the_settings() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let backups = dir.path().join("elsewhere");
    st.update_settings(&json!({
        "network": {"timeout_secs": 12, "concurrency": 2},
        "safety": {"backup_dir": backups.to_string_lossy()}
    }))
    .unwrap();
    let before = st.fill_config();
    assert_eq!(before.net.timeout, Duration::from_secs(12));
    assert_eq!(before.net.concurrency, 2);
    assert_eq!(before.data_dir, backups);
    assert_eq!(before.settings.network.timeout_secs, 12);
    st.update_settings(&json!({"network": {"timeout_secs": 99}})).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(before.net.timeout, Duration::from_secs(12), "a running job keeps its snapshot");
    assert_eq!(st.fill_config().net.timeout, Duration::from_secs(99));
}

#[test]
fn opening_a_library_applies_the_settings() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let lib = common::small_library();
    fs::create_dir_all(lib.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Junk")).unwrap();
    fs::write(lib.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Junk/Hidden Gem.gba"), b"x").unwrap();
    st.update_settings(&json!({
        "library": {"ignored_dirs": ["Junk"]},
        "art": {"single_slot_media": "screenshot"}
    }))
    .unwrap();
    let view = st.open(lib.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {}).unwrap();
    assert!(view.entries.iter().all(|e| !e.rel_path.contains("Junk")));
    assert_eq!(view.profile.media, [Media::Screenshot]);
}

#[test]
fn a_backup_folder_inside_the_library_is_refused() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let lib = common::small_library();
    let view = st.open(lib.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {}).unwrap();
    let inside = std::path::absolute(lib.path()).unwrap().join("backups");
    st.update_settings(&json!({"safety": {"backup_dir": inside.to_string_lossy()}})).unwrap();
    let err = st
        .remove_entry_art(view.generation, 0, Media::Cover)
        .unwrap_err();
    assert!(matches!(err, IpcError::BadRequest(ref m) if m.contains("backup folder")), "{err:?}");
}

#[test]
fn clear_cache_reports_freed_bytes_and_is_refused_while_a_job_runs() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let art = dir.path().join("cache/art/ab");
    fs::create_dir_all(&art).unwrap();
    fs::write(art.join("ab".repeat(20)), vec![1u8; 5000]).unwrap();
    assert!(st.cache_usage() >= 5000);
    {
        let _busy = st.jobs().begin_manual().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st.clear_cache().unwrap_err(), IpcError::JobRunning);
    }
    assert!(st.clear_cache().unwrap() >= 5000);
    assert_eq!(st.cache_usage(), 0);
}
```

```rust
//! Settings on the wire, and the fixed links the About screen may open.

use coverly_core::settings::{FieldError, LoadNotice, LoadReport, Settings};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct SettingsView {
    pub settings: Settings,
    /// What loading had to do (shown once as a notice).
    pub notice: Option<LoadNotice>,
    /// File holding what was on disk, when loading set it aside or copied it.
    pub backup: Option<String>,
    /// Fields that fell back to their defaults.
    pub fixed: Vec<String>,
}

impl SettingsView {
    pub fn new(settings: Settings, report: &LoadReport) -> SettingsView {
        SettingsView {
            settings,
            notice: report.notice,
            backup: report.backup.as_ref().map(|p| p.display().to_string()),
            fixed: report.fixed.clone(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct SettingsUpdateView {
    /// The saved settings (unchanged when `errors` is not empty).
    pub settings: Settings,
    pub errors: Vec<FieldError>,
    /// The open library must be reopened to show the change.
    pub reopen: bool,
}

/// Links the webview may ask to open (never an arbitrary URL).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum LinkId {
    Repo,
    License,
    Notices,
    Trademarks,
    Releases,
    Issues,
}

pub fn link_url(id: LinkId) -> &'static str {
    match id {
        LinkId::Repo => "https://github.com/MAECLY/coverly-roms",
        LinkId::License => "https://github.com/MAECLY/coverly-roms/blob/main/LICENSE",
        LinkId::Notices => "https://github.com/MAECLY/coverly-roms/blob/main/THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md",
        LinkId::Trademarks => "https://github.com/MAECLY/coverly-roms/blob/main/TRADEMARKS.md",
        LinkId::Releases => "https://github.com/MAECLY/coverly-roms/releases",
        LinkId::Issues => "https://github.com/MAECLY/coverly-roms/issues",
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn links_are_https_on_the_project_repo() {
        for id in [LinkId::Repo, LinkId::License, LinkId::Notices, LinkId::Trademarks, LinkId::Releases, LinkId::Issues] {
            assert!(link_url(id).starts_with("https://github.com/MAECLY/coverly-roms"), "{id:?}");
        }
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(LinkId::Trademarks).unwrap(), "trademarks");
    }
}
```

`src-tauri/src/logging.rs`:

```rust
//! A small file logger: `<data dir>/logs/coverly.log`, rotated once at
//! 1 MiB, level from the Advanced settings (applied live). Messages are
//! one line each (file names are hostile: newlines are escaped). Never
//! logs credential values (none reach this layer).

use std::fs::{self, File, OpenOptions};
use std::io::Write;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Mutex;

use coverly_core::settings::LogLevel;
use log::{LevelFilter, Log, Metadata, Record};

pub const LOG_FILE: &str = "coverly.log";
pub const MAX_LOG_BYTES: u64 = 1024 * 1024;

pub fn level_filter(l: LogLevel) -> LevelFilter {
    match l {
        LogLevel::Error => LevelFilter::Error,
        LogLevel::Warn => LevelFilter::Warn,
        LogLevel::Info => LevelFilter::Info,
        LogLevel::Debug => LevelFilter::Debug,
    }
}

pub struct FileLogger {
    path: PathBuf,
    file: Mutex<Option<File>>,
}

impl FileLogger {
    pub fn new(dir: &Path) -> FileLogger {
        FileLogger { path: dir.join(LOG_FILE), file: Mutex::new(None) }
    }

    fn write_line(&self, line: &str) {
        let Ok(mut slot) = self.file.lock() else { return };
        if fs::metadata(&self.path).is_ok_and(|m| m.len() > MAX_LOG_BYTES) {
            *slot = None;
            let _ = fs::rename(&self.path, self.path.with_extension("log.1"));
        }
        if slot.is_none() {
            if let Some(dir) = self.path.parent() {
                let _ = fs::create_dir_all(dir);
            }
            *slot = OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(&self.path).ok();
        }
        if let Some(f) = slot.as_mut() {
            let _ = f.write_all(line.as_bytes());
        }
    }
}

impl Log for FileLogger {
    fn enabled(&self, metadata: &Metadata) -> bool {
        metadata.level() <= log::max_level()
    }

    fn log(&self, record: &Record) {
        if !self.enabled(record.metadata()) {
            return;
        }
        let msg = record.args().to_string().replace('\r', "\\r").replace('\n', "\\n");
        let line = format!("{} {:<5} {}: {msg}\n", coverly_core::cache::now_unix(), record.level(), record.target());
        self.write_line(&line);
    }

    fn flush(&self) {
        if let Ok(mut slot) = self.file.lock() {
            if let Some(f) = slot.as_mut() {
                let _ = f.flush();
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Installs the logger once (later calls only set the level).
pub fn init(dir: &Path, level: LogLevel) -> bool {
    let installed = log::set_boxed_logger(Box::new(FileLogger::new(dir))).is_ok();
    set_level(level);
    installed
}

pub fn set_level(level: LogLevel) {
    log::set_max_level(level_filter(level));
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn levels_map_one_to_one() {
        assert_eq!(level_filter(LogLevel::Error), LevelFilter::Error);
        assert_eq!(level_filter(LogLevel::Debug), LevelFilter::Debug);
    }

    #[test]
    fn writes_one_line_per_record_and_rotates() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let logger = FileLogger::new(dir.path());
        log::set_max_level(LevelFilter::Info);
        logger.log(&Record::builder().args(format_args!("opened\n<evil>")).level(log::Level::Info).target("t").build());
        let text = fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join(LOG_FILE)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(text.lines().count(), 1);
        assert!(text.contains("opened\\n<evil>"));
        fs::write(dir.path().join(LOG_FILE), vec![b'x'; MAX_LOG_BYTES as usize + 1]).unwrap();
        logger.log(&Record::builder().args(format_args!("after")).level(log::Level::Warn).target("t").build());
        assert!(dir.path().join("coverly.log.1").exists());
        assert!(fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join(LOG_FILE)).unwrap().contains("after"));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The settings state**

`src-tauri/src/jobs/mod.rs`: `FillConfig` gains `pub settings: Settings` (`FillConfig::new` → `Settings::default()`). `settings_for(session, req, settings: &Settings)` starts from `settings.fill_settings()` and overrides `media` (filtered by the profile, as today), `scope` (from `req.overwrite`), `strategy` (`req.strategy`) and `regions` (`req.regions`, or the settings' order when empty). `spawn_apply` builds `ApplyOptions { overwrite: job.overwrite, include_review: false, concurrency: cfg.net.concurrency.clamp(1, 6), resume_job: …, ..cfg.settings.apply_options(&root) }` and applies with `cfg.settings.art_profile(&job.session.base_profile)`; `spawn_gather` plans with the same art profile.

`src-tauri/src/library/mod.rs`:
- `Session` gains `pub base_profile: Profile` (the fitted profile); `profile` becomes `settings.map_or(fitted.clone(), |s| s.art_profile(&fitted))`.
- `choose_profile(root, id, custom, default)`: `id` → `find_profile(custom, id)` (else `UnknownProfile`); no `id` and a `default` that exists → that profile (`detected = false`); else detection as today.
- `open_session` scans with `req.scan` and the art-adjusted profile.

`src-tauri/src/state.rs` (replace the Task 1 stopgap):

```rust
pub struct AppState {
    latest: AtomicU64,
    session: Mutex<Option<Arc<Session>>>,
    config_dir: PathBuf,
    prefs: Mutex<Prefs>,
    base: FillConfig,
    jobs: JobManager,
    picks: Mutex<Option<EntryPick>>,
    credentials: Arc<dyn CredentialStore>,
}

struct Prefs {
    settings: Settings,
    report: LoadReport,
}

impl AppState {
    pub fn new(config_dir: PathBuf, base: FillConfig) -> AppState {
        let custom = custom_dir_store(&config_dir, None).load().0;
        let (mut settings, mut report) = load_settings(&config_dir, &known_ids(&custom));
        if let Some(dir) = settings.advanced.custom_dir.clone() {
            let custom = ProfileStore::new(dir).load().0;
            let (s, r) = load_settings(&config_dir, &known_ids(&custom));
            settings = s;
            if r.notice.is_some() {
                report = r;
            }
        }
        AppState {
            latest: AtomicU64::new(0),
            session: Mutex::new(None),
            config_dir,
            prefs: Mutex::new(Prefs { settings, report }),
            base,
            jobs: JobManager::default(),
            picks: Mutex::new(None),
            credentials: Arc::new(MemoryStore::default()),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_credentials(mut self, store: Arc<dyn CredentialStore>) -> AppState {
        self.credentials = store;
        self
    }

    pub fn settings(&self) -> Settings {
        lock(&self.prefs).settings.clone()
    }

    pub fn settings_view(&self) -> SettingsView {
        let p = lock(&self.prefs);
        SettingsView::new(p.settings.clone(), &p.report)
    }

    pub fn custom_store(&self) -> ProfileStore {
        custom_dir_store(&self.config_dir, self.settings().advanced.custom_dir.as_deref())
    }

    pub fn custom_profiles(&self) -> Vec<ProfileFile> {
        self.custom_store().load().0
    }

    pub fn known_profiles(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        known_ids(&self.custom_profiles())
    }

    /// Validates and saves `patch`; field problems come back as data.
    pub fn update_settings(&self, patch: &serde_json::Value) -> Result<SettingsUpdateView, IpcError> {
        let known = self.known_profiles();
        let mut p = lock(&self.prefs);
        match p.settings.with_patch(patch, &known) {
            Err(errors) => Ok(SettingsUpdateView { settings: p.settings.clone(), errors, reopen: false }),
            Ok(next) => {
                save_settings(&self.config_dir, &next).map_err(|e| IpcError::Settings(e.to_string()))?;
                let reopen = p.settings.needs_reopen(&next);
                p.settings = next.clone();
                p.report = LoadReport::default();
                Ok(SettingsUpdateView { settings: next, errors: Vec::new(), reopen })
            }
        }
    }

    /// Defaults again (recent libraries kept), after a backup copy.
    pub fn reset_settings(&self) -> Result<SettingsView, IpcError> {
        let mut p = lock(&self.prefs);
        backup_settings(&self.config_dir, "reset").map_err(|e| IpcError::Settings(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut next = Settings::default();
        next.library.recent = p.settings.library.recent.clone();
        save_settings(&self.config_dir, &next).map_err(|e| IpcError::Settings(e.to_string()))?;
        p.settings = next;
        p.report = LoadReport::default();
        Ok(SettingsView::new(p.settings.clone(), &p.report))
    }

    pub fn config_dir(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.config_dir
    }

    pub fn logs_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.base.data_dir.join("logs")
    }

    /// The fill configuration with the current settings: each job keeps
    /// the snapshot it started with.
    pub fn fill_config(&self) -> Arc<FillConfig> {
        let s = self.settings();
        Arc::new(FillConfig {
            cache_dir: self.base.cache_dir.clone(),
            data_dir: s.data_dir(&self.base.data_dir),
            endpoints: self.base.endpoints.clone(),
            net: NetConfig {
                min_interval: self.base.net.min_interval,
                backoff_base: self.base.net.backoff_base,
                ..s.net_config()
            },
            settings: s,
        })
    }

    pub fn cache_usage(&self) -> u64 {
        coverly_core::cache::cache_usage(&self.base.cache_dir)
    }

    pub fn clear_cache(&self) -> Result<u64, IpcError> {
        let _writing = self.jobs.begin_manual()?;
        coverly_core::cache::clear_cache(&self.base.cache_dir).map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))
    }
}

fn lock<T>(m: &Mutex<T>) -> std::sync::MutexGuard<'_, T> {
    m.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
}

fn known_ids(custom: &[ProfileFile]) -> Vec<String> {
    builtin_profiles().into_iter().map(|p| p.id).chain(custom.iter().map(|c| c.id.clone())).collect()
}

fn custom_dir_store(config_dir: &Path, custom_dir: Option<&Path>) -> ProfileStore {
    ProfileStore::new(custom_dir.map_or_else(|| config_dir.join(PROFILES_DIR), Path::to_path_buf))
}
```

> Keep the old `self.fill` users working by replacing `self.fill`/`self.fill_config()` reads with `let cfg = self.fill_config();` at the start of each method (jobs, drawer, undo, history). `open` uses `self.settings()` for `scan_options()`, `custom_profiles()`, `library.default_profile` and `Some(&settings)`; it saves the recents with `save_settings` through `update`-like code (`p.settings.remember(…)` under the lock, then `save_settings`; a failed save only logs a warning). `library_settings`/`set_ignored_dirs` read/write `settings.library.ignored_dirs` through `update_settings(json!({"library": {"ignored_dirs": dirs}}))`. Tests that built `AppState` and relied on `NetConfig { retries: 0, .. }` in their `FillConfig` now set it with `st.update_settings(&json!({"network": {"retries": 0}}))` (add a `common::fast(&st)` helper).

Data folder guard (used by `start_fill`, `writable_entry`, `set_entry_art_from_search` in Task 10):

```rust
fn check_data_dir(cfg: &FillConfig, session: &Session) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
    let root = absolute_root(session)?;
    let data = std::path::absolute(&cfg.data_dir).map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
    if data.starts_with(&root) {
        return Err(IpcError::BadRequest("the backup folder is inside the library".into()));
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

Drawer writes pass `cfg.settings.manual_options()` to `write_local_image` and `cfg.settings.manual_options().keep_jobs` to `remove_art`; `set_entry_art` uses `ApplyOptions { keep_jobs: …, ..}` from the settings.

`src-tauri/src/ipc.rs`:

```rust
/// Event carrying the saved `Settings` after an update or a reset.
pub const SETTINGS_CHANGED_EVENT: &str = "settings-changed";

#[tauri::command]
pub fn get_settings(state: State<'_, AppState>) -> SettingsView {
    state.settings_view()
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn update_settings(app: AppHandle, patch: serde_json::Value) -> Result<SettingsUpdateView, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || {
        let state = app.state::<AppState>();
        let out = state.update_settings(&patch)?;
        if out.errors.is_empty() {
            crate::logging::set_level(out.settings.advanced.log_level);
            let _ = app.emit(SETTINGS_CHANGED_EVENT, &out.settings);
        }
        Ok(out)
    })
    .await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn reset_settings(app: AppHandle) -> Result<SettingsView, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || {
        let view = app.state::<AppState>().reset_settings()?;
        crate::logging::set_level(view.settings.advanced.log_level);
        let _ = app.emit(SETTINGS_CHANGED_EVENT, &view.settings);
        Ok(view)
    })
    .await
}

fn open_dir(app: &AppHandle, dir: &Path) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(dir).map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
    app.opener()
        .open_path(dir.to_string_lossy(), None::<&str>)
        .map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn open_config_folder(app: AppHandle, state: State<'_, AppState>) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
    open_dir(&app, state.config_dir())
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn open_logs_folder(app: AppHandle, state: State<'_, AppState>) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
    open_dir(&app, &state.logs_dir())
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn open_link(app: AppHandle, link: LinkId) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
    app.opener()
        .open_url(link_url(link), None::<&str>)
        .map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn cache_usage(app: AppHandle) -> Result<u64, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || Ok(app.state::<AppState>().cache_usage())).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn clear_cache(app: AppHandle) -> Result<u64, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || app.state::<AppState>().clear_cache()).await
}
```

```rust
mod common;

use std::collections::BTreeMap;

use coverly_core::profiles::custom::{PreviewExample, ProfileFile, SlotFile};
use coverly_core::profiles::ImageFormat;
use coverly_core::settings::FieldErrorCode;
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_roms_lib::error::IpcError;
use coverly_roms_lib::jobs::FillConfig;
use coverly_roms_lib::state::AppState;
use serde_json::json;
use tempfile::TempDir;

fn state(dir: &TempDir) -> AppState {
    AppState::new(dir.path().join("config"), FillConfig::new(dir.path().join("cache"), dir.path().join("data")))
}

fn mine(id: &str, template: &str) -> ProfileFile {
    ProfileFile {
        id: id.into(),
        name: "Mine".into(),
        roms_root: "Games".into(),
        art_dirs: vec!["covers".into()],
        gamelist: false,
        slots: BTreeMap::from([(
            "cover".into(),
            SlotFile { template: template.into(), format: ImageFormat::Png, max_width: None, max_height: None },
        )]),
    }
}

#[test]
fn builtins_are_listed_read_only_and_custom_ones_are_saved_renamed_and_deleted() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let list = st.list_profiles();
    assert!(list.iter().all(|p| p.builtin));
    let out = st.save_profile(mine("mine", "{system_dir}/covers/{stem}.png"), None).unwrap();
    assert!(out.errors.is_empty(), "{:?}", out.errors);
    assert!(out.profiles.iter().any(|p| p.profile.id == "mine" && !p.builtin));
    assert!(dir.path().join("config/profiles/mine.toml").exists());
    st.update_settings(&json!({"library": {"default_profile": "mine"}})).unwrap();
    let out = st.save_profile(mine("mine-2", "{system_dir}/covers/{stem}.png"), Some("mine".into())).unwrap();
    assert!(out.errors.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(st.settings().library.default_profile.as_deref(), Some("mine-2"), "the default follows a rename");
    st.delete_profile("mine-2").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(st.settings().library.default_profile, None, "deleting the default falls back to detect");
    assert!(matches!(st.delete_profile("onion"), Err(IpcError::BadRequest(_))), "built-ins are read-only");
    assert!(matches!(st.delete_profile("ghost"), Err(IpcError::UnknownProfile(_))));
}

#[test]
fn invalid_profiles_come_back_as_field_errors_and_are_not_saved() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let out = st.save_profile(mine("mine", "{root}/../x/{stem}.png"), None).unwrap();
    assert!(out.errors.iter().any(|e| e.field == "slots.cover.template" && e.code == FieldErrorCode::Invalid));
    assert!(!dir.path().join("config/profiles/mine.toml").exists());
    st.save_profile(mine("taken", "{system_dir}/covers/{stem}.png"), None).unwrap();
    let out = st.save_profile(mine("taken", "{system_dir}/covers/{stem}.png"), None).unwrap();
    assert!(out.errors.iter().any(|e| e.field == "id" && e.code == FieldErrorCode::Duplicate));
}

#[test]
fn preview_and_opening_with_a_custom_default_profile() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir);
    let p = mine("mine", "{system_dir}/covers/{stem}.png");
    let preview = st.preview_profile(&p, &PreviewExample { system_folder: "GBA".into(), rel_dir: "".into(), file_name: "Golden Sun.gba".into() });
    assert_eq!(preview.paths["cover"], "Games/GBA/covers/Golden Sun.png");
    assert!(preview.errors.is_empty());
    st.save_profile(p, None).unwrap();
    st.update_settings(&json!({"library": {"default_profile": "mine"}})).unwrap();
    let lib = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    std::fs::create_dir_all(lib.path().join("Games/GBA")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(lib.path().join("Games/GBA/Golden Sun.gba"), b"x").unwrap();
    let view = st.open(lib.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {}).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(view.profile.id, "mine");
    assert_eq!(view.entries.len(), 1);
}
```

`src-tauri/tests/credentials_state.rs`:

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;

use coverly_core::credentials::{CredentialKey, MemoryStore};
use coverly_roms_lib::error::IpcError;
use coverly_roms_lib::jobs::FillConfig;
use coverly_roms_lib::state::AppState;
use tempfile::TempDir;

fn state(dir: &TempDir, store: MemoryStore) -> AppState {
    AppState::new(dir.path().join("config"), FillConfig::new(dir.path().join("cache"), dir.path().join("data")))
        .with_credentials(Arc::new(store))
}

#[test]
fn credential_values_never_come_back() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir, MemoryStore::default());
    let status = st.set_credential("steamgriddb-api-key", "sk-SECRET-123").unwrap();
    assert!(status.available && status.set[&CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey]);
    let wire = serde_json::to_string(&status).unwrap();
    assert!(!wire.contains("SECRET"));
    assert!(!serde_json::to_string(&st.settings_view()).unwrap().contains("SECRET"));
    st.update_settings(&serde_json::json!({"general": {"theme": "dark"}})).unwrap();
    let file = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join("config/settings.json")).unwrap();
    assert!(!file.contains("SECRET"), "never in the settings file");
    let cleared = st.clear_credential("steamgriddb-api-key").unwrap();
    assert!(!cleared.set[&CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey]);
}

#[test]
fn unknown_keys_bad_values_and_a_missing_keychain_are_errors() {
    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();
    let st = state(&dir, MemoryStore::default());
    assert!(matches!(st.set_credential("github-token", "x"), Err(IpcError::BadRequest(_))));
    assert!(matches!(st.set_credential("screenscraper-user", "a\u{0}b"), Err(IpcError::BadRequest(_))));
    let none = state(&TempDir::new().unwrap(), MemoryStore::unavailable());
    assert!(!none.credential_status().available);
    assert_eq!(none.set_credential("screenscraper-user", "me").unwrap_err(), IpcError::KeychainUnavailable);
    assert_eq!(
        serde_json::to_value(IpcError::KeychainLocked).unwrap(),
        serde_json::json!({"code": "keychain-locked"})
    );
}
```

`src-tauri/tests/search_state.rs` (uses `common::fill::fill_world`, entry 3 = `004 Gldn Rsh`):

```rust
mod common;

use std::sync::Arc;

use common::fill::fill_world;
use coverly_core::identify::IdMethod;
use coverly_core::profiles::Media;
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_roms_lib::error::IpcError;
use coverly_roms_lib::state::AppState;

#[test]
fn search_pick_is_remembered_and_keeps_the_file_name() {
    let w = fill_world();
    let st = AppState::new(w.dirs.path().join("config"), (*w.cfg).clone());
    common::fast(&st);
    let view = st.open(w.lib.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {}).unwrap();
    let g = view.generation;
    let found = st.search_entry_art(g, 3, Media::Cover, "golden sun").unwrap();
    let pick = found.iter().find(|c| c.name == "Golden Sun (USA, Europe)").expect("listed");
    let bytes = st.entry_search_art(g, 3, Media::Cover, pick.index).unwrap();
    assert!(!bytes.is_empty());
    let updates = st.set_entry_art_from_search(g, 3, Media::Cover, pick.index).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(updates.len(), 1);
    assert!(w.lib.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Imgs/004 Gldn Rsh.png").exists(), "the ROM's own name");
    assert!(!w.lib.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Imgs/Golden Sun (USA, Europe).png").exists());
    let id = st.identify_entry(g, 3, &SystemDb::builtin()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(id.method, IdMethod::Manual);
    assert_eq!(id.canonical.as_deref(), Some("Golden Sun (USA, Europe)"));
    st.undo_last_job(g).unwrap();
    assert!(!w.lib.path().join("ROMS/GBA/Imgs/004 Gldn Rsh.png").exists());
    assert_eq!(st.identify_entry(g, 3, &SystemDb::builtin()).unwrap().method, IdMethod::Manual, "a manual match is data, not undone with the art");
    st.clear_identity_override(g, 3).unwrap();
    assert_ne!(st.identify_entry(g, 3, &SystemDb::builtin()).unwrap().method, IdMethod::Manual);
}

#[test]
fn hostile_queries_and_unlisted_picks_are_refused() {
    let w = fill_world();
    let st = Arc::new(AppState::new(w.dirs.path().join("config"), (*w.cfg).clone()));
    common::fast(&st);
    let g = st.open(w.lib.path(), None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {}).unwrap().generation;
    for q in ["", "x\u{1b}[31m", &"y".repeat(5000)] {
        assert!(matches!(st.search_entry_art(g, 3, Media::Cover, q), Err(IpcError::BadRequest(_))), "{q:?}");
    }
    assert!(matches!(st.set_entry_art_from_search(g, 3, Media::Cover, 0), Err(IpcError::BadRequest(_))), "search first");
}
```

```rust
//! Layout profiles on the wire.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;

use coverly_core::profiles::custom::ProfileFile;
use coverly_core::profiles::Profile;
use coverly_core::settings::FieldError;
use serde::Serialize;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ProfileInfoView {
    pub profile: ProfileFile,
    pub builtin: bool,
    pub mirror_subdirs: bool,
}

impl ProfileInfoView {
    pub fn of(p: &Profile, builtin: bool) -> ProfileInfoView {
        ProfileInfoView { profile: ProfileFile::from_profile(p), builtin, mirror_subdirs: p.mirror_subdirs }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct ProfileSaveView {
    pub profiles: Vec<ProfileInfoView>,
    pub errors: Vec<FieldError>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct ProfilePreviewView {
    /// `cover`/`screenshot`/`title` → library-relative path.
    pub paths: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub errors: Vec<FieldError>,
}
```

`state.rs` (key parts):

```rust
    pub fn list_profiles(&self) -> Vec<ProfileInfoView> {
        all_profiles(&self.custom_profiles()).iter().map(|(p, b)| ProfileInfoView::of(p, *b)).collect()
    }

    pub fn save_profile(&self, p: ProfileFile, replaces: Option<String>) -> Result<ProfileSaveView, IpcError> {
        let store = self.custom_store();
        let customs = store.load().0;
        if let Some(old) = &replaces {
            if !customs.iter().any(|c| &c.id == old) {
                return Err(IpcError::BadRequest(format!("no custom profile `{old}`")));
            }
        }
        let builtin_ids: Vec<String> = builtin_profiles().into_iter().map(|b| b.id).collect();
        let mut errors = validate_profile(&p, &builtin_ids);
        let taken = customs.iter().any(|c| c.id == p.id && replaces.as_deref() != Some(c.id.as_str()));
        if taken && !errors.iter().any(|e| e.field == "id") {
            errors.push(FieldError::new("id", FieldErrorCode::Duplicate));
        }
        if !errors.is_empty() {
            return Ok(ProfileSaveView { profiles: self.list_profiles(), errors });
        }
        store.save(&p, replaces.as_deref()).map_err(|e| IpcError::Settings(e.to_string()))?;
        if let Some(old) = replaces.filter(|o| *o != p.id) {
            self.rename_profile_refs(&old, Some(&p.id))?;
        }
        Ok(ProfileSaveView { profiles: self.list_profiles(), errors: Vec::new() })
    }

    pub fn delete_profile(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Vec<ProfileInfoView>, IpcError> {
        if builtin_profiles().iter().any(|b| b.id.eq_ignore_ascii_case(id)) {
            return Err(IpcError::BadRequest("built-in profiles are read-only".into()));
        }
        let deleted = self.custom_store().delete(id).map_err(|_| IpcError::UnknownProfile(id.to_string()))?;
        if !deleted {
            return Err(IpcError::UnknownProfile(id.to_string()));
        }
        self.rename_profile_refs(id, None)?;
        Ok(self.list_profiles())
    }

    /// The default profile and the per-profile max size follow a rename
    /// (or are dropped with a delete).
    fn rename_profile_refs(&self, old: &str, new: Option<&str>) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
        let s = self.settings();
        let mut patch = serde_json::Map::new();
        if s.library.default_profile.as_deref() == Some(old) {
            patch.insert("library".into(), json!({"default_profile": new}));
        }
        if let Some(size) = s.art.max_size.get(old) {
            let mut sizes = s.art.max_size.clone();
            sizes.remove(old);
            if let Some(n) = new {
                sizes.insert(n.to_string(), *size);
            }
            patch.insert("art".into(), json!({"max_size": sizes}));
        }
        if patch.is_empty() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let out = self.update_settings(&serde_json::Value::Object(patch))?;
        if out.errors.is_empty() { Ok(()) } else { Err(IpcError::Settings(format!("{:?}", out.errors))) }
    }

    pub fn preview_profile(&self, p: &ProfileFile, ex: &PreviewExample) -> ProfilePreviewView {
        let builtin_ids: Vec<String> = builtin_profiles().into_iter().map(|b| b.id).collect();
        ProfilePreviewView {
            paths: preview_paths(p, ex, &SystemDb::builtin()),
            // The id of a profile being created may still be empty: only
            // the layout errors matter for a preview.
            errors: validate_profile(p, &builtin_ids).into_iter().filter(|e| e.field != "id").collect(),
        }
    }

    pub fn credential_status(&self) -> CredentialStatus {
        coverly_core::credentials::status(self.credentials.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn set_credential(&self, key: &str, value: &str) -> Result<CredentialStatus, IpcError> {
        let key = CredentialKey::parse(key).ok_or_else(|| IpcError::BadRequest(format!("unknown credential `{key}`")))?;
        store_secret(self.credentials.as_ref(), key, value)?;
        Ok(self.credential_status())
    }

    pub fn clear_credential(&self, key: &str) -> Result<CredentialStatus, IpcError> {
        let key = CredentialKey::parse(key).ok_or_else(|| IpcError::BadRequest(format!("unknown credential `{key}`")))?;
        if !self.credentials.available() {
            return Err(IpcError::KeychainUnavailable);
        }
        self.credentials.clear(key)?;
        Ok(self.credential_status())
    }
```

Search (a `searches: Mutex<Option<EntrySearch>>` slot next to `picks`, `struct EntrySearch { generation: u64, id: u32, media: Media, candidates: Vec<Candidate> }`):

```rust
    fn rom_ref<'s>(session: &'s Session, id: u32, rel: &'s mut PathBuf) -> RomRef<'s> {
        let entry = &session.entries[id as usize];
        let sys = &session.systems[entry.system as usize];
        *rel = entry.rom.path.strip_prefix(&sys.dir).map(Path::to_path_buf).unwrap_or_else(|_| entry.rom.path.clone());
        RomRef { system_id: &entry.system_id, folder: &sys.folder, system_dir: &sys.dir, rel_path: rel, stem: &entry.rom.stem }
    }

    pub fn search_entry_art(&self, generation: u64, id: u32, media: Media, query: &str) -> Result<Vec<CandidateView>, IpcError> {
        let session = self.known_entry(generation, id)?;
        if session.profile.slot(media).is_none() {
            return Err(IpcError::BadRequest("this layout cannot store that media".into()));
        }
        let cfg = self.fill_config();
        let db = SystemDb::builtin();
        let system = db.get(&session.entries[id as usize].system_id).ok_or(IpcError::UnknownEntry(id))?;
        let found = with_env(&cfg, |env| Ok(env.search_title(system, media, query, &cfg.settings.fill_settings())?))?;
        let views = found.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, c)| candidate_view(i, c)).collect();
        *lock(&self.searches) = Some(EntrySearch { generation, id, media, candidates: found });
        Ok(views)
    }

    fn searched(&self, generation: u64, id: u32, media: Media, candidate: u32) -> Result<Candidate, IpcError> {
        match lock(&self.searches).as_ref() {
            Some(s) if s.generation == generation && s.id == id && s.media == media => s
                .candidates
                .get(candidate as usize)
                .cloned()
                .ok_or_else(|| IpcError::BadRequest(format!("no candidate {candidate}"))),
            _ => Err(IpcError::BadRequest("search first".into())),
        }
    }

    pub fn entry_search_art(&self, generation: u64, id: u32, media: Media, candidate: u32) -> Result<Vec<u8>, IpcError> {
        let session = self.session(generation)?;
        let c = self.searched(generation, id, media, candidate)?;
        let cfg = self.fill_config();
        with_env(&cfg, |env| Ok(env.candidate_image(&session.profile, &c)?.bytes))
    }

    pub fn set_entry_art_from_search(&self, generation: u64, id: u32, media: Media, candidate: u32) -> Result<Vec<EntryArtUpdate>, IpcError> {
        let (_writing, session) = self.writable_entry(generation, id)?;
        let c = self.searched(generation, id, media, candidate)?;
        let cfg = self.fill_config();
        check_data_dir(&cfg, &session)?;
        let target = art_target(&session, id, media)?;
        let mut rel = PathBuf::new();
        let rom = Self::rom_ref(&session, id, &mut rel);
        let root = absolute_root(&session)?;
        let report = with_env(&cfg, |env| {
            env.apply_candidate(&root, &session.profile, &rom, media, &target, &c, cfg.settings.manual_options().keep_jobs)
                .map_err(IpcError::from)
        })?;
        if let Some(ItemStatus::Failed { kind, message }) = report.items.first().map(|i| &i.status) {
            return Err(item_error(*kind, message));
        }
        if let Some(why) = report.paused {
            return Err(IpcError::Network(why));
        }
        remember_manual(&cfg.data_dir, &session.entries[id as usize].rom.path, &c.name, &c.source)
            .map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))?;
        self.refresh_entries(generation, &[id], &SystemDb::builtin())
    }

    pub fn clear_identity_override(&self, generation: u64, id: u32) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
        let session = self.known_entry(generation, id)?;
        forget_manual(&self.fill_config().data_dir, &session.entries[id as usize].rom.path)
            .map(|_| ())
            .map_err(|e| IpcError::Io(e.to_string()))
    }
```


```rust
impl From<coverly_core::credentials::CredentialError> for IpcError {
    fn from(e: coverly_core::credentials::CredentialError) -> Self {
        use coverly_core::credentials::CredentialError as C;
        match e {
            C::Unavailable => IpcError::KeychainUnavailable,
            C::Locked => IpcError::KeychainLocked,
            C::Failed(m) => IpcError::KeychainFailed(m),
            C::BadValue => IpcError::BadRequest("the value is too long or holds control characters".into()),
        }
    }
}
```

`ipc.rs` — one-liners on the blocking pool (keychain calls block and may raise an OS prompt; never on the main thread):

```rust
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn list_profiles(app: AppHandle) -> Result<Vec<ProfileInfoView>, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || Ok(app.state::<AppState>().list_profiles())).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn save_profile(app: AppHandle, profile: ProfileFile, replaces: Option<String>) -> Result<ProfileSaveView, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || app.state::<AppState>().save_profile(profile, replaces)).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn delete_profile(app: AppHandle, id: String) -> Result<Vec<ProfileInfoView>, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || app.state::<AppState>().delete_profile(&id)).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn preview_profile(state: State<'_, AppState>, profile: ProfileFile, example: PreviewExample) -> ProfilePreviewView {
    state.preview_profile(&profile, &example)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn credential_status(app: AppHandle) -> Result<CredentialStatus, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || Ok(app.state::<AppState>().credential_status())).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn set_credential(app: AppHandle, key: String, value: String) -> Result<CredentialStatus, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || app.state::<AppState>().set_credential(&key, &value)).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn clear_credential(app: AppHandle, key: String) -> Result<CredentialStatus, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || app.state::<AppState>().clear_credential(&key)).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn search_entry_art(app: AppHandle, generation: u64, entry_id: u32, media: Media, query: String) -> Result<Vec<CandidateView>, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || app.state::<AppState>().search_entry_art(generation, entry_id, media, &query)).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn entry_search_art(app: AppHandle, generation: u64, entry_id: u32, media: Media, candidate: u32) -> Result<tauri::ipc::Response, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || app.state::<AppState>().entry_search_art(generation, entry_id, media, candidate).map(tauri::ipc::Response::new)).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn set_entry_art_from_search(app: AppHandle, generation: u64, entry_id: u32, media: Media, candidate: u32) -> Result<Vec<EntryArtUpdate>, IpcError> {
    blocking(move || app.state::<AppState>().set_entry_art_from_search(generation, entry_id, media, candidate)).await
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn clear_identity_override(app: AppHandle, generation: u64, entry_id: u32) -> Result<(), IpcError> {
    blocking(move || app.state::<AppState>().clear_identity_override(generation, entry_id)).await
}
```

```ts
export type LanguageChoice = 'auto' | 'en' | 'es';
export type ThemeChoice = 'system' | 'light' | 'dark';
export type UpdateChannel = 'stable' | 'beta';
export type OverwriteSetting = 'never' | 'ask' | 'backup';
export type OutputFormat = 'profile' | 'png' | 'jpg';
export type ProxyChoice = 'system' | 'env' | 'manual';
export type LogLevel = 'error' | 'warn' | 'info' | 'debug';
export type MatchMethod = 'hash-then-name' | 'hash-only' | 'name-only';
export type SourceId = 'libretro' | 'steamgriddb' | 'screenscraper';
export interface RecentEntry { path: string; name: string; profile_id: string; roms: number; opened_at: number }
export interface MaxSize { width: number; height: number }
export interface SourceEntry { id: SourceId; enabled: boolean }
export interface Settings {
  version: number;
  general: { language: LanguageChoice; theme: ThemeChoice; check_updates_on_launch: boolean; update_channel: UpdateChannel };
  library: { default_profile: string | null; ignored_dirs: string[]; ignored_exts: string[]; include_hidden: boolean; follow_symlinks: boolean; recent: RecentEntry[] };
  art: { media: Media[]; single_slot_media: Media; output_format: OutputFormat; max_size: Record<string, MaxSize>; keep_aspect: boolean; jpeg_quality: number; overwrite: OverwriteSetting };
  matching: { strategy: StrategyKind; regions: RegionPref[]; languages: string[]; auto_threshold: number; review_threshold: number; fuzzy: boolean; arcade_translation: boolean; match_method: MatchMethod; max_hash_mb: number };
  sources: { order: SourceEntry[] };
  network: { concurrency: number; timeout_secs: number; retries: number; proxy: ProxyChoice; proxy_url: string; cache_max_mb: number; index_refresh_hours: number };
  safety: { backup_before_overwrite: boolean; backup_dir: string | null; dry_run_default: boolean; clean_apple_double: boolean; undo_depth: number };
  advanced: { log_level: LogLevel; custom_dir: string | null };
}
export type SettingsGroup = Exclude<keyof Settings, 'version'>;
export type SettingsPatch = { [G in SettingsGroup]?: Partial<Settings[G]> };
export type FieldErrorCode = 'range' | 'required' | 'invalid' | 'order' | 'unknown' | 'duplicate' | 'not-absolute';
export interface FieldError { field: string; code: FieldErrorCode; min: number | null; max: number | null }
export type SettingsNotice = 'migrated' | 'reset-corrupt' | 'fixed' | 'future-version';
export interface SettingsView { settings: Settings; notice: SettingsNotice | null; backup: string | null; fixed: string[] }
export interface SettingsUpdate { settings: Settings; errors: FieldError[]; reopen: boolean }
export type LinkId = 'repo' | 'license' | 'notices' | 'trademarks' | 'releases' | 'issues';
export type CredentialKey = 'steamgriddb-api-key' | 'screenscraper-user' | 'screenscraper-password';
export const CREDENTIAL_KEYS: readonly CredentialKey[] = ['steamgriddb-api-key', 'screenscraper-user', 'screenscraper-password'];
export interface CredentialStatus { available: boolean; set: Record<CredentialKey, boolean> }
export interface SlotFile { template: string; format: 'png' | 'jpg'; max_width: number | null; max_height: number | null }
export interface ProfileFile { id: string; name: string; roms_root: string; art_dirs: string[]; gamelist: boolean; slots: Partial<Record<Media, SlotFile>> }
export interface ProfileInfo { profile: ProfileFile; builtin: boolean; mirror_subdirs: boolean }
export interface ProfileSaveOutcome { profiles: ProfileInfo[]; errors: FieldError[] }
export interface PreviewExample { system_folder: string; rel_dir: string; file_name: string }
export interface ProfilePreview { paths: Partial<Record<Media, string>>; errors: FieldError[] }
// IdMethod gains 'manual'.
```

`src/test/storage.ts`:

```ts
/** An in-memory `Storage` for tests (jsdom's is shared between tests). */
export class MemoryStorage implements Storage {
  private map = new Map<string, string>();
  get length(): number {
    return this.map.size;
  }
  clear(): void {
    this.map.clear();
  }
  getItem(key: string): string | null {
    return this.map.get(key) ?? null;
  }
  key(index: number): string | null {
    return [...this.map.keys()][index] ?? null;
  }
  removeItem(key: string): void {
    this.map.delete(key);
  }
  setItem(key: string, value: string): void {
    this.map.set(key, String(value));
  }
}
```

`src/lib/settingsRules.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import {
  DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  cloneSettings,
  leafPaths,
  mergePatch,
  needsReopen,
  normaliseSettings,
  tolerantSettings,
  validateSettings,
} from './settingsRules';

const known = ['anbernic-stock', 'onion', 'esde', 'batocera', 'retroarch'];
const codes = (s = DEFAULT_SETTINGS) =>
  validateSettings(s, known).map((e) => `${e.field}:${e.code}`);

describe('settings rules (mirror of coverly_core::settings)', () => {
  it('defaults are valid', () => {
    expect(codes()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('reports ranges with their bounds', () => {
    const s = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    s.network.timeout_secs = 1;
    const e = validateSettings(s, known).find((x) => x.field === 'network.timeout_secs');
    expect(e).toEqual({ field: 'network.timeout_secs', code: 'range', min: 5, max: 300 });
  });

  it('checks lists, order, proxy, locked backups and folders', () => {
    const s = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    s.matching.review_threshold = 0.95;
    s.matching.regions = [];
    s.art.media = [];
    s.network.proxy = 'manual';
    s.safety.backup_before_overwrite = false;
    s.safety.backup_dir = 'relative';
    s.library.default_profile = 'ghost';
    expect(codes(s)).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining([
        'matching.review_threshold:order',
        'matching.regions:required',
        'art.media:required',
        'network.proxy_url:required',
        'safety.backup_before_overwrite:invalid',
        'safety.backup_dir:not-absolute',
        'library.default_profile:unknown',
      ]),
    );
  });

  it('merges patches like RFC 7386', () => {
    const next = mergePatch(DEFAULT_SETTINGS, {
      network: { timeout_secs: 60 },
      safety: { backup_dir: null },
    });
    expect(next.network.timeout_secs).toBe(60);
    expect(next.network.retries).toBe(3);
    expect(DEFAULT_SETTINGS.network.timeout_secs).toBe(30);
    expect(leafPaths({ network: { timeout_secs: 1 }, art: { media: ['cover'] } })).toEqual([
      'network.timeout_secs',
      'art.media',
    ]);
  });

  it('normalises lists and keeps unavailable sources off', () => {
    const s = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    s.library.ignored_exts = ['.PNG', 'png', ' txt '];
    s.sources.order = [
      { id: 'steamgriddb', enabled: true },
      { id: 'libretro', enabled: true },
    ];
    const n = normaliseSettings(s);
    expect(n.library.ignored_exts).toEqual(['png', 'txt']);
    expect(n.sources.order).toEqual([
      { id: 'steamgriddb', enabled: false },
      { id: 'libretro', enabled: true },
      { id: 'screenscraper', enabled: false },
    ]);
  });

  it('reads stored settings field by field', () => {
    const { settings, fixed } = tolerantSettings(
      { general: { theme: 'dark' }, network: { timeout_secs: 'soon', concurrency: 99 }, junk: 1 },
      known,
    );
    expect(settings.general.theme).toBe('dark');
    expect(settings.network.timeout_secs).toBe(30);
    expect(settings.network.concurrency).toBe(4);
    expect(fixed).toEqual(['network.timeout_secs', 'network.concurrency']);
    expect(tolerantSettings('garbage', known).settings).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  });

  it('knows which changes need a reopen', () => {
    const b = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    b.general.theme = 'dark';
    expect(needsReopen(DEFAULT_SETTINGS, b)).toBe(false);
    b.library.include_hidden = true;
    expect(needsReopen(DEFAULT_SETTINGS, b)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import type { ProfileFile } from '@/ipc/types';

import { previewProfile, renderTemplate, validateProfile } from './profileTemplate';

const mine: ProfileFile = {
  id: 'my-sd',
  name: 'My SD',
  roms_root: 'Roms',
  art_dirs: ['Imgs'],
  gamelist: false,
  slots: { cover: { template: '{system_dir}/{rel_dir}/Imgs/{stem}.png', format: 'png', max_width: 250, max_height: 360 } },
};
const builtins = ['onion', 'batocera'];

describe('profile templates (mirror of profiles::custom)', () => {
  it('renders without empty or doubled separators', () => {
    expect(
      renderTemplate('{system_dir}/{rel_dir}/Imgs/{stem}.png', {
        root: 'LIB',
        system_dir: 'LIB/Roms/SFC',
        rel_dir: '',
        stem: 'Zelda',
        system_id: 'snes',
        libretro: null,
      }),
    ).toBe('LIB/Roms/SFC/Imgs/Zelda.png');
  });

  it('previews library-relative paths', () => {
    expect(
      previewProfile(mine, { system_folder: 'SFC', rel_dir: 'Hacks', file_name: 'Zelda (USA).sfc' }),
    ).toEqual({ cover: 'Roms/SFC/Hacks/Imgs/Zelda (USA).png' });
  });

  it('refuses templates that escape the library and reserved ids', () => {
    const bad: ProfileFile = {
      ...mine,
      id: 'onion',
      slots: { cover: { ...mine.slots.cover!, template: '{root}/../{stem}.png' } },
    };
    expect(validateProfile(bad, builtins).map((e) => `${e.field}:${e.code}`)).toEqual([
      'id:duplicate',
      'slots.cover.template:invalid',
    ]);
    expect(validateProfile(mine, builtins)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`src/ipc/mock/mockSettings.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from '@/lib/settingsRules';
import { MemoryStorage } from '@/test/storage';

import { BackendError } from '../errors';
import { FIXTURE_NOW } from './fixture';
import { createMockBackend } from './mockBackend';

const make = (storage: Storage | null = new MemoryStorage()) =>
  createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW, storage });

describe('mock settings', () => {
  it('starts with the defaults and persists valid updates across a reload', async () => {
    const storage = new MemoryStorage();
    const a = make(storage);
    expect((await a.getSettings()).settings).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    const changed: string[] = [];
    await a.onSettingsChanged((s) => changed.push(s.general.theme));
    const out = await a.updateSettings({ general: { theme: 'dark' }, network: { timeout_secs: 45 } });
    expect(out.errors).toEqual([]);
    expect(changed).toEqual(['dark']);
    const b = make(storage);
    const again = (await b.getSettings()).settings;
    expect(again.general.theme).toBe('dark');
    expect(again.network.timeout_secs).toBe(45);
  });

  it('returns field errors and saves nothing for invalid input', async () => {
    const storage = new MemoryStorage();
    const m = make(storage);
    const out = await m.updateSettings({ network: { timeout_secs: 1 } });
    expect(out.errors).toEqual([{ field: 'network.timeout_secs', code: 'range', min: 5, max: 300 }]);
    expect(out.settings.network.timeout_secs).toBe(30);
    expect(storage.getItem('coverly.mock.settings')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('falls back to defaults with a notice when storage holds garbage', async () => {
    const storage = new MemoryStorage();
    storage.setItem('coverly.mock.settings', '{nope');
    const view = await make(storage).getSettings();
    expect(view.settings).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    expect(view.notice).toBe('reset-corrupt');
  });

  it('works without storage', async () => {
    const m = make(null);
    await m.updateSettings({ general: { language: 'es' } });
    expect((await m.getSettings()).settings.general.language).toBe('es');
  });

  it('resets to defaults', async () => {
    const m = make();
    await m.updateSettings({ art: { jpeg_quality: 70 } });
    expect((await m.resetSettings()).settings.art.jpeg_quality).toBe(90);
  });

  it('never stores secret values', async () => {
    const storage = new MemoryStorage();
    const m = make(storage);
    const st = await m.setCredential('steamgriddb-api-key', 'sk-SECRET');
    expect(st.set['steamgriddb-api-key']).toBe(true);
    for (let i = 0; i < storage.length; i++) {
      expect(storage.getItem(storage.key(i)!)).not.toContain('SECRET');
    }
    expect((await make(storage).credentialStatus()).set['steamgriddb-api-key']).toBe(true);
    expect((await m.clearCredential('steamgriddb-api-key')).set['steamgriddb-api-key']).toBe(false);
  });

  it('lists built-in profiles read-only and saves, renames and deletes custom ones', async () => {
    const m = make();
    const builtins = await m.listProfiles();
    expect(builtins.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(builtins.every((p) => p.builtin)).toBe(true);
    const onion = builtins.find((p) => p.profile.id === 'onion')!;
    const copy = { ...onion.profile, id: 'my-onion', name: 'My Onion' };
    expect((await m.saveProfile(copy, null)).errors).toEqual([]);
    const renamed = await m.saveProfile({ ...copy, id: 'my-onion-2' }, 'my-onion');
    expect(renamed.profiles.map((p) => p.profile.id)).toContain('my-onion-2');
    expect(renamed.profiles.map((p) => p.profile.id)).not.toContain('my-onion');
    const bad = await m.saveProfile({ ...copy, id: 'onion' }, null);
    expect(bad.errors.map((e) => e.field)).toContain('id');
    await expect(m.deleteProfile('onion')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(BackendError);
    expect((await m.deleteProfile('my-onion-2')).some((p) => p.profile.id === 'my-onion-2')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('previews a profile and records opened folders and links', async () => {
    const m = make();
    const p = (await m.listProfiles()).find((x) => x.profile.id === 'onion')!.profile;
    const prev = await m.previewProfile(p, { system_folder: 'GBA', rel_dir: '', file_name: 'Golden Sun.gba' });
    expect(prev.paths.cover).toBe('Roms/GBA/Imgs/Golden Sun.png');
    await m.openLink('license');
    await m.openLogsFolder();
    expect(m.opened).toEqual(['link:license', 'folder:logs']);
    expect(await m.cacheUsage()).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(await m.clearCache()).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(await m.cacheUsage()).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('mock drawer search', () => {
  it('lists candidates for a typed title, writes the pick and remembers it', async () => {
    const b = createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW, storage: null });
    const view = await b.openLibrary(MOCK_LIBRARIES[1].path);
    const entry = view.entries.find((e) => e.art.cover === 'missing')!;
    const found = await b.searchEntryArt(view.generation, entry.id, 'cover', '  Golden Sun ');
    expect(found.map((c) => c.name)).toEqual(['Golden Sun (USA)', 'Golden Sun (Europe)', 'Golden Sun (Japan)']);
    const art = await b.entrySearchArt(view.generation, entry.id, 'cover', 1);
    expect(art.mime).toBe('image/svg+xml');
    const updates = await b.setEntryArtFromSearch(view.generation, entry.id, 'cover', 1);
    expect(updates[0]!.art.cover).toBe('present');
    const id = await b.identifyEntry(view.generation, entry.id);
    expect(id).toMatchObject({ method: 'manual', canonical: 'Golden Sun (Europe)', confidence: 1 });
    await b.clearIdentityOverride(view.generation, entry.id);
    expect((await b.identifyEntry(view.generation, entry.id)).method).not.toBe('manual');
  });

  it('refuses empty, control-character and overlong queries', async () => {
    const b = createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW, storage: null });
    const view = await b.openLibrary(MOCK_LIBRARIES[1].path);
    for (const q of ['', '   ', 'a\u0007b', 'x'.repeat(121)]) {
      await expect(b.searchEntryArt(view.generation, 0, 'cover', q)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'bad-request' });
    }
    await expect(b.setEntryArtFromSearch(view.generation, 0, 'cover', 0)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'bad-request' });
  });
});
```

`src-tauri/tests/contract.rs` additions:

```rust
#[test]
fn settings_samples_match_rust() {
    let sample: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../src/ipc/contract.sample.json")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(coverly_core::settings::Settings::default()).unwrap(), sample["settings"]);
    assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(coverly_core::settings::RANGES).unwrap(), sample["ranges"]);
    let e = coverly_core::settings::FieldError {
        field: "network.timeout_secs".into(),
        code: coverly_core::settings::FieldErrorCode::Range,
        min: Some(5.0),
        max: Some(300.0),
    };
    assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(e).unwrap(), sample["field_error"]);
    let report = coverly_core::settings::LoadReport {
        notice: Some(coverly_core::settings::LoadNotice::Fixed),
        backup: Some("/config/settings.json.bak-1".into()),
        fixed: vec!["network.timeout_secs".into()],
    };
    let mut view = serde_json::to_value(coverly_roms_lib::prefs::SettingsView::new(Default::default(), &report)).unwrap();
    view.as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("settings");
    assert_eq!(view, sample["settings_view_meta"]);
    let store = coverly_core::credentials::MemoryStore::default();
    store.set(coverly_core::credentials::CredentialKey::SteamgriddbApiKey, "x").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(coverly_core::credentials::status(&store)).unwrap(), sample["credential_status"]);
    let info: coverly_core::profiles::custom::ProfileFile = serde_json::from_value(sample["profile_info"]["profile"].clone()).unwrap();
    let view = coverly_roms_lib::profiles::ProfileInfoView::of(&info.to_profile(), false);
    assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(view).unwrap(), sample["profile_info"]);
}

#[test]
fn settings_requests_deserialize() {
    let req: Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../src/ipc/contract.requests.json")).unwrap();
    let s = coverly_core::settings::Settings::default().with_patch(&req["settings_patch"], &[]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(s.network.timeout_secs, 60);
    for id in req["link_ids"].as_array().unwrap() {
        serde_json::from_value::<coverly_roms_lib::prefs::LinkId>(id.clone()).unwrap();
    }
    for k in req["credential_keys"].as_array().unwrap() {
        assert!(coverly_core::credentials::CredentialKey::parse(k.as_str().unwrap()).is_some());
    }
    serde_json::from_value::<coverly_core::profiles::custom::PreviewExample>(req["preview_example"].clone()).unwrap();
}
```
