- [ ] **Step 4: `settingsRules.ts` and `profileTemplate.ts`**

`src/lib/settingsRules.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Mirror of `coverly_core::settings` for the mock backend and for live
 * hints: defaults, ranges, normalisation, validation, merge patches. The
 * Rust core is the authority; `contract.test.ts` pins the defaults and
 * ranges to the shared sample.
 */
import {
  MEDIA,
  REGIONS,
  type FieldError,
  type FieldErrorCode,
  type Settings,
  type SettingsGroup,
  type SourceId,
} from '@/ipc/types';

export interface Range {
  field: string;
  min: number;
  max: number;
}

export const KNOWN_SOURCES: readonly SourceId[] = ['libretro', 'steamgriddb', 'screenscraper'];
export const AVAILABLE_SOURCES: readonly SourceId[] = ['libretro'];

export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: Settings = {
  version: 2,
  general: { language: 'auto', theme: 'system', check_updates_on_launch: true, update_channel: 'stable' },
  library: {
    default_profile: null,
    ignored_dirs: ['bios', 'saves', 'states', 'Shortcuts'],
    ignored_exts: ['txt', 'xml', 'json', 'cfg', 'ini', 'db', 'png', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'webp', 'bmp', 'srm', 'sav', 'state', 'pdf', 'md', 'nfo', 'log', 'dat'],
    include_hidden: false,
    follow_symlinks: false,
    recent: [],
  },
  art: { media: ['cover', 'screenshot', 'title'], single_slot_media: 'cover', output_format: 'profile', max_size: {}, keep_aspect: true, jpeg_quality: 90, overwrite: 'never' },
  matching: {
    strategy: 'rom-region',
    regions: ['rom', 'world', 'usa', 'europe', 'japan'],
    languages: ['en'],
    auto_threshold: 0.9,
    review_threshold: 0.65,
    fuzzy: true,
    arcade_translation: true,
    match_method: 'hash-then-name',
    max_hash_mb: 64,
  },
  sources: {
    order: [
      { id: 'libretro', enabled: true },
      { id: 'steamgriddb', enabled: false },
      { id: 'screenscraper', enabled: false },
    ],
  },
  network: { concurrency: 4, timeout_secs: 30, retries: 3, proxy: 'system', proxy_url: '', cache_max_mb: 2048, index_refresh_hours: 48 },
  safety: { backup_before_overwrite: true, backup_dir: null, dry_run_default: true, clean_apple_double: true, undo_depth: 10 },
  advanced: { log_level: 'info', custom_dir: null },
};

export const RANGES: readonly Range[] = [
  { field: 'art.jpeg_quality', min: 1, max: 100 },
  { field: 'art.max_size.width', min: 16, max: 4096 },
  { field: 'art.max_size.height', min: 16, max: 4096 },
  { field: 'matching.auto_threshold', min: 0.3, max: 1 },
  { field: 'matching.review_threshold', min: 0.3, max: 1 },
  { field: 'matching.max_hash_mb', min: 1, max: 4096 },
  { field: 'network.concurrency', min: 1, max: 6 },
  { field: 'network.timeout_secs', min: 5, max: 300 },
  { field: 'network.retries', min: 0, max: 10 },
  { field: 'network.cache_max_mb', min: 64, max: 102400 },
  { field: 'network.index_refresh_hours', min: 1, max: 720 },
  { field: 'safety.undo_depth', min: 1, max: 100 },
];

export const GROUPS: readonly SettingsGroup[] = ['general', 'library', 'art', 'matching', 'sources', 'network', 'safety', 'advanced'];

export function cloneSettings(s: Settings): Settings {
  return structuredClone(s);
}

export const rangeOf = (field: string) => RANGES.find((r) => r.field === field);

const isObject = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> =>
  typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);

/** RFC 7386 merge patch; returns a new value. */
export function mergePatch<T>(target: T, patch: unknown): T {
  if (!isObject(patch)) return structuredClone(patch) as T;
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = isObject(target) ? { ...target } : {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(patch)) {
    if (v === null) delete out[k];
    else out[k] = mergePatch(out[k], v);
  }
  return out as T;
}

/** Dotted paths of the leaves of a patch, in order. */
export function leafPaths(patch: unknown, prefix = ''): string[] {
  if (!isObject(patch) || Object.keys(patch).length === 0) return prefix ? [prefix] : [];
  return Object.entries(patch).flatMap(([k, v]) => leafPaths(v, prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k));
}

const err = (field: string, code: FieldErrorCode, r?: Range): FieldError => ({
  field,
  code,
  min: r ? r.min : null,
  max: r ? r.max : null,
});

function check(out: FieldError[], rule: string, field: string, value: number) {
  const r = rangeOf(rule)!;
  if (!(value >= r.min && value <= r.max)) out.push(err(field, 'range', r));
}

const isAbsolute = (p: string) => p.startsWith('/') || /^[A-Za-z]:[\\/]/.test(p);

export function validateSettings(s: Settings, known: readonly string[]): FieldError[] {
  const out: FieldError[] = [];
  const simple = (f: string, v: number) => check(out, f, f, v);
  if (s.library.default_profile !== null && !known.includes(s.library.default_profile)) {
    out.push(err('library.default_profile', 'unknown'));
  }
  if (s.art.media.length === 0) out.push(err('art.media', 'required'));
  for (const [id, size] of Object.entries(s.art.max_size)) {
    if (!known.includes(id)) {
      out.push(err(`art.max_size.${id}`, 'unknown'));
      continue;
    }
    check(out, 'art.max_size.width', `art.max_size.${id}.width`, size.width);
    check(out, 'art.max_size.height', `art.max_size.${id}.height`, size.height);
  }
  simple('art.jpeg_quality', s.art.jpeg_quality);
  const m = s.matching;
  const regions = m.regions.map((r) => String(r).trim().toLowerCase());
  if (regions.length === 0) out.push(err('matching.regions', 'required'));
  else if (!regions.every((r) => r === 'rom' || (REGIONS as readonly string[]).includes(r)))
    out.push(err('matching.regions', 'invalid'));
  else if (new Set(regions).size !== regions.length) out.push(err('matching.regions', 'duplicate'));
  if (m.languages.length > 10 || !m.languages.every((l) => /^[a-z]{2}$/.test(l)))
    out.push(err('matching.languages', 'invalid'));
  simple('matching.auto_threshold', m.auto_threshold);
  simple('matching.review_threshold', m.review_threshold);
  if (m.review_threshold > m.auto_threshold) out.push(err('matching.review_threshold', 'order'));
  simple('matching.max_hash_mb', m.max_hash_mb);
  const ids = s.sources.order.map((e) => e.id);
  if (!ids.every((id) => KNOWN_SOURCES.includes(id))) out.push(err('sources.order', 'unknown'));
  else if (new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) out.push(err('sources.order', 'duplicate'));
  else if (!s.sources.order.some((e) => e.enabled)) out.push(err('sources.order', 'required'));
  const n = s.network;
  simple('network.concurrency', n.concurrency);
  simple('network.timeout_secs', n.timeout_secs);
  simple('network.retries', n.retries);
  simple('network.cache_max_mb', n.cache_max_mb);
  simple('network.index_refresh_hours', n.index_refresh_hours);
  if (n.proxy === 'manual') {
    const url = n.proxy_url.trim();
    if (!url) out.push(err('network.proxy_url', 'required'));
    else if (!/^https?:\/\/\S+$/.test(url)) out.push(err('network.proxy_url', 'invalid'));
  }
  if (!s.safety.backup_before_overwrite) out.push(err('safety.backup_before_overwrite', 'invalid'));
  if (s.safety.backup_dir !== null && !isAbsolute(s.safety.backup_dir)) out.push(err('safety.backup_dir', 'not-absolute'));
  simple('safety.undo_depth', s.safety.undo_depth);
  if (s.advanced.custom_dir !== null && !isAbsolute(s.advanced.custom_dir)) out.push(err('advanced.custom_dir', 'not-absolute'));
  return out;
}

function uniq(list: string[]): string[] {
  return [...new Set(list)];
}

export function normaliseSettings(input: Settings): Settings {
  const s = cloneSettings(input);
  s.version = 2;
  const dirs: string[] = [];
  for (const raw of s.library.ignored_dirs) {
    const d = raw.trim();
    if (!d || d === '.' || d === '..' || /[\\/]/.test(d)) continue;
    if (dirs.some((o) => o.toLowerCase() === d.toLowerCase())) continue;
    dirs.push(d);
  }
  s.library.ignored_dirs = dirs;
  s.library.ignored_exts = uniq(
    s.library.ignored_exts
      .map((e) => e.trim().replace(/^\.+/, '').toLowerCase())
      .filter((e) => e.length > 0 && e.length <= 16 && /^[a-z0-9_-]+$/.test(e)),
  );
  s.library.recent = s.library.recent.slice(0, 10);
  s.art.media = MEDIA.filter((m) => s.art.media.includes(m));
  s.matching.regions = uniq(s.matching.regions.map((r) => String(r).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean)) as Settings['matching']['regions'];
  s.matching.languages = uniq(s.matching.languages.map((l) => l.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean));
  const order: Settings['sources']['order'] = [];
  for (const e of s.sources.order) {
    if (KNOWN_SOURCES.includes(e.id) && !order.some((o) => o.id === e.id)) {
      order.push({ id: e.id, enabled: e.enabled && AVAILABLE_SOURCES.includes(e.id) });
    }
  }
  for (const id of KNOWN_SOURCES) if (!order.some((o) => o.id === id)) order.push({ id, enabled: false });
  s.sources.order = order;
  s.network.proxy_url = s.network.proxy_url.trim();
  return s;
}

const sameKind = (a: unknown, b: unknown) =>
  Array.isArray(a) ? Array.isArray(b) : a === null || b === null ? true : typeof a === typeof b;

/** Stored JSON → settings, field by field (mock storage; Rust has its own). */
export function tolerantSettings(raw: unknown, known: readonly string[]): { settings: Settings; fixed: string[] } {
  const fixed: string[] = [];
  let s = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  if (!isObject(raw)) return { settings: s, fixed };
  for (const g of GROUPS) {
    const user = raw[g];
    if (user === undefined) continue;
    if (!isObject(user)) {
      fixed.push(g);
      continue;
    }
    const group = s[g] as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(user)) {
      if (!(k in group)) continue;
      if (sameKind(group[k], v)) group[k] = v;
      else fixed.push(`${g}.${k}`);
    }
  }
  s = normaliseSettings(s);
  for (const e of validateSettings(s, known)) {
    const [g, f] = e.field.split('.') as [SettingsGroup, string];
    const def = DEFAULT_SETTINGS[g] as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (e.code === 'order') {
      s.matching.auto_threshold = DEFAULT_SETTINGS.matching.auto_threshold;
      s.matching.review_threshold = DEFAULT_SETTINGS.matching.review_threshold;
    } else {
      (s[g] as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[f] = structuredClone(def[f]);
    }
    const key = `${g}.${f}`;
    if (!fixed.includes(key)) fixed.push(key);
  }
  return { settings: s, fixed };
}

export function needsReopen(a: Settings, b: Settings): boolean {
  const lib = (s: Settings) =>
    JSON.stringify([s.library.ignored_dirs, s.library.ignored_exts, s.library.include_hidden, s.library.follow_symlinks]);
  return (
    lib(a) !== lib(b) ||
    a.art.single_slot_media !== b.art.single_slot_media ||
    a.art.output_format !== b.art.output_format ||
    JSON.stringify(a.art.max_size) !== JSON.stringify(b.art.max_size) ||
    a.advanced.custom_dir !== b.advanced.custom_dir
  );
}
```

`src/lib/profileTemplate.ts`:

```ts
/** Mirror of `profiles::custom` for live hints and the mock backend. */
import type { FieldError, Media, PreviewExample, ProfileFile, ProfileInfo, SlotFile } from '@/ipc/types';
import { MEDIA } from '@/ipc/types';

export const TOKENS = ['{root}', '{system_dir}', '{rel_dir}', '{stem}', '{system_id}', '{libretro}'] as const;

export interface TemplateVars {
  root: string;
  system_dir: string;
  rel_dir: string;
  stem: string;
  system_id: string;
  libretro: string | null;
}

/** A few folders the preview knows (the app uses the full systems database). */
const PREVIEW_SYSTEMS: Record<string, { id: string; libretro: string }> = {
  sfc: { id: 'snes', libretro: 'Nintendo - Super Nintendo Entertainment System' },
  snes: { id: 'snes', libretro: 'Nintendo - Super Nintendo Entertainment System' },
  gba: { id: 'gba', libretro: 'Nintendo - Game Boy Advance' },
  gb: { id: 'gb', libretro: 'Nintendo - Game Boy' },
  fc: { id: 'nes', libretro: 'Nintendo - Nintendo Entertainment System' },
  nes: { id: 'nes', libretro: 'Nintendo - Nintendo Entertainment System' },
  md: { id: 'megadrive', libretro: 'Sega - Mega Drive - Genesis' },
  ps: { id: 'psx', libretro: 'Sony - PlayStation' },
};

export function renderTemplate(template: string, v: TemplateVars): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (const seg of template.split('/')) {
    if (!seg) continue;
    if (seg === '{root}' || seg === '{system_dir}' || seg === '{rel_dir}') {
      const value = seg === '{root}' ? v.root : seg === '{system_dir}' ? v.system_dir : v.rel_dir;
      parts.push(...value.split('/').filter(Boolean));
      continue;
    }
    const text = seg.replace(/\{(root|system_dir|rel_dir|stem|system_id|libretro)\}/g, (_, name: string) =>
      name === 'libretro' ? (v.libretro ?? '') : String(v[name as keyof TemplateVars] ?? ''),
    );
    if (text) parts.push(text);
  }
  return parts.join('/');
}

const plainName = (s: string) => {
  const t = s.trim();
  return t.length > 0 && t !== '.' && t !== '..' && !/[/\\:]/.test(t) && !/\p{Cc}/u.test(t);
};

function templateError(slot: SlotFile): FieldError['code'] | null {
  const s = slot.template.trim();
  if (!s) return 'required';
  const tokens = s.match(/\{[^}]*\}?/g) ?? [];
  if (tokens.some((t) => !(TOKENS as readonly string[]).includes(t))) return 'unknown';
  if (!(s.startsWith('{root}/') || s.startsWith('{system_dir}/')) || s.includes('\\') || s.includes('//')) return 'invalid';
  const segs = s.split('/');
  if (segs.some((x) => x === '.' || x === '..' || x === '')) return 'invalid';
  const last = segs[segs.length - 1] ?? '';
  if (!last.includes('{stem}')) return 'invalid';
  const extOk = slot.format === 'png' ? last.endsWith('.png') : last.endsWith('.jpg') || last.endsWith('.jpeg');
  return extOk ? null : 'invalid';
}

const fe = (field: string, code: FieldError['code'], min: number | null = null, max: number | null = null): FieldError => ({ field, code, min, max });

export function validateProfile(p: ProfileFile, builtinIds: readonly string[]): FieldError[] {
  const out: FieldError[] = [];
  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,39}$/.test(p.id)) out.push(fe('id', 'invalid'));
  else if (builtinIds.some((b) => b.toLowerCase() === p.id.toLowerCase())) out.push(fe('id', 'duplicate'));
  const name = p.name.trim();
  if (!name) out.push(fe('name', 'required'));
  else if ([...name].length > 60 || /\p{Cc}/u.test(name)) out.push(fe('name', 'invalid'));
  if (p.roms_root && (p.roms_root.startsWith('/') || !p.roms_root.split('/').every(plainName))) out.push(fe('roms_root', 'invalid'));
  if (!p.art_dirs.every(plainName)) out.push(fe('art_dirs', 'invalid'));
  const keys = Object.keys(p.slots);
  if (keys.length === 0) out.push(fe('slots', 'required'));
  for (const key of keys) {
    const slot = p.slots[key as Media];
    if (!(MEDIA as readonly string[]).includes(key) || !slot) {
      out.push(fe(`slots.${key}`, 'unknown'));
      continue;
    }
    const code = templateError(slot);
    if (code) out.push(fe(`slots.${key}.template`, code));
    for (const f of ['max_width', 'max_height'] as const) {
      const v = slot[f];
      if (v !== null && (v < 16 || v > 4096)) out.push(fe(`slots.${key}.${f}`, 'range', 16, 4096));
    }
  }
  const mirrors = Object.values(p.slots).map((s) => s!.template.includes('{rel_dir}'));
  if (mirrors.some(Boolean) && !mirrors.every(Boolean)) out.push(fe('slots', 'order'));
  return out;
}

export function previewProfile(p: ProfileFile, ex: PreviewExample): Partial<Record<Media, string>> {
  const folder = ex.system_folder.trim();
  const sys = PREVIEW_SYSTEMS[folder.toLowerCase()];
  const file = ex.file_name.trim();
  const dot = file.lastIndexOf('.');
  const stem = dot > 0 ? file.slice(0, dot) : file;
  const root = '__library__';
  const systemDir = [root, ...p.roms_root.split('/').filter(Boolean), folder].join('/');
  const out: Partial<Record<Media, string>> = {};
  for (const m of MEDIA) {
    const slot = p.slots[m];
    if (!slot || templateError(slot)) continue;
    const path = renderTemplate(slot.template, {
      root,
      system_dir: systemDir,
      rel_dir: ex.rel_dir.trim().replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, ''),
      stem,
      system_id: sys?.id ?? 'unknown',
      libretro: sys?.libretro ?? null,
    });
    out[m] = path.startsWith(`${root}/`) ? path.slice(root.length + 1) : path;
  }
  return out;
}

/** A built-in profile as an editable copy named `id`. */
export function profileFromBuiltin(info: ProfileInfo, id: string): ProfileFile {
  return { ...structuredClone(info.profile), id, name: `${info.profile.name} (copy)` };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The mock settings**

`src/ipc/mock/mockSettings.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Mock of the settings, profiles, credentials, folders, links and cache
 * commands. Persists to `localStorage` (when usable) so a reload keeps
 * them, like the app's settings file. Credential VALUES are never stored:
 * only whether each key is set.
 */
import type { Backend } from '../backend';
import { BackendError } from '../errors';
import {
  CREDENTIAL_KEYS,
  type CredentialKey,
  type CredentialStatus,
  type FieldError,
  type ProfileFile,
  type ProfileInfo,
  type Settings,
  type SettingsNotice,
} from '../types';
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, cloneSettings, mergePatch, needsReopen, normaliseSettings, tolerantSettings, validateSettings } from '@/lib/settingsRules';
import { previewProfile, validateProfile } from '@/lib/profileTemplate';

const KEYS = { settings: 'coverly.mock.settings', profiles: 'coverly.mock.profiles', credentials: 'coverly.mock.credentials' };

export type MockSettingsApi = Pick<
  Backend,
  | 'getSettings' | 'updateSettings' | 'resetSettings' | 'onSettingsChanged'
  | 'openConfigFolder' | 'openLogsFolder' | 'openLink' | 'cacheUsage' | 'clearCache'
  | 'listProfiles' | 'saveProfile' | 'deleteProfile' | 'previewProfile'
  | 'credentialStatus' | 'setCredential' | 'clearCredential'
> & { readonly opened: string[] };

function read(storage: Storage | null, key: string): unknown {
  if (!storage) return undefined;
  try {
    const raw = storage.getItem(key);
    return raw === null ? undefined : JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    return Symbol.for('corrupt');
  }
}

function write(storage: Storage | null, key: string, value: unknown) {
  try {
    storage?.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
  } catch {
    // Storage may be full or blocked: the session keeps working.
  }
}

export function createMockSettings(opts: { storage: Storage | null; builtins: ProfileInfo[] }): MockSettingsApi {
  const { storage, builtins } = opts;
  const opened: string[] = [];
  const listeners = new Set<(s: Settings) => void>();
  const builtinIds = builtins.map((b) => b.profile.id);
  const storedProfiles = read(storage, KEYS.profiles);
  let custom: ProfileFile[] = Array.isArray(storedProfiles)
    ? (storedProfiles as ProfileFile[]).filter((p) => p && typeof p === 'object' && validateProfile(p, builtinIds).length === 0)
    : [];
  const known = () => [...builtinIds, ...custom.map((p) => p.id)];
  const raw = read(storage, KEYS.settings);
  let notice: SettingsNotice | null = null;
  let settings: Settings;
  if (raw === Symbol.for('corrupt')) {
    settings = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    notice = 'reset-corrupt';
    try {
      storage?.removeItem(KEYS.settings);
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  } else if (raw === undefined) {
    settings = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  } else {
    const t = tolerantSettings(raw, known());
    settings = t.settings;
    if (t.fixed.length) notice = 'fixed';
  }
  const storedCreds = read(storage, KEYS.credentials);
  const creds: Record<CredentialKey, boolean> = Object.fromEntries(
    CREDENTIAL_KEYS.map((k) => [k, typeof storedCreds === 'object' && storedCreds !== null && (storedCreds as Record<string, unknown>)[k] === true]),
  ) as Record<CredentialKey, boolean>;
  let cacheBytes = 48 * 1024 * 1024;

  const save = () => write(storage, KEYS.settings, settings);
  const emit = () => listeners.forEach((cb) => cb(cloneSettings(settings)));
  const status = (): CredentialStatus => ({ available: true, set: { ...creds } });
  const infos = (): ProfileInfo[] => [
    ...builtins,
    ...custom.map((p) => ({ profile: structuredClone(p), builtin: false, mirror_subdirs: Object.values(p.slots).some((s) => s!.template.includes('{rel_dir}')) })),
  ];

  return {
    opened,
    getSettings: () => Promise.resolve({ settings: cloneSettings(settings), notice, backup: notice ? 'mock-storage' : null, fixed: [] }),
    async updateSettings(patch) {
      let next: Settings;
      try {
        next = mergePatch(settings, patch);
      } catch {
        return { settings: cloneSettings(settings), errors: [{ field: 'settings', code: 'invalid', min: null, max: null }], reopen: false };
      }
      const errors: FieldError[] = validateSettings(next, known());
      if (errors.length) return { settings: cloneSettings(settings), errors, reopen: false };
      next = normaliseSettings(next);
      const reopen = needsReopen(settings, next);
      settings = next;
      notice = null;
      save();
      emit();
      return { settings: cloneSettings(settings), errors: [], reopen };
    },
    async resetSettings() {
      const recent = settings.library.recent;
      settings = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
      settings.library.recent = recent;
      notice = null;
      save();
      emit();
      return { settings: cloneSettings(settings), notice: null, backup: null, fixed: [] };
    },
    onSettingsChanged(cb) {
      listeners.add(cb);
      return Promise.resolve(() => {
        listeners.delete(cb);
      });
    },
    async openConfigFolder() {
      opened.push('folder:config');
    },
    async openLogsFolder() {
      opened.push('folder:logs');
    },
    async openLink(link) {
      opened.push(`link:${link}`);
    },
    cacheUsage: () => Promise.resolve(cacheBytes),
    async clearCache() {
      const freed = cacheBytes;
      cacheBytes = 0;
      return freed;
    },
    listProfiles: () => Promise.resolve(infos()),
    async saveProfile(profile, replaces) {
      if (replaces !== null && !custom.some((c) => c.id === replaces)) throw new BackendError('bad-request', `no custom profile ${replaces}`);
      const errors = validateProfile(profile, builtinIds);
      if (custom.some((c) => c.id === profile.id && c.id !== replaces) && !errors.some((e) => e.field === 'id')) {
        errors.push({ field: 'id', code: 'duplicate', min: null, max: null });
      }
      if (errors.length) return { profiles: infos(), errors };
      custom = [...custom.filter((c) => c.id !== replaces && c.id !== profile.id), structuredClone(profile)].sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
      write(storage, KEYS.profiles, custom);
      if (replaces && replaces !== profile.id && settings.library.default_profile === replaces) {
        settings = { ...settings, library: { ...settings.library, default_profile: profile.id } };
        save();
      }
      return { profiles: infos(), errors: [] };
    },
    async deleteProfile(id) {
      if (builtinIds.includes(id)) throw new BackendError('bad-request', 'built-in profiles are read-only');
      if (!custom.some((c) => c.id === id)) throw new BackendError('unknown-profile', id);
      custom = custom.filter((c) => c.id !== id);
      write(storage, KEYS.profiles, custom);
      if (settings.library.default_profile === id) {
        settings = { ...settings, library: { ...settings.library, default_profile: null } };
        save();
      }
      return infos();
    },
    previewProfile: (profile, example) =>
      Promise.resolve({ paths: previewProfile(profile, example), errors: validateProfile(profile, builtinIds).filter((e) => e.field !== 'id') }),
    credentialStatus: () => Promise.resolve(status()),
    async setCredential(key, value) {
      if (!CREDENTIAL_KEYS.includes(key)) throw new BackendError('bad-request', key);
      const v = value.trim();
      if ([...v].length > 512 || /\p{Cc}/u.test(v)) throw new BackendError('bad-request', 'credential value');
      creds[key] = v.length > 0;
      write(storage, KEYS.credentials, creds);
      return status();
    },
    async clearCredential(key) {
      creds[key] = false;
      write(storage, KEYS.credentials, creds);
      return status();
    },
  };
}
```

`mockBackend.ts`: `MockOptions.storage?: Storage | null`; build the built-in `ProfileInfo[]` from a constant `MOCK_BUILTIN_PROFILES` (the eight built-ins with their real templates — copy them from `crates/coverly-core/src/profiles/builtin.rs`: e.g. `onion` = `{ id: 'onion', name: 'Onion OS', roms_root: 'Roms', art_dirs: ['Imgs'], gamelist: false, slots: { cover: { template: '{system_dir}/{rel_dir}/Imgs/{stem}.png', format: 'png', max_width: 250, max_height: 360 } } }`, `mirror_subdirs: true`; `batocera` with `gamelist: true`), then `...createMockSettings({ storage: opts.storage === undefined ? defaultStorage() : opts.storage, builtins })` where:

```ts
function defaultStorage(): Storage | null {
  try {
    return typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.localStorage ? window.localStorage : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
```

The existing tests that create the mock without `storage` now use jsdom's `localStorage`: add `localStorage.clear()` to `src/test/setup.ts` in a global `beforeEach` (import from vitest) so tests stay independent.

`identifyEntry` in the mock: if `jobs.manualIdentity(id)` returns a name → `{ canonical: name, method: 'manual', confidence: 1, title: mockTitle(name), regions: [], offline: false }`.

```ts
import { applyTheme, resolveTheme } from './theme';

describe('theme', () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme');
    document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');

  });

    expect(resolveTheme('system', false)).toBe('light');

    expect(resolveTheme('dark', false)).toBe('dark');
  });
```

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { setBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { FIXTURE_NOW } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';
import { createMockBackend } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import { MemoryStorage } from '@/test/storage';

import { resetSettingsStore, useSettingsStore } from './settingsStore';

describe('settings store', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    setBackend(createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW, storage: new MemoryStorage() }));
    resetSettingsStore();
  });
  afterEach(() => setBackend(null));

  it('loads, saves and keeps field errors per field', async () => {
    const s = useSettingsStore.getState();
    await s.load();
    expect(useSettingsStore.getState().status).toBe('ready');
    const bad = await s.update({ network: { timeout_secs: 1 } });
    expect(bad).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(useSettingsStore.getState().errors['network.timeout_secs']?.code).toBe('range');
    expect(useSettingsStore.getState().settings!.network.timeout_secs).toBe(30);
    expect(await s.update({ network: { retries: 5 } })).toEqual([]);
    expect(useSettingsStore.getState().errors['network.timeout_secs']).toBeDefined();
    expect(await s.update({ network: { timeout_secs: 40 } })).toEqual([]);
    expect(useSettingsStore.getState().errors['network.timeout_secs']).toBeUndefined();
    expect(useSettingsStore.getState().settings!.network).toMatchObject({ retries: 5, timeout_secs: 40 });
  });

    await s.load();
    await s.update({ library: { include_hidden: true } });
    expect(useSettingsStore.getState().reopen).toBe(true);
    await s.reset();
    expect(useSettingsStore.getState().settings!.library.include_hidden).toBe(false);
  });

  it('moves the old language choice into the settings once', async () => {
    localStorage.setItem('coverly.locale', 'es');
    await useSettingsStore.getState().load();
    expect(useSettingsStore.getState().settings!.general.language).toBe('es');
    expect(localStorage.getItem('coverly.locale')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```tsx
    expect(backend.opened).toEqual(['link:license', 'link:repo']);
  });
});
```

`src/lib/theme.ts`:

```ts
import type { ThemeChoice } from '@/ipc/types';

export type ResolvedTheme = 'light' | 'dark';

export function resolveTheme(choice: ThemeChoice, prefersDark: boolean): ResolvedTheme {
  return choice === 'system' ? (prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light') : choice;
}

/** Marks `<html>` (`data-theme`, `.dark`, `color-scheme`); for `system`,
 * follows the OS until the returned cleanup runs. */
export function applyTheme(choice: ThemeChoice, root: HTMLElement = document.documentElement): () => void {
  const mq =
    typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.matchMedia === 'function'
      ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)')
      : null;
  const set = () => {
    const resolved = resolveTheme(choice, mq?.matches ?? false);
    root.dataset.theme = resolved;
    root.classList.toggle('dark', resolved === 'dark');
    root.style.colorScheme = resolved;
  };
  set();
  if (choice !== 'system' || !mq) return () => undefined;
  mq.addEventListener('change', set);
  return () => mq.removeEventListener('change', set);
}
```

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';

export type SettingsSection = 'general' | 'library' | 'art' | 'matching' | 'sources' | 'network' | 'safety' | 'advanced' | 'profiles' | 'about';
export const SETTINGS_SECTIONS: readonly SettingsSection[] = ['general', 'library', 'art', 'matching', 'sources', 'network', 'safety', 'advanced', 'profiles', 'about'];

interface NavState {
  screen: 'main' | 'settings';
  section: SettingsSection;
  openSettings(section?: SettingsSection): void;
  closeSettings(): void;
  setSection(section: SettingsSection): void;
}

export const useNavStore = create<NavState>()((set) => ({
  screen: 'main',
  section: 'general',
  openSettings: (section) => set((s) => ({ screen: 'settings', section: section ?? s.section })),
  closeSettings: () => set({ screen: 'main' }),
  setSection: (section) => set({ section }),
}));
```

`src/stores/settingsStore.ts`:

```ts
/** The app settings (spec §9) as the UI sees them: loaded once, updated
 * per field with the backend's validation errors kept per field. */
import { create } from 'zustand';

import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { toBackendError, type BackendError } from '@/ipc/errors';
import type { FieldError, Settings, SettingsNotice, SettingsPatch } from '@/ipc/types';
import { leafPaths } from '@/lib/settingsRules';

const LEGACY_LOCALE = 'coverly.locale';

interface SettingsState {
  status: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error';
  settings: Settings | null;
  notice: SettingsNotice | null;
  backup: string | null;
  fixed: string[];
  errors: Record<string, FieldError>;
  reopen: boolean;
  saved: number;
  error: BackendError | null;
  load(): Promise<void>;
  update(patch: SettingsPatch): Promise<FieldError[]>;
  reset(): Promise<void>;
  dismissNotice(): void;
  dismissReopen(): void;
  clearErrors(prefix: string): void;
}

const INITIAL = {
  status: 'idle' as const,
  settings: null,
  notice: null,
  backup: null,
  fixed: [],
  errors: {},
  reopen: false,
  saved: 0,
  error: null,
};

function takeLegacyLocale(): 'en' | 'es' | null {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_LOCALE);
    localStorage.removeItem(LEGACY_LOCALE);
    return v === 'en' || v === 'es' ? v : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export const useSettingsStore = create<SettingsState>()((set, get) => ({
  ...INITIAL,
  async load() {
    set({ status: 'loading', error: null });
    try {
      const b = await getBackend();
      const view = await b.getSettings();
      set({ status: 'ready', settings: view.settings, notice: view.notice, backup: view.backup, fixed: view.fixed });
      const legacy = takeLegacyLocale();
      if (legacy && view.settings.general.language === 'auto') {
        await get().update({ general: { language: legacy } });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      set({ status: 'error', error: toBackendError(e) });
    }
  },
  async update(patch) {
    const b = await getBackend();
    const out = await b.updateSettings(patch);
    const paths = leafPaths(patch);
    set((s) => {
      const errors = { ...s.errors };
      for (const key of Object.keys(errors)) {
        if (paths.some((p) => key === p || key.startsWith(`${p}.`))) delete errors[key];
      }
      for (const e of out.errors) errors[e.field] = e;
      return out.errors.length
        ? { errors }
        : { errors, settings: out.settings, reopen: s.reopen || out.reopen, notice: null, saved: s.saved + 1 };
    });
    return out.errors;
  },
  async reset() {
    const b = await getBackend();
    const view = await b.resetSettings();
    set({ settings: view.settings, notice: null, errors: {}, reopen: true, saved: get().saved + 1 });
  },
  dismissNotice: () => set({ notice: null }),
  dismissReopen: () => set({ reopen: false }),
  clearErrors: (prefix) =>
    set((s) => ({
      errors: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(s.errors).filter(([k]) => k !== prefix && !k.startsWith(`${prefix}.`))),
    })),
}));

/** Tests only. */
export function resetSettingsStore(): void {
  useSettingsStore.setState({ ...INITIAL });
}
```

`src/Root.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react';

import App from './App';
import { ErrorBoundary } from './components/ErrorBoundary';
import { I18nProvider } from './i18n/I18nProvider';
import { applyTheme } from './lib/theme';
import { useSettingsStore } from './stores/settingsStore';

/** The app inside the i18n provider, following the language and theme settings. */
export function Root() {
  const language = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings?.general.language ?? 'auto');
  const theme = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings?.general.theme ?? 'system');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (useSettingsStore.getState().status === 'idle') void useSettingsStore.getState().load();
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => applyTheme(theme), [theme]);
  return (
    <I18nProvider {...(language === 'auto' ? {} : { locale: language })}>
      <ErrorBoundary>
        <App />
      </ErrorBoundary>
    </I18nProvider>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { LibraryScreen } from './screens/LibraryScreen';
import { SettingsScreen } from './screens/SettingsScreen';
import { StartScreen } from './screens/StartScreen';
import { useLibraryStore } from './stores/libraryStore';
import { useNavStore } from './stores/navStore';

export default function App() {
  const status = useLibraryStore((s) => s.status);
  const screen = useNavStore((s) => s.screen);
  if (screen === 'settings') return <SettingsScreen />;
  return status === 'ready' ? <LibraryScreen /> : <StartScreen />;
}
```

`src/components/settings/Field.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import type { FieldError, SettingsPatch } from '@/ipc/types';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';

type T = ReturnType<typeof useT>;

export const settingId = (path: string) => `setting-${path.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]+/g, '-')}`;

export function fieldErrorText(t: T, e: FieldError): string {
  return t(`errors.field.${e.code}`, { min: e.min ?? '', max: e.max ?? '' });
}

export function useFieldError(path: string): string | null {
  const t = useT();
  const e = useSettingsStore((s) => s.errors[path]);
  return e ? fieldErrorText(t, e) : null;
}

export function Field({ id, label, hint, error, children }: { id: string; label: string; hint?: string; error?: string | null; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="setting">
      <label htmlFor={id} className="setting-label">
        {label}
      </label>
      {children}
      {hint && (
        <p id={`${id}-hint`} className="hint">
          {hint}
        </p>
      )}
      {error && (
        <p id={`${id}-error`} role="alert" className="field-error">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/** A number committed on blur or Enter; the typed text stays when the
 * backend refuses it, with the error under the field. */
export function NumberSetting({
  path,
  label,
  hint,
  value,
  step = 1,
  scale = 1,
  toPatch,
}: {
  path: string;
  label: string;
  hint?: string;
  value: number;
  step?: number;
  /** Shown value = stored value × scale (e.g. 100 for a percentage). */
  scale?: number;
  toPatch(stored: number): SettingsPatch;
}) {
  const t = useT();
  const id = settingId(path);
  const update = useSettingsStore((s) => s.update);
  const remote = useFieldError(path);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [local, setLocal] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const error = local ?? remote;
  async function commit() {
    if (draft === null) return;
    const n = Number(draft.trim().replace(',', '.'));
    if (draft.trim() === '' || !Number.isFinite(n)) {
      setLocal(t('errors.field.invalid'));
      return;
    }
    setLocal(null);
    const errors = await update(toPatch(Math.round((n / scale) * 1000) / 1000));
    if (errors.length === 0) setDraft(null);
  }
  return (
    <Field id={id} label={label} {...(hint ? { hint } : {})} error={error}>
      <input
        id={id}
        name={id}
        type="text"
        inputMode="decimal"
        className="text-input setting-number"
        value={draft ?? String(Math.round(value * scale * 100) / 100)}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={[hint ? `${id}-hint` : '', error ? `${id}-error` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || undefined}
        onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
        onBlur={() => void commit()}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter') void commit();
        }}
      />
    </Field>
  );
}
```

> `step` is accepted for future use (a text input avoids jsdom/locale number quirks); drop it if lint flags it as unused.

`src/screens/SettingsScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';
import { SETTINGS_SECTIONS, useNavStore, type SettingsSection } from '@/stores/navStore';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';
import { AboutSection } from '@/components/settings/AboutSection';
import { GeneralSection } from '@/components/settings/GeneralSection';
import { LibrarySection } from '@/components/settings/LibrarySection';

function Pending() {
  const t = useT();
  return <p className="hint">{t('settings.loading')}</p>;
}

/** Each section's panel (Tasks 13–14 replace the pending ones). */
export const SECTION_COMPONENTS: Record<SettingsSection, () => React.JSX.Element> = {
  general: GeneralSection,
  library: LibrarySection,
  art: Pending,
  matching: Pending,
  sources: Pending,
  network: Pending,
  safety: Pending,
  advanced: Pending,
  profiles: Pending,
  about: AboutSection,
};

export function SettingsScreen() {
  const t = useT();
  const section = useNavStore((s) => s.section);
  const setSection = useNavStore((s) => s.setSection);
  const close = useNavStore((s) => s.closeSettings);
  const status = useSettingsStore((s) => s.status);
  const notice = useSettingsStore((s) => s.notice);
  const backup = useSettingsStore((s) => s.backup);
  const fixed = useSettingsStore((s) => s.fixed);
  const reopen = useSettingsStore((s) => s.reopen);
  const dismissNotice = useSettingsStore((s) => s.dismissNotice);
  const dismissReopen = useSettingsStore((s) => s.dismissReopen);
  const libraryPath = useLibraryStore((s) => (s.status === 'ready' ? s.path : null));
  const Panel = SECTION_COMPONENTS[section];

  async function reopenNow() {
    if (!libraryPath) return;
    await useLibraryStore.getState().open(libraryPath);
    dismissReopen();
  }

  return (
    <main className="settings-screen">
      <header className="settings-header">
        <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={close}>
          {t('settings.back')}
        </button>
        <h1>{t('settings.title')}</h1>
      </header>
      {notice && (
        <div role="status" className="notice-banner">
          <p>{t(`settings.notice.${notice}`, { backup: backup ?? '—', fields: fixed.join(', ') || '—' })}</p>
          <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={dismissNotice}>
            {t('settings.dismiss')}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      {reopen && libraryPath && (
        <div role="status" className="notice-banner">
          <p>{t('settings.reopen')}</p>
          <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => void reopenNow()}>
            {t('settings.reopenNow')}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="settings-body">
        <nav className="settings-nav">
          <div role="tablist" aria-label={t('settings.sections')} aria-orientation="vertical">
            {SETTINGS_SECTIONS.map((s) => (
              <button
                key={s}
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                id={`settings-tab-${s}`}
                aria-selected={s === section}
                aria-controls={`settings-panel-${s}`}
                tabIndex={s === section ? 0 : -1}
                className="settings-tab"
                onClick={() => setSection(s)}
              >
                {t(`settings.section.${s}`)}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </nav>
        <section role="tabpanel" id={`settings-panel-${section}`} aria-labelledby={`settings-tab-${section}`} className="settings-panel">
          {status === 'ready' || section === 'about' ? <Panel /> : <p role="status">{t('settings.loading')}</p>}
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

(Arrow-key navigation between tabs: Up/Down move the selection, as in WAI-ARIA tabs; add an `onKeyDown` on the tablist.)

`src/components/settings/GeneralSection.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import type { LanguageChoice, ThemeChoice, UpdateChannel } from '@/ipc/types';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';

import { Field, settingId } from './Field';

const THEMES: readonly ThemeChoice[] = ['system', 'light', 'dark'];

export function GeneralSection() {
  const t = useT();
  const g = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings!.general);
  const update = useSettingsStore((s) => s.update);
  return (
    <div className="settings-group">
      <Field id={settingId('general.language')} label={t('settings.general.language')}>
        <select
          id={settingId('general.language')}
          name="language"
          className="select"
          value={g.language}
          onChange={(e) => void update({ general: { language: e.target.value as LanguageChoice } })}
        >
          {(['auto', 'en', 'es'] as const).map((l) => (
            <option key={l} value={l}>
              {t(`language.${l}`)}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <fieldset className="setting">
        <legend className="setting-label">{t('settings.general.theme')}</legend>
        {THEMES.map((th) => (
          <label key={th} className="radio-row">
            <input type="radio" name="theme" checked={g.theme === th} onChange={() => void update({ general: { theme: th } })} />
            {t(`settings.general.themes.${th}`)}
          </label>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          name="check-updates"
          checked={g.check_updates_on_launch}
          onChange={(e) => void update({ general: { check_updates_on_launch: e.target.checked } })}
        />
        {t('settings.general.checkUpdates')}
      </label>
      <Field id={settingId('general.update_channel')} label={t('settings.general.channel')} hint={t('settings.general.updatesLater')}>
        <select
          id={settingId('general.update_channel')}
          name="update-channel"
          className="select"
          value={g.update_channel}
          onChange={(e) => void update({ general: { update_channel: e.target.value as UpdateChannel } })}
        >
          {(['stable', 'beta'] as const).map((c) => (
            <option key={c} value={c}>
              {t(`settings.general.channels.${c}`)}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Field>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/settings/LibrarySection.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import type { ProfileInfo } from '@/ipc/types';
import { useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';

import { Field, settingId, useFieldError } from './Field';

/** A list edited as text, committed on blur. */
function ListSetting({ path, label, hint, value, split, join, toPatch }: {
  path: string; label: string; hint: string; value: string[];
  split(text: string): string[]; join(list: string[]): string;
  toPatch(list: string[]): Parameters<ReturnType<typeof useSettingsStore.getState>['update']>[0];
}) {
  const id = settingId(path);
  const update = useSettingsStore((s) => s.update);
  const error = useFieldError(path);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<string | null>(null);
  return (
    <Field id={id} label={label} hint={hint} error={error}>
      <textarea
        id={id}
        name={id}
        className="text-input"
        rows={4}
        value={draft ?? join(value)}
        onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
        onBlur={async () => {
          if (draft === null) return;
          const errors = await update(toPatch(split(draft)));
          if (errors.length === 0) setDraft(null);
        }}
      />
    </Field>
  );
}

export function LibrarySection() {
  const t = useT();
  const lib = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings!.library);
  const update = useSettingsStore((s) => s.update);
  const recents = useLibraryStore((s) => s.recents);
  const forget = useLibraryStore((s) => s.forget);
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<ProfileInfo[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void getBackend()
      .then((b) => b.listProfiles())
      .then((p) => alive && setProfiles(p), () => undefined);
    void useLibraryStore.getState().loadRecents();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);
  const lines = (s: string) => s.split('\n').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const commas = (s: string) => s.split(',').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return (
    <div className="settings-group">
      <Field id={settingId('library.default_profile')} label={t('settings.library.defaultProfile')} error={useFieldError('library.default_profile')}>
        <select
          id={settingId('library.default_profile')}
          name="default-profile"
          className="select"
          value={lib.default_profile ?? ''}
          onChange={(e) => void update({ library: { default_profile: e.target.value || null } })}
        >
          <option value="">{t('settings.library.defaultProfileAuto')}</option>
          {profiles.map((p) => (
            <option key={p.profile.id} value={p.profile.id}>
              {p.profile.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <ListSetting
        path="library.ignored_dirs"
        label={t('settings.library.ignoredDirs')}
        hint={t('settings.library.ignoredDirsHint')}
        value={lib.ignored_dirs}
        split={lines}
        join={(l) => l.join('\n')}
        toPatch={(l) => ({ library: { ignored_dirs: l } })}
      />
      <ListSetting
        path="library.ignored_exts"
        label={t('settings.library.ignoredExts')}
        hint={t('settings.library.ignoredExtsHint')}
        value={lib.ignored_exts}
        split={commas}
        join={(l) => l.join(', ')}
        toPatch={(l) => ({ library: { ignored_exts: l } })}
      />
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input type="checkbox" name="include-hidden" checked={lib.include_hidden} onChange={(e) => void update({ library: { include_hidden: e.target.checked } })} />
        {t('settings.library.includeHidden')}
      </label>
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input type="checkbox" name="follow-symlinks" checked={lib.follow_symlinks} onChange={(e) => void update({ library: { follow_symlinks: e.target.checked } })} />
        {t('settings.library.followSymlinks')}
      </label>
      <h2 className="section-title">{t('settings.library.recent')}</h2>
      {recents.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="hint">{t('settings.library.recentEmpty')}</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="recent-list">
          {recents.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.path}>
              <span className="truncate" title={r.path}>{r.name}</span>
              <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => void forget(r.path)}>
                {t('settings.library.forget')}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/settings/AboutSection.tsx` (Pluryprint's About pattern, without the updater):

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { fetchAppInfo, type AppInfo } from '@/ipc/appInfo';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import type { LinkId } from '@/ipc/types';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';

const LINKS: readonly LinkId[] = ['repo', 'license', 'notices', 'trademarks', 'releases', 'issues'];

export function AboutSection() {
  const t = useT();
  const [info, setInfo] = useState<AppInfo | null>(null);
  const channel = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings?.general.update_channel ?? 'stable');
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void fetchAppInfo().then((i) => alive && setInfo(i));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);
  return (
    <div className="settings-group">
      <h2 className="wordmark-small">Coverly ROMs</h2>
      <p>{t('footer.version', { version: info?.version ?? '—' })}</p>
      <p>{t('footer.coreVersion', { version: info?.core_version ?? '—' })}</p>
      <p>{t('about.license')}</p>
      <p className="hint">{t('about.noTelemetry')}</p>
      <p className="hint">{t('about.updates', { channel: t(`settings.general.channels.${channel}`) })}</p>
      <div className="about-links">
        {LINKS.map((l) => (
          <button key={l} type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => void getBackend().then((b) => b.openLink(l))}>
            {t(`about.link.${l}`)}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { act, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { Root } from '@/Root';
import { setBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { FIXTURE_NOW } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';
import { createMockBackend, type MockBackend } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import { resetLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';
import { useNavStore, type SettingsSection } from '@/stores/navStore';
import { resetSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';
import { stubLayout } from '@/test/layout';
import { MemoryStorage } from '@/test/storage';

let backend: MockBackend;
let restore: () => void;

async function open(section: SettingsSection) {
  render(<Root />);
  await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Settings' });
  act(() => useNavStore.getState().openSettings(section));
  return screen.findByRole('tabpanel');
}

const saved = async () => (await backend.getSettings()).settings;

describe('settings sections', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    backend = createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW, storage: new MemoryStorage() });
    setBackend(backend);
    resetSettingsStore();
    resetLibraryStore();
    restore = stubLayout(1280, 800);
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    restore();
    setBackend(null);
  });

  it('art: media, single slot, format, aspect, quality, overwrite and a per-profile size', async () => {
    await open('art');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Title screen' }));
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Image for single-slot layouts' }), 'screenshot');
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Output format' }), 'jpg');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Keep the aspect ratio' }));
    const quality = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'JPEG quality' });
    await userEvent.clear(quality);
    await userEvent.type(quality, '75{enter}');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Ask every time' }));
    const width = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Onion OS: maximum width' });
    const height = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Onion OS: maximum height' });
    await userEvent.type(width, '300');
    await userEvent.type(height, '420{enter}');
    const s = await saved();
    expect(s.art).toMatchObject({
      media: ['cover', 'screenshot'],
      single_slot_media: 'screenshot',
      output_format: 'jpg',
      keep_aspect: false,
      jpeg_quality: 75,
      overwrite: 'ask',
    });
    expect(s.art.max_size['onion']).toEqual({ width: 300, height: 420 });
  });

  it('matching: strategy, region order, languages, thresholds and toggles', async () => {
    await open('matching');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Source priority' }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Move USA up' }));
    const langs = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Preferred languages' });
    await userEvent.clear(langs);
    await userEvent.type(langs, 'es, en');
    await userEvent.tab();
    const auto = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Auto-accept threshold (%)' });
    await userEvent.clear(auto);
    await userEvent.type(auto, '95{enter}');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Fuzzy name matching' }));
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'How ROMs are identified' }), 'name-only');
    const s = await saved();
    expect(s.matching).toMatchObject({
      strategy: 'priority',
      regions: ['rom', 'usa', 'world', 'europe', 'japan'],
      languages: ['es', 'en'],
      auto_threshold: 0.95,
      fuzzy: false,
      match_method: 'name-only',
    });
  });

  it('matching: a review threshold above the auto one is an error next to the field', async () => {
    await open('matching');
    const review = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Review threshold (%)' });
    await userEvent.clear(review);
    await userEvent.type(review, '99{enter}');
    expect(await screen.findByText('This must not be higher than the auto-accept threshold.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(review).toHaveValue('99');
    expect((await saved()).matching.review_threshold).toBe(0.65);
  });

  it('sources: later sources are shown as coming soon and credentials are stored without coming back', async () => {
    const panel = await open('sources');
    const sgdb = within(panel).getByRole('checkbox', { name: /SteamGridDB/ });
    expect(sgdb).toBeDisabled();
    expect(within(panel).getAllByText('Coming in a later version').length).toBe(2);
    const key = within(panel).getByLabelText('SteamGridDB API key');
    expect(key).toHaveAttribute('type', 'password');
    await userEvent.type(key, 'sk-SECRET');
    await userEvent.click(within(panel).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save SteamGridDB API key' }));
    expect(await within(panel).findByText('Saved in the system keychain.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(key).toHaveValue('');
    expect(document.body.innerHTML).not.toContain('sk-SECRET');
    await userEvent.click(within(panel).getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove SteamGridDB API key' }));
    expect((await backend.credentialStatus()).set['steamgriddb-api-key']).toBe(false);
  });

  it('network: invalid timeout shows an error, keeps the typed value and other fields still save', async () => {
    await open('network');
    const timeout = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Timeout (seconds)' });
    await userEvent.clear(timeout);
    await userEvent.type(timeout, '1{enter}');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Use a value from 5 to 300.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(timeout).toHaveValue('1');
    const retries = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Retries' });
    await userEvent.clear(retries);
    await userEvent.type(retries, '5{enter}');
    expect((await saved()).network).toMatchObject({ retries: 5, timeout_secs: 30 });
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Manual' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('This cannot be empty.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const url = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Proxy URL' });
    await userEvent.type(url, 'http://proxy.local:3128');
    await userEvent.tab();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Manual' }));
    expect((await saved()).network).toMatchObject({ proxy: 'manual', proxy_url: 'http://proxy.local:3128' });
  });

  it('network: clears the cache after a confirmation', async () => {
    await open('network');
    expect(await screen.findByText(/In use: 48/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Clear cache…' }));
    await userEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Clear cache' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Freed 48/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await backend.cacheUsage()).toBe(0);
  });

  it('safety: backups are locked on; the rest saves', async () => {
    await open('safety');
    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Back up files before replacing them' })).toBeDisabled();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Simulate before writing (dry run first)' }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Delete macOS ._ files on removable drives' }));
    const depth = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Jobs kept for undo' });
    await userEvent.clear(depth);
    await userEvent.type(depth, '20{enter}');
    const folder = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Backup folder' });
    await userEvent.type(folder, 'relative/path');
    await userEvent.tab();
    expect(await screen.findByText('Use a full path (starting with / or a drive letter).')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect((await saved()).safety).toMatchObject({ dry_run_default: false, clean_apple_double: false, undo_depth: 20, backup_dir: null });
  });

  it('advanced: log level, folders and reset with confirmation', async () => {
    await open('advanced');
    await userEvent.selectOptions(screen.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Log level' }), 'debug');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open logs folder' }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open settings folder' }));
    expect(backend.opened).toEqual(['folder:logs', 'folder:config']);
    expect((await saved()).advanced.log_level).toBe('debug');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reset all settings…' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog');
    await userEvent.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    expect((await saved()).advanced.log_level).toBe('debug');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reset all settings…' }));
    await userEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Reset settings' }));
    expect((await saved()).advanced.log_level).toBe('info');
  });
});
```
