- [ ] **Step 3: Write the sections**

Every control follows Task 12's pattern: selects, radios and checkboxes call `update(…)` on change; numbers use `NumberSetting`; text and list fields commit on blur; errors come from `useFieldError(path)`.

`ArtSection.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { MEDIA, type OutputFormat, type OverwriteSetting, type ProfileInfo } from '@/ipc/types';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';

import { Field, NumberSetting, settingId, useFieldError } from './Field';

function SizeRow({ info }: { info: ProfileInfo }) {
  const t = useT();
  const id = info.profile.id;
  const art = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings!.art);
  const update = useSettingsStore((s) => s.update);
  const cur = art.max_size[id];
  const slot = info.profile.slots.cover ?? Object.values(info.profile.slots)[0];
  const [w, setW] = useState(cur ? String(cur.width) : '');
  const [h, setH] = useState(cur ? String(cur.height) : '');
  const errW = useFieldError(`art.max_size.${id}.width`);
  const errH = useFieldError(`art.max_size.${id}.height`);
  async function commit() {
    const next = { ...art.max_size };
    if (w.trim() === '' && h.trim() === '') delete next[id];
    else next[id] = { width: Number(w), height: Number(h) };
    await update({ art: { max_size: next } });
  }
  const placeholder = slot?.max_width ? `${slot.max_width}` : t('settings.art.noLimit');
  return (
    <tr>
      <th scope="row">{info.profile.name}</th>
      {(['width', 'height'] as const).map((dim) => (
        <td key={dim}>
          <input
            name={`max-${dim}-${id}`}
            className="text-input setting-number"
            inputMode="numeric"
            aria-label={t(`settings.art.max${dim === 'width' ? 'Width' : 'Height'}`, { profile: info.profile.name })}
            placeholder={dim === 'width' ? placeholder : slot?.max_height ? `${slot.max_height}` : t('settings.art.noLimit')}
            value={dim === 'width' ? w : h}
            onChange={(e) => (dim === 'width' ? setW : setH)(e.target.value)}
            onBlur={() => void commit()}
            onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && void commit()}
          />
        </td>
      ))}
      <td>{(errW ?? errH) && <span role="alert" className="field-error">{errW ?? errH}</span>}</td>
    </tr>
  );
}

export function ArtSection() {
  const t = useT();
  const art = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings!.art);
  const update = useSettingsStore((s) => s.update);
  const mediaError = useFieldError('art.media');
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<ProfileInfo[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void getBackend().then((b) => b.listProfiles()).then((p) => alive && setProfiles(p), () => undefined);
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);
  return (
    <div className="settings-group">
      <fieldset className="setting">
        <legend className="setting-label">{t('settings.art.media')}</legend>
        {MEDIA.map((m) => (
          <label key={m} className="checkbox-row">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              name={`media-${m}`}
              checked={art.media.includes(m)}
              onChange={(e) =>
                void update({ art: { media: e.target.checked ? [...art.media, m] : art.media.filter((x) => x !== m) } })
              }
            />
            {t(`art.media.${m}`)}
          </label>
        ))}
        {mediaError && <p role="alert" className="field-error">{mediaError}</p>}
      </fieldset>
      <Field id={settingId('art.single_slot_media')} label={t('settings.art.singleSlot')} hint={t('settings.art.singleSlotHint')}>
        <select id={settingId('art.single_slot_media')} name="single-slot" className="select" value={art.single_slot_media}
          onChange={(e) => void update({ art: { single_slot_media: e.target.value as (typeof MEDIA)[number] } })}>
          {MEDIA.map((m) => <option key={m} value={m}>{t(`art.media.${m}`)}</option>)}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <Field id={settingId('art.output_format')} label={t('settings.art.format')} hint={t('settings.art.formatHint')}>
        <select id={settingId('art.output_format')} name="output-format" className="select" value={art.output_format}
          onChange={(e) => void update({ art: { output_format: e.target.value as OutputFormat } })}>
          {(['profile', 'png', 'jpg'] as const).map((f) => <option key={f} value={f}>{t(`settings.art.formats.${f}`)}</option>)}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input type="checkbox" name="keep-aspect" checked={art.keep_aspect} onChange={(e) => void update({ art: { keep_aspect: e.target.checked } })} />
        {t('settings.art.keepAspect')}
      </label>
      <NumberSetting path="art.jpeg_quality" label={t('settings.art.jpegQuality')} value={art.jpeg_quality} toPatch={(v) => ({ art: { jpeg_quality: v } })} />
      <fieldset className="setting">
        <legend className="setting-label">{t('settings.art.overwrite')}</legend>
        {(['never', 'ask', 'backup'] as const satisfies readonly OverwriteSetting[]).map((o) => (
          <label key={o} className="radio-row">
            <input type="radio" name="overwrite" checked={art.overwrite === o} onChange={() => void update({ art: { overwrite: o } })} />
            {t(`settings.art.overwrites.${o}`)}
          </label>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      <h2 className="section-title">{t('settings.art.maxSize')}</h2>
      <p className="hint">{t('settings.art.maxSizeHint')}</p>
      <table className="settings-table">
        <thead>
          <tr><th scope="col">{t('settings.art.profile')}</th><th scope="col">{t('settings.art.width')}</th><th scope="col">{t('settings.art.height')}</th><th /></tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>{profiles.map((p) => <SizeRow key={p.profile.id} info={p} />)}</tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`MatchingSection.tsx` (strategy labels reuse M4's `fill.strategy.<s>`; the label holds the name only and the hint `fill.strategyHint.<s>` is a separate `<p>`, so the radio's accessible name stays exact):

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { REGIONS, STRATEGIES, type MatchMethod, type RegionPref } from '@/ipc/types';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';

import { Field, NumberSetting, settingId, useFieldError } from './Field';

const METHODS: readonly MatchMethod[] = ['hash-then-name', 'hash-only', 'name-only'];

export function MatchingSection() {
  const t = useT();
  const m = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings!.matching);
  const update = useSettingsStore((s) => s.update);
  const regionsError = useFieldError('matching.regions');
  const langError = useFieldError('matching.languages');
  const [langs, setLangs] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const label = (r: RegionPref) => (r === 'rom' ? t('fill.regionRom') : t(`regions.${r}`));
  const setRegions = (next: RegionPref[]) => void update({ matching: { regions: next } });
  const move = (i: number, d: -1 | 1) => {
    const next = [...m.regions];
    const [x] = next.splice(i, 1);
    next.splice(i + d, 0, x!);
    setRegions(next);
  };
  const missing = (['rom', ...REGIONS] as RegionPref[]).filter((r) => !m.regions.includes(r));
  const langId = settingId('matching.languages');
  return (
    <div className="settings-group">
      <fieldset className="setting">
        <legend className="setting-label">{t('settings.matching.strategy')}</legend>
        {STRATEGIES.map((s) => (
          <div key={s}>
            <label className="radio-row">
              <input type="radio" name="strategy" checked={m.strategy === s} onChange={() => void update({ matching: { strategy: s } })} />
              {t(`fill.strategy.${s}`)}
            </label>
            <p className="hint">{t(`fill.strategyHint.${s}`)}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      <h2 className="section-title" id="settings-regions">{t('settings.matching.regions')}</h2>
      <ol className="region-order" aria-labelledby="settings-regions">
        {m.regions.map((r, i) => (
          <li key={r}>
            <span>{label(r)}</span>
            <button type="button" className="icon-button" disabled={i === 0} aria-label={t('fill.moveUp', { region: label(r) })} onClick={() => move(i, -1)}>↑</button>
            <button type="button" className="icon-button" disabled={i === m.regions.length - 1} aria-label={t('fill.moveDown', { region: label(r) })} onClick={() => move(i, 1)}>↓</button>
            <button type="button" className="icon-button" aria-label={t('settings.matching.remove', { region: label(r) })} onClick={() => setRegions(m.regions.filter((x) => x !== r))}>×</button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
      {missing.length > 0 && (
        <select name="add-region" className="select" aria-label={t('settings.matching.addRegion')} value=""
          onChange={(e) => e.target.value && setRegions([...m.regions, e.target.value as RegionPref])}>
          <option value="">{t('settings.matching.addRegion')}</option>
          {missing.map((r) => <option key={r} value={r}>{label(r)}</option>)}
        </select>
      )}
      {regionsError && <p role="alert" className="field-error">{regionsError}</p>}
      <Field id={langId} label={t('settings.matching.languages')} hint={t('settings.matching.languagesHint')} error={langError}>
        <input id={langId} name={langId} className="text-input" value={langs ?? m.languages.join(', ')}
          onChange={(e) => setLangs(e.target.value)}
          onBlur={async () => {
            if (langs === null) return;
            const list = langs.split(',').map((x) => x.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean);
            if ((await update({ matching: { languages: list } })).length === 0) setLangs(null);
          }} />
      </Field>
      <NumberSetting path="matching.auto_threshold" label={t('settings.matching.auto')} value={m.auto_threshold} scale={100}
        toPatch={(v) => ({ matching: { auto_threshold: v } })} />
      <NumberSetting path="matching.review_threshold" label={t('settings.matching.review')} value={m.review_threshold} scale={100}
        toPatch={(v) => ({ matching: { review_threshold: v } })} />
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input type="checkbox" name="fuzzy" checked={m.fuzzy} onChange={(e) => void update({ matching: { fuzzy: e.target.checked } })} />
        {t('settings.matching.fuzzy')}
      </label>
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input type="checkbox" name="arcade" checked={m.arcade_translation} onChange={(e) => void update({ matching: { arcade_translation: e.target.checked } })} />
        {t('settings.matching.arcade')}
      </label>
      <Field id={settingId('matching.match_method')} label={t('settings.matching.method')}>
        <select id={settingId('matching.match_method')} name="match-method" className="select" value={m.match_method}
          onChange={(e) => void update({ matching: { match_method: e.target.value as MatchMethod } })}>
          {METHODS.map((x) => <option key={x} value={x}>{t(`settings.matching.methods.${x}`)}</option>)}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <NumberSetting path="matching.max_hash_mb" label={t('settings.matching.maxHash')} value={m.max_hash_mb}
        toPatch={(v) => ({ matching: { max_hash_mb: v } })} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`NetworkSection.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { Modal } from '@/components/Modal';
import { LocaleContext } from '@/i18n/core';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { errorKey, toBackendError, type BackendError } from '@/ipc/errors';
import type { ProxyChoice } from '@/ipc/types';
import { formatSize } from '@/lib/format';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';

import { Field, NumberSetting, settingId, useFieldError } from './Field';

export function NetworkSection() {
  const t = useT();
  const locale = useContext(LocaleContext);
  const n = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings!.network);
  const update = useSettingsStore((s) => s.update);
  const urlError = useFieldError('network.proxy_url');
  const [url, setUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [usage, setUsage] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [freed, setFreed] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<BackendError | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void getBackend().then((b) => b.cacheUsage()).then((u) => alive && setUsage(u), () => undefined);
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);
  async function clear() {
    setConfirm(false);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const b = await getBackend();
      setFreed(await b.clearCache());
      setUsage(await b.cacheUsage());
    } catch (e) {
      setError(toBackendError(e));
    }
  }
  const urlId = settingId('network.proxy_url');
  return (
    <div className="settings-group">
      <NumberSetting path="network.concurrency" label={t('settings.network.concurrency')} value={n.concurrency} toPatch={(v) => ({ network: { concurrency: v } })} />
      <NumberSetting path="network.timeout_secs" label={t('settings.network.timeout')} value={n.timeout_secs} toPatch={(v) => ({ network: { timeout_secs: v } })} />
      <NumberSetting path="network.retries" label={t('settings.network.retries')} value={n.retries} toPatch={(v) => ({ network: { retries: v } })} />
      <fieldset className="setting">
        <legend className="setting-label">{t('settings.network.proxy')}</legend>
        {(['system', 'env', 'manual'] as const satisfies readonly ProxyChoice[]).map((p) => (
          <label key={p} className="radio-row">
            <input type="radio" name="proxy" checked={n.proxy === p} onChange={() => void update({ network: { proxy: p } })} />
            {t(`settings.network.proxies.${p}`)}
          </label>
        ))}
        <p className="hint">{t('settings.network.proxyHint')}</p>
      </fieldset>
      <Field id={urlId} label={t('settings.network.proxyUrl')} error={urlError}>
        <input id={urlId} name={urlId} className="text-input" value={url ?? n.proxy_url} placeholder="http://proxy:3128"
          onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)}
          onBlur={async () => {
            if (url === null) return;
            if ((await update({ network: { proxy_url: url } })).length === 0) setUrl(null);
          }} />
      </Field>
      <NumberSetting path="network.cache_max_mb" label={t('settings.network.cacheMax')} value={n.cache_max_mb} toPatch={(v) => ({ network: { cache_max_mb: v } })} />
      <p className="hint">{usage === null ? '—' : t('settings.network.inUse', { size: formatSize(usage, locale) })}</p>
      <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => setConfirm(true)}>{t('settings.network.clear')}</button>
      {freed !== null && <p role="status" className="hint">{t('settings.network.freed', { size: formatSize(freed, locale) })}</p>}
      {error && <p role="alert" className="error-banner">{t(errorKey(error.code), { detail: error.detail ?? '' })}</p>}
      <NumberSetting path="network.index_refresh_hours" label={t('settings.network.refresh')} value={n.index_refresh_hours} toPatch={(v) => ({ network: { index_refresh_hours: v } })} />
      {confirm && (
        <Modal labelledBy="clear-cache-title" onClose={() => setConfirm(false)}>
          <h2 id="clear-cache-title">{t('settings.network.clearTitle')}</h2>
          <p>{t('settings.network.clearBody')}</p>
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => setConfirm(false)}>{t('settings.network.cancel')}</button>
            <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => void clear()}>{t('settings.network.clearYes')}</button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`SafetySection.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';

import { Field, NumberSetting, settingId, useFieldError } from './Field';

export function SafetySection() {
  const t = useT();
  const s = useSettingsStore((st) => st.settings!.safety);
  const update = useSettingsStore((st) => st.update);
  const dirError = useFieldError('safety.backup_dir');
  const [dir, setDir] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const commitDir = async (value: string) => {
    if ((await update({ safety: { backup_dir: value.trim() || null } })).length === 0) setDir(null);
  };
  const id = settingId('safety.backup_dir');
  return (
    <div className="settings-group">
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input type="checkbox" name="backup" checked disabled aria-describedby="backup-locked" />
        {t('settings.safety.backup')}
      </label>
      <p id="backup-locked" className="hint">{t('settings.safety.backupHint')}</p>
      <Field id={id} label={t('settings.safety.backupDir')} hint={t('settings.safety.backupDirHint')} error={dirError}>
        <input id={id} name={id} className="text-input" value={dir ?? s.backup_dir ?? ''}
          onChange={(e) => setDir(e.target.value)} onBlur={() => dir !== null && void commitDir(dir)} />
      </Field>
      <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={async () => {
        const picked = await (await getBackend()).pickFolder();
        if (picked) await commitDir(picked);
      }}>
        {t('settings.safety.choose')}
      </button>
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input type="checkbox" name="dry-run" checked={s.dry_run_default} onChange={(e) => void update({ safety: { dry_run_default: e.target.checked } })} />
        {t('settings.safety.dryRun')}
      </label>
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input type="checkbox" name="apple-double" checked={s.clean_apple_double} onChange={(e) => void update({ safety: { clean_apple_double: e.target.checked } })} />
        {t('settings.safety.appleDouble')}
      </label>
      <NumberSetting path="safety.undo_depth" label={t('settings.safety.undoDepth')} value={s.undo_depth} toPatch={(v) => ({ safety: { undo_depth: v } })} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`AdvancedSection.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

import { Modal } from '@/components/Modal';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import type { LogLevel } from '@/ipc/types';
import { useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';

import { Field, settingId, useFieldError } from './Field';

export function AdvancedSection() {
  const t = useT();
  const a = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings!.advanced);
  const update = useSettingsStore((s) => s.update);
  const reset = useSettingsStore((s) => s.reset);
  const dirError = useFieldError('advanced.custom_dir');
  const [dir, setDir] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false);
  const id = settingId('advanced.custom_dir');
  return (
    <div className="settings-group">
      <Field id={settingId('advanced.log_level')} label={t('settings.advanced.logLevel')}>
        <select id={settingId('advanced.log_level')} name="log-level" className="select" value={a.log_level}
          onChange={(e) => void update({ advanced: { log_level: e.target.value as LogLevel } })}>
          {(['error', 'warn', 'info', 'debug'] as const).map((l) => <option key={l} value={l}>{t(`settings.advanced.levels.${l}`)}</option>)}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <div className="row-actions">
        <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => void getBackend().then((b) => b.openLogsFolder())}>{t('settings.advanced.openLogs')}</button>
        <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => void getBackend().then((b) => b.openConfigFolder())}>{t('settings.advanced.openConfig')}</button>
      </div>
      <Field id={id} label={t('settings.advanced.customDir')} hint={t('settings.advanced.customDirHint')} error={dirError}>
        <input id={id} name={id} className="text-input" value={dir ?? a.custom_dir ?? ''} onChange={(e) => setDir(e.target.value)}
          onBlur={async () => {
            if (dir === null) return;
            if ((await update({ advanced: { custom_dir: dir.trim() || null } })).length === 0) setDir(null);
          }} />
      </Field>
      <button type="button" className="ghost-button danger" onClick={() => setConfirm(true)}>{t('settings.advanced.reset')}</button>
      {confirm && (
        <Modal labelledBy="reset-title" onClose={() => setConfirm(false)}>
          <h2 id="reset-title">{t('settings.advanced.resetTitle')}</h2>
          <p>{t('settings.advanced.resetBody')}</p>
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => setConfirm(false)}>{t('settings.advanced.cancel')}</button>
            <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => { setConfirm(false); void reset(); }}>{t('settings.advanced.resetYes')}</button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { act, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { Root } from '@/Root';
import { setBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { FIXTURE_NOW } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';
import { createMockBackend, type MockBackend } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import { resetLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';
import { useNavStore } from '@/stores/navStore';
import { resetSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';
import { stubLayout } from '@/test/layout';
import { MemoryStorage } from '@/test/storage';

let backend: MockBackend;
let storage: MemoryStorage;
let restore: () => void;

async function openProfiles() {
  render(<Root />);
  await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Settings' });
  act(() => useNavStore.getState().openSettings('profiles'));
  return screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Layout profiles' });
}

describe('profiles editor', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    storage = new MemoryStorage();
    backend = createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW, storage });
    setBackend(backend);
    resetSettingsStore();
    resetLibraryStore();
    restore = stubLayout(1280, 800);
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    restore();
    setBackend(null);
  });

  it('lists the built-ins read-only, each duplicable', async () => {
    const list = await openProfiles();
    const onion = within(list).getByRole('listitem', { name: 'Onion OS' });
    expect(within(onion).getByText('Built-in (read-only)')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(onion).getByRole('button', { name: 'Duplicate Onion OS' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(onion).queryByRole('button', { name: /Edit|Delete/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('duplicates, previews live, saves and keeps the profile after a reload', async () => {
    const list = await openProfiles();
    await userEvent.click(within(list).getByRole('button', { name: 'Duplicate Onion OS' }));
    const form = screen.getByRole('form', { name: 'Edit layout profile' });
    expect(within(form).getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Profile id' })).toHaveValue('onion-copy');
    expect(await within(form).findByText('Roms/SFC/Imgs/Zelda (USA).png')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const template = within(form).getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Cover path template' });
    await userEvent.clear(template);
    await userEvent.type(template, '{{system_dir}/covers/{{stem}.png');
    expect(await within(form).findByText('Roms/SFC/covers/Zelda (USA).png')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const sub = within(form).getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Example subfolder' });
    await userEvent.type(sub, 'Hacks');
    expect(await within(form).findByText('Roms/SFC/covers/Zelda (USA).png')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(within(form).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save profile' }));
    const after = await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Layout profiles' });
    expect(within(after).getByRole('listitem', { name: 'Onion OS (copy)' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const again = await createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW, storage }).listProfiles();
    expect(again.find((p) => p.profile.id === 'onion-copy')?.profile.slots.cover?.template).toBe('{system_dir}/covers/{stem}.png');
  });

  it('shows errors for a template that leaves the library and does not save it', async () => {
    const list = await openProfiles();
    await userEvent.click(within(list).getByRole('button', { name: 'New profile' }));
    const form = screen.getByRole('form', { name: 'Edit layout profile' });
    const template = within(form).getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Cover path template' });
    await userEvent.clear(template);
    await userEvent.type(template, '{{root}/../x/{{stem}.png');
    expect(await within(form).findByText('This value is not valid.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(form).queryByText(/x\/Zelda/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(within(form).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save profile' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('form', { name: 'Edit layout profile' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect((await backend.listProfiles()).some((p) => p.profile.id === 'my-layout')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('edits and deletes a custom profile after a confirmation', async () => {
    const list = await openProfiles();
    await userEvent.click(within(list).getByRole('button', { name: 'New profile' }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save profile' }));
    let item = within(await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Layout profiles' })).getByRole('listitem', { name: 'My layout' });
    await userEvent.click(within(item).getByRole('button', { name: 'Edit My layout' }));
    const name = screen.getByRole('textbox', { name: 'Name' });
    await userEvent.clear(name);
    await userEvent.type(name, 'My SD');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save profile' }));
    item = within(await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Layout profiles' })).getByRole('listitem', { name: 'My SD' });
    await userEvent.click(within(item).getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete My SD' }));
    await userEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole('dialog')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete profile' }));
    expect(within(await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Layout profiles' })).queryByRole('listitem', { name: 'My SD' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders hostile profile names as text', async () => {
    await backend.saveProfile(
      { id: 'evil', name: '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>', roms_root: 'ROMS', art_dirs: [], gamelist: false, slots: { cover: { template: '{system_dir}/{stem}.png', format: 'png', max_width: null, max_height: null } } },
      null,
    );
    await openProfiles();
    expect(screen.getByText('<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(document.querySelectorAll('img[src="x"], [onerror]').length).toBe(0);
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the editor**

`src/components/settings/ProfileEditor.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { MEDIA, type FieldError, type Media, type PreviewExample, type ProfileFile, type ProfileInfo, type SlotFile } from '@/ipc/types';

import { fieldErrorText } from './Field';

export const BLANK_PROFILE: ProfileFile = {
  id: 'my-layout',
  name: 'My layout',
  roms_root: 'ROMS',
  art_dirs: ['Imgs'],
  gamelist: false,
  slots: { cover: { template: '{system_dir}/{rel_dir}/Imgs/{stem}.png', format: 'png', max_width: null, max_height: null } },
};

const EMPTY_SLOT: SlotFile = { template: '{system_dir}/{rel_dir}/media/{stem}.png', format: 'png', max_width: null, max_height: null };

export function ProfileEditor({ initial, replaces, onSaved, onCancel }: {
  initial: ProfileFile;
  replaces: string | null;
  onSaved(list: ProfileInfo[]): void;
  onCancel(): void;
}) {
  const t = useT();
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ProfileFile>(initial);
  const [example, setExample] = useState<PreviewExample>({ system_folder: 'SFC', rel_dir: '', file_name: 'Zelda (USA).sfc' });
  const [paths, setPaths] = useState<Partial<Record<Media, string>>>({});
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FieldError[]>([]);
  const [saveErrors, setSaveErrors] = useState<FieldError[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      void getBackend()
        .then((b) => b.previewProfile(draft, example))
        .then((p) => {
          if (!alive) return;
          setPaths(p.paths);
          setErrors(p.errors);
        }, () => undefined);
    }, 150);
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [draft, example]);

  const all = [...saveErrors.filter((e) => !errors.some((x) => x.field === e.field)), ...errors];
  const errorFor = (field: string) => {
    const e = all.find((x) => x.field === field);
    return e ? fieldErrorText(t, e) : null;
  };
  const set = (patch: Partial<ProfileFile>) => {
    setSaveErrors([]);
    setDraft((d) => ({ ...d, ...patch }));
  };
  const setSlot = (m: Media, slot: SlotFile | null) =>
    setDraft((d) => {
      const slots = { ...d.slots };
      if (slot) slots[m] = slot;
      else delete slots[m];
      return { ...d, slots };
    });

  async function save() {
    const b = await getBackend();
    const out = await b.saveProfile(draft, replaces);
    if (out.errors.length) setSaveErrors(out.errors);
    else onSaved(out.profiles);
  }

  const text = (id: string, label: string, value: string, onChange: (v: string) => void, field: string) => {
    const error = errorFor(field);
    return (
      <div className="setting">
        <label htmlFor={id} className="setting-label">{label}</label>
        <input id={id} name={id} className="text-input" value={value} aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} />
        {error && <p role="alert" className="field-error">{error}</p>}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <form aria-label={t('profiles.editTitle')} className="profile-editor" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); void save(); }}>
      {text('profile-id', t('profiles.id'), draft.id, (v) => set({ id: v }), 'id')}
      {text('profile-name', t('profiles.name'), draft.name, (v) => set({ name: v }), 'name')}
      {text('profile-roms-root', t('profiles.romsRoot'), draft.roms_root, (v) => set({ roms_root: v }), 'roms_root')}
      {text('profile-art-dirs', t('profiles.artDirs'), draft.art_dirs.join(', '),
        (v) => set({ art_dirs: v.split(',').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean) }), 'art_dirs')}
      <label className="checkbox-row">
        <input type="checkbox" name="profile-gamelist" checked={draft.gamelist} onChange={(e) => set({ gamelist: e.target.checked })} />
        {t('profiles.gamelist')}
      </label>
      {errorFor('slots') && <p role="alert" className="field-error">{errorFor('slots')}</p>}
      {MEDIA.map((m) => {
        const slot = draft.slots[m];
        const media = t(`art.media.${m}`);
        return (
          <fieldset key={m} className="setting">
            <legend className="setting-label">
              <label className="checkbox-row">
                <input type="checkbox" name={`slot-${m}`} checked={Boolean(slot)} onChange={(e) => setSlot(m, e.target.checked ? EMPTY_SLOT : null)} />
                {t('profiles.slot', { media })}
              </label>
            </legend>
            {slot && (
              <>
                {text(`slot-${m}-template`, t('profiles.template', { media }), slot.template, (v) => setSlot(m, { ...slot, template: v }), `slots.${m}.template`)}
                <label className="field-label">
                  {t('profiles.format')}{' '}
                  <select name={`slot-${m}-format`} className="select" value={slot.format}
                    onChange={(e) => setSlot(m, { ...slot, format: e.target.value as 'png' | 'jpg' })}>
                    <option value="png">PNG</option>
                    <option value="jpg">JPEG</option>
                  </select>
                </label>
                {(['max_width', 'max_height'] as const).map((f) =>
                  text(`slot-${m}-${f}`, t(`profiles.${f}`, { media }), slot[f] === null ? '' : String(slot[f]),
                    (v) => setSlot(m, { ...slot, [f]: v.trim() === '' ? null : Number(v) }), `slots.${m}.${f}`),
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </fieldset>
        );
      })}
      <h3 className="section-title">{t('profiles.preview')}</h3>
      <div className="preview-inputs">
        {text('example-folder', t('profiles.exampleFolder'), example.system_folder, (v) => setExample((x) => ({ ...x, system_folder: v })), 'example.system_folder')}
        {text('example-sub', t('profiles.exampleSub'), example.rel_dir, (v) => setExample((x) => ({ ...x, rel_dir: v })), 'example.rel_dir')}
        {text('example-file', t('profiles.exampleFile'), example.file_name, (v) => setExample((x) => ({ ...x, file_name: v })), 'example.file_name')}
      </div>
      <dl className="preview-paths" aria-live="polite">
        {MEDIA.filter((m) => paths[m]).map((m) => (
          <div key={m}>
            <dt>{t(`art.media.${m}`)}</dt>
            <dd><code>{paths[m]}</code></dd>
          </div>
        ))}
      </dl>
      <div className="modal-actions">
        <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={onCancel}>{t('profiles.cancel')}</button>
        <button type="submit" className="primary-button">{t('profiles.save')}</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`src/components/settings/ProfilesSection.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import { Modal } from '@/components/Modal';
import { useT } from '@/i18n/useT';
import { getBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { errorKey, toBackendError, type BackendError } from '@/ipc/errors';
import type { ProfileFile, ProfileInfo } from '@/ipc/types';
import { profileFromBuiltin } from '@/lib/profileTemplate';

import { BLANK_PROFILE, ProfileEditor } from './ProfileEditor';

export function ProfilesSection() {
  const t = useT();
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<ProfileInfo[]>([]);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<{ initial: ProfileFile; replaces: string | null } | null>(null);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState<ProfileInfo | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<BackendError | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    void getBackend().then((b) => b.listProfiles()).then((p) => alive && setProfiles(p), (e) => alive && setError(toBackendError(e)));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);

  async function remove(p: ProfileInfo) {
    setDeleting(null);
    try {
      setProfiles(await (await getBackend()).deleteProfile(p.profile.id));
    } catch (e) {
      setError(toBackendError(e));
    }
  }

  if (editing) {
    return (
      <ProfileEditor
        initial={editing.initial}
        replaces={editing.replaces}
        onSaved={(list) => {
          setProfiles(list);
          setEditing(null);
        }}
        onCancel={() => setEditing(null)}
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="settings-group">
      <p className="hint">{t('profiles.hint')}</p>
      <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => setEditing({ initial: BLANK_PROFILE, replaces: null })}>
        {t('profiles.new')}
      </button>
      <ul aria-label={t('profiles.list')} className="profile-list">
        {profiles.map((p) => (
          <li key={p.profile.id} aria-label={p.profile.name}>
            <span className="truncate" title={p.profile.id}>{p.profile.name}</span>
            {p.builtin ? (
              <>
                <span className="hint">{t('profiles.builtin')}</span>
                <button type="button" className="ghost-button" aria-label={t('profiles.duplicate', { name: p.profile.name })}
                  onClick={() => setEditing({ initial: profileFromBuiltin(p, `${p.profile.id}-copy`), replaces: null })}>
                  {t('profiles.duplicateShort')}
                </button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <button type="button" className="ghost-button" aria-label={t('profiles.edit', { name: p.profile.name })}
                  onClick={() => setEditing({ initial: p.profile, replaces: p.profile.id })}>
                  {t('profiles.editShort')}
                </button>
                <button type="button" className="ghost-button" aria-label={t('profiles.delete', { name: p.profile.name })}
                  onClick={() => setDeleting(p)}>
                  {t('profiles.deleteShort')}
                </button>
              </>
            )}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="error-banner">{t(errorKey(error.code), { detail: error.detail ?? '' })}</p>}
      {deleting && (
        <Modal labelledBy="delete-profile-title" onClose={() => setDeleting(null)}>
          <h2 id="delete-profile-title">{t('profiles.deleteTitle', { name: deleting.profile.name })}</h2>
          <p>{t('profiles.deleteBody')}</p>
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => setDeleting(null)}>{t('profiles.cancel')}</button>
            <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => void remove(deleting)}>{t('profiles.deleteYes')}</button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

> `Modal` (M4) takes `labelledBy` + `onClose` and renders `role="dialog"`. `profileFromBuiltin` names the copy "<name> (copy)": the test expects "Onion OS (copy)".

import { act, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import App from '@/App';
import { I18nProvider } from '@/i18n/I18nProvider';
import { setBackend } from '@/ipc/backend';
import { FIXTURE_NOW } from '@/ipc/mock/fixture';
import { createMockBackend, MOCK_LIBRARIES } from '@/ipc/mock/mockBackend';
import type { EntryView } from '@/ipc/types';
import { useJobStore } from '@/stores/jobStore';
import { resetLibraryStore, useLibraryStore } from '@/stores/libraryStore';
import { useSelectionStore } from '@/stores/selectionStore';
import { resetSettingsStore, useSettingsStore } from '@/stores/settingsStore';
import { useViewStore } from '@/stores/viewStore';
import { stubLayout } from '@/test/layout';
import { MemoryStorage } from '@/test/storage';

async function setup() {
  setBackend(createMockBackend({ delayMs: 0, now: FIXTURE_NOW, storage: new MemoryStorage() }));
  resetLibraryStore();
  resetSettingsStore();
  useJobStore.getState().reset();
  await useSettingsStore.getState().load();
  await useLibraryStore.getState().open(MOCK_LIBRARIES[1].path);
  render(<I18nProvider locale="en"><App /></I18nProvider>);
  return useLibraryStore.getState().view!;
}

function show(entry: EntryView) {
  act(() => {
    useSelectionStore.getState().select(entry.id);
    useViewStore.getState().openDrawer();
  });
  return screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: entry.name });
}

describe('drawer search by title', () => {
  let restore: () => void;
  beforeEach(() => {
    restore = stubLayout(1280, 800);
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    restore();
    setBackend(null);
  });

  it('searches a typed title, writes the pick and shows the manual match', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    const entry = view.entries.find((e) => e.art.cover === 'missing')!;
    const drawer = show(entry);
    await userEvent.click(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Search…' }));
    const box = within(drawer).getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Title to search' });
    await userEvent.clear(box);
    await userEvent.type(box, 'Golden Sun{enter}');
    const group = await within(drawer).findByRole('group', { name: 'Search results' });
    await userEvent.click(within(group).getByRole('button', { name: /Golden Sun \(Europe\)/ }));
    expect(await within(drawer).findByText('Art updated. This ROM now matches “Golden Sun (Europe)”.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(useLibraryStore.getState().view!.entries[entry.id]!.art.cover).toBe('present');
    expect(await within(drawer).findByText('Chosen by you')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await userEvent.click(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Forget manual match' }));
    expect(within(drawer).queryByText('Chosen by you')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('asks before replacing existing art', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    const entry = view.entries.find((e) => e.art.cover === 'present')!;
    const drawer = show(entry);
    await userEvent.click(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Search…' }));
    await userEvent.type(within(drawer).getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Title to search' }), '{selectall}Zelda{enter}');
    const group = await within(drawer).findByRole('group', { name: 'Search results' });
    await userEvent.click(within(group).getAllByRole('button')[0]!);

    await userEvent.click(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Use this image' }));
    expect(await within(drawer).findByText(/Art updated/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('search renders hostile names as text and refuses an empty title', async () => {
    const view = await setup();
    const entry = view.entries[0]!;
    const drawer = show(entry);
    await userEvent.click(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Search…' }));
    const box = within(drawer).getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Title to search' });
    await userEvent.clear(box);
    expect(within(drawer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Search' })).toBeDisabled();
    await userEvent.type(box, '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>{enter}');
    await within(drawer).findByRole('group', { name: 'Search results' });
    expect(within(drawer).getAllByText(/<img src=x onerror=alert\(1\)> \(USA\)/).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(document.querySelectorAll('img[src="x"], [onerror]').length).toBe(0);
  });
});

Append to `src/lib/fill.test.ts`:

```ts
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, cloneSettings } from '@/lib/settingsRules';
import { shouldAutoRun, wizardDefaults } from './fill';

describe('wizard defaults from settings', () => {
  it('uses the art and matching settings, filtered by the layout', () => {
    const s = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    s.art.media = ['cover', 'title'];
    s.matching.strategy = 'priority';
    s.matching.regions = ['japan', 'rom'];
    expect(wizardDefaults(s, ['cover'])).toEqual({ media: ['cover'], strategy: 'priority', regions: ['japan', 'rom'], overwrite: 'never' });
    s.art.overwrite = 'ask';
    expect(wizardDefaults(s, ['cover']).overwrite).toBeNull();
    s.art.overwrite = 'backup';
    expect(wizardDefaults(s, ['cover']).overwrite).toBe('backup');
    s.art.media = ['title'];
    expect(wizardDefaults(s, ['cover']).media).toEqual(['cover'], 'falls back to the first media the layout stores');
    expect(wizardDefaults(null, ['cover', 'screenshot']).media).toEqual(['cover']);
  });

  it('runs by itself only with dry-run-first off and nothing to review', () => {
    const s = cloneSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    const job = { state: 'planned', plan: { totals: { auto: 3, review: 0, none: 1 } } } as never;
    expect(shouldAutoRun(job, s, false)).toBe(false);
    s.safety.dry_run_default = false;
    expect(shouldAutoRun(job, s, false)).toBe(true);
    expect(shouldAutoRun(job, s, true)).toBe(false);
    const review = { state: 'planned', plan: { totals: { auto: 3, review: 2, none: 0 } } } as never;
    expect(shouldAutoRun(review, s, false)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`src/hooks/useImageUrl.ts`:

```ts
export type ImageSource =
  | { kind: 'job'; job: number; index: number; candidate: number }
  | { kind: 'entry' | 'search'; generation: number; entryId: number; media: Media; candidate: number };

export function sourceKey(s: ImageSource): string {
  return s.kind === 'job'
    ? `job:${s.job}:${s.index}:${s.candidate}`
    : `${s.kind}:${s.generation}:${s.entryId}:${s.media}:${s.candidate}`;
}
// in the loader:
        const img =
          source.kind === 'job'
            ? await b.candidateArt(source.job, source.index, source.candidate)
            : source.kind === 'entry'
              ? await b.entryCandidateArt(source.generation, source.entryId, source.media, source.candidate)
              : await b.entrySearchArt(source.generation, source.entryId, source.media, source.candidate);
```

`DetailDrawer.tsx` — in `DrawerActions` add:

```tsx
  const overwrite = useSettingsStore((s) => s.settings?.art.overwrite ?? 'never');
  const identity = useIdentityStore((s) => s.byKey[identityKey(view.generation, entry.id)]);
  const [searching, setSearching] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [results, setResults] = useState<CandidateView[] | null>(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<{ kind: 'entry' | 'search'; candidate: CandidateView } | null>(null);
  const [matched, setMatched] = useState<string | null>(null);

  function openSearch() {
    setSearching(true);
    setResults(null);
    setPending(null);
    const title = identity?.status === 'done' ? identity.identity.title : entry.name.replace(/\s*[([][^)\]]*[)\]]/g, '').trim();
    setQuery(title);
  }

  async function runSearch() {
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);
    setResults(null);
    try {
      const b = await getBackend();
      setResults(await b.searchEntryArt(view.generation, entry.id, media, query));
    } catch (e) {
      setError(toBackendError(e));
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  /** D25: replacing an existing image is confirmed unless the setting says "replace". */
  function pick(kind: 'entry' | 'search', c: CandidateView) {
    if (entry.art[media] === 'present' && overwrite !== 'backup') setPending({ kind, candidate: c });
    else void write(kind, c);
  }

  async function write(kind: 'entry' | 'search', c: CandidateView) {
    setPending(null);
    const ok = await attempt((b) =>
      kind === 'entry'
        ? b.setEntryArt(view.generation, entry.id, media, c.index)
        : b.setEntryArtFromSearch(view.generation, entry.id, media, c.index),
    );
    if (!ok) return;
    if (kind === 'entry') setCandidates(null);
    else {
      setResults(null);
      setSearching(false);
      setMatched(c.name);
      void useIdentityStore.getState().identify(view.generation, entry.id);
    }
  }
```

The "Change…" candidate buttons call `pick('entry', c)` instead of `choose(c.index)`. The "Search…" button is enabled (`disabled={busy || loading}`, `onClick={openSearch}`), the `drawer-search-later` hint is removed. The search block:

```tsx
      {searching && (
        <form className="drawer-search" role="search" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); void runSearch(); }}>
          <label className="field-label">
            {t('drawer.searchLabel')}{' '}
            <input type="search" name="drawer-search" className="text-input" value={query} maxLength={120}
              onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)} />
          </label>
          <button type="submit" className="ghost-button" disabled={!query.trim() || loading}>{t('drawer.searchGo')}</button>
          <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => setSearching(false)}>{t('drawer.removeNo')}</button>
        </form>
      )}
      {results &&
        (results.length === 0 ? (
          <p>{t('drawer.searchNone')}</p>
        ) : (
          <div role="group" aria-label={t('drawer.searchResults')} className="candidate-grid drawer-candidates">
            {results.map((c) => (
              <button key={c.index} type="button" className="candidate" onClick={() => pick('search', c)}>
                <CandidateImage source={{ kind: 'search', generation: view.generation, entryId: entry.id, media, candidate: c.index }} label={c.name} />
                <span className="truncate" title={c.name}>{c.name}</span>
                <span className="hint">{t('review.meta', { key: c.index + 1, source: c.source, regions: c.regions.map((r) => t(`regions.${r}`)).join(', ') || '—', score: Math.round(c.confidence * 100) })}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))}
      {pending && (
        <div className="undo-confirm">
          <p>{t('drawer.replaceConfirm', { name: pending.candidate.name })}</p>
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <button type="button" className="ghost-button" onClick={() => setPending(null)}>{t('drawer.removeNo')}</button>
            <button type="button" className="primary-button" onClick={() => void write(pending.kind, pending.candidate)}>{t('drawer.replaceYes')}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {updated && <p role="status" className="hint">{matched ? t('drawer.searchUpdated', { name: matched }) : t('drawer.updated')}</p>}

- [ ] **Step 5: Wizard defaults, "ask", dry-run default and the gamelist note**

`src/lib/fill.ts`:

```ts
export function wizardDefaults(settings: Settings | null, profileMedia: readonly Media[]) {
  const wanted = settings?.art.media.filter((m) => profileMedia.includes(m)) ?? [];
  return {
    media: wanted.length ? wanted : profileMedia.slice(0, 1),
    strategy: settings?.matching.strategy ?? 'rom-region',
    regions: settings?.matching.regions ?? (['rom', 'world', 'usa', 'europe', 'japan'] as RegionPref[]),
    overwrite: (settings?.art.overwrite === 'ask' ? null : (settings?.art.overwrite ?? 'never')) as OverwritePolicy | null,
  };
}

export function shouldAutoRun(job: JobView | null, settings: Settings | null, alreadyRan: boolean): boolean {
  return Boolean(
    job && job.state === 'planned' && !alreadyRan && settings && !settings.safety.dry_run_default &&
      job.plan && job.plan.totals.review === 0 && job.plan.totals.auto > 0,
  );
}
```

`FillWizard.tsx`: the initial `useState`s come from `wizardDefaults(useSettingsStore.getState().settings, view.profile.media)`; `overwrite` is `OverwritePolicy | null` and the overwrite step's "Preview (dry run)" button is disabled while `null` (the request sends the chosen value); an effect runs `runFill(job.id)` once when `shouldAutoRun(job, settings, ranRef.current)`. Replace `{view.profile.id === 'batocera' && <p className="hint">{t('fill.batocera')}</p>}` with `{view.profile.gamelist && <p className="hint">{t('fill.gamelist')}</p>}`.

- [ ] **Step 1: The real settings test (ignored)**

`src-tauri/tests/real_m5.rs`:

```rust
//! M5 exit check through the app's real core path on a COPY (never the
//! source, never /Volumes): an ignored folder and a max-size setting
//! change what a dry run and a fill do; Undo restores the copy. Run by
//! hand with the real art servers:
//!
//! ```text
//! COVERLY_M5_COPY=$SC/m5-copy COVERLY_M5_WORK=$SC/m5-work \
//!   cargo test -p coverly-roms --release --test real_m5 -- --ignored --nocapture
//! ```

mod common;

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Instant;

use common::fill::{wait_for, Recorder};
use coverly_core::image::probe_dimensions;
use coverly_core::matching::StrategyKind;
use coverly_core::profiles::Media;
use coverly_core::systems::SystemDb;
use coverly_core::write::{sha1_hex, OverwritePolicy};
use coverly_roms_lib::jobs::view::{FillRequest, JobState};
use coverly_roms_lib::jobs::FillConfig;
use coverly_roms_lib::state::AppState;
use serde_json::json;

fn tree(root: &Path) -> BTreeMap<PathBuf, String> {
    fn walk(root: &Path, dir: &Path, out: &mut BTreeMap<PathBuf, String>) {
        for e in fs::read_dir(dir).unwrap().flatten() {
            let p = e.path();
            if p.is_dir() {
                walk(root, &p, out);
            } else {
                out.insert(p.strip_prefix(root).unwrap().to_path_buf(), sha1_hex(&fs::read(&p).unwrap()));
            }
        }
    }
    let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
    walk(root, root, &mut out);
    out
}

#[test]
#[ignore = "real network and a copy of real ROMs: run by hand (M5 Task 16)"]
fn settings_change_a_real_dry_run_and_fill_on_a_copy() {
    let copy = PathBuf::from(std::env::var("COVERLY_M5_COPY").expect("COVERLY_M5_COPY"));
    let work = PathBuf::from(std::env::var("COVERLY_M5_WORK").expect("COVERLY_M5_WORK"));
    let home = PathBuf::from(std::env::var("HOME").unwrap_or_default());
    for forbidden in [PathBuf::from("/Volumes"), home.join("Documents/ROMS")] {
        assert!(!copy.starts_with(&forbidden) && !work.starts_with(&forbidden), "refusing {}", forbidden.display());
    }
    let before = tree(&copy);
    let st = Arc::new(AppState::new(work.join("config"), FillConfig::new(work.join("cache"), work.join("data"))));
    let v0 = st.open(&copy, None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {}).unwrap();
    let junk = v0.entries.iter().filter(|e| e.rel_path.contains("/Junk/")).count();
    println!("default settings: {} ROMs ({junk} under Junk/)", v0.entries.len());
    assert!(junk > 0, "prepare the copy with a Junk/ subfolder holding a few ROMs");

    let out = st.update_settings(&json!({
        "library": {"ignored_dirs": ["bios", "saves", "states", "Shortcuts", "Junk"]},
        "art": {"max_size": {"anbernic-stock": {"width": 120, "height": 160}}, "media": ["cover"]}
    })).unwrap();
    assert!(out.errors.is_empty() && out.reopen);
    let v1 = st.open(&copy, None, &SystemDb::builtin(), &mut |_| {}).unwrap();
    println!("ignored Junk/: {} ROMs", v1.entries.len());
    assert_eq!(v1.entries.len(), v0.entries.len() - junk);

    let rec = Arc::new(Recorder::default());
    let request = FillRequest {
        entry_ids: None,
        media: vec![Media::Cover],
        strategy: StrategyKind::RomRegion,
        regions: Vec::new(),
        overwrite: OverwritePolicy::Never,
    };
    let t0 = Instant::now();
    let job = st.start_fill(v1.generation, &request, rec.clone()).unwrap();
    let planned = wait_for(&rec, JobState::Planned);
    let totals = planned.plan.as_ref().unwrap().totals;
    println!("dry run {:?}: auto {} review {} none {}", t0.elapsed(), totals.auto, totals.review, totals.none);
    st.jobs().run(st.fill_config(), job.id, rec.clone()).unwrap();
    let done = wait_for(&rec, JobState::Done);
    println!("filled {:?}", done.result);
    let mut written = 0;
    for (path, _) in tree(&copy).iter().filter(|(p, _)| !before.contains_key(*p)) {
        let (w, h) = probe_dimensions(&fs::read(copy.join(path)).unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert!(w <= 120 && h <= 160, "{} is {w}x{h}", path.display());
        written += 1;
    }
    println!("{written} new files, all within 120x160");
    assert!(written > 0);
    st.undo_last_job(v1.generation).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(tree(&copy), before, "undo restores the copy exactly");
}
```

> `wait_for(&Recorder, JobState)` and `jobs().run(cfg, job, events)` are the existing helpers of `src-tauri/tests/common/fill.rs` and `JobManager` (as in `real_fill.rs`); if `wait_for` times out too early for real-network dry runs, give it a longer limit (as `real_fill.rs` does with its own `wait`). The request passes `regions: Vec::new()` so the settings' order is used.
